Joseph ben Abraham Gikatilla. <term>Gates of Justice</term> <note>original Hebrew: *Sha’are Tzedek*</note> (in Hebrew). (A <term>Kabbalistic</term> <note>relating to Kabbalah, the ancient Jewish tradition of mystical interpretation of the Bible</note> work on the ten <term>Sefiroth</term> <note>the ten attributes or emanations in Kabbalah through which the Infinite reveals itself</note> and the names of God). Riva di Trento, Jacob Marcaria, 1561. *Three large ornamental woodcut Hebrew characters on the title page. The first word of the actual text is surrounded by a fine, large woodcut border of foliage (105 x 80 mm). Printed in double columns.* 52 leaves. <term>Quarto</term> <note>a book size where each sheet is folded twice to create four leaves</note>. 18th-century half-calf leather binding (slightly rubbed, corners worn).

*Steinschneider CB 5923.17. Fürst I, 174 (listed under Chiquitilla). Zedner 330. Roest 564. Amram, Makers of Hebrew Books in Italy 305.* <note>These are standard bibliographical references used by scholars to identify rare books.</note> Rare first edition of this fundamental introduction to the philosophy of the <term>Kabbalah</term> by Joseph ben Abraham Gikatilla <note>also spelled Gitalilia or Giquatilla in various sources</note>, 1248–1305, a pupil of Abraham Abulafia and Moses de Leon during the years when the <term>Zohar</term> <note>the foundational work of Jewish mysticism</note> originated, with which he was undoubtedly familiar. He was a leading Spanish Kabbalist at the end of the 13th century, who wrote several influential works on the symbolism of the Sefiroth. The present work was printed by the famous physician Jacob Marcaria, who established a notable Hebrew press in Riva di Trento in 1558.

> "In an active period of five years this press produced upwards of 30 books of value and importance." (Amram).

On the <term>verso</term> <note>the back side of a leaf</note> of the title page is a short preface by the learned printer. Not in the <term>STC</term> <note>Short Title Catalogue</note> of Italian books. — Old handwritten notes on the title page; slightly waterstained.

<summary>This bibliographic record describes a rare 1561 first edition of Joseph Gikatilla’s "Sha’are Tzedek," a foundational Kabbalistic text on the ten Sefiroth. The book was printed in Riva di Trento by the physician-printer Jacob Marcaria.</summary>

Donash ben Abraham the Pious <note>original Hebrew: *ha-Chasid*</note>: <term>Book of Memory</term> <note>original Hebrew: *Sefer ha-Zikkaron*</note> (in Hebrew).

A homiletic <note>intended for preaching or as a sermon</note> work on the <term>Sefiroth</term> <note>the ten divine attributes or emanations in Kabbalistic mysticism</note> and the names of God.

Printed in Riva di Trento, 1561. First three leaves (original) without Hebrew characters. First word of the text is surrounded by a woodcut ornament. Woodcut border on the title page. 32 pages (small <term>quarto</term> <note>a book size where each sheet is folded twice to create four leaves</note>). Signatures: [A]-D4. Each page has a half-calf leather binding (slightly rubbed, corners worn).

*Steinschneider CB 5032,17. Zedner p. 194 (listed under Chayat). Roest p. 306. First edition of this important and rare work.*

(Chayat) <note>Judah ben Jacob Hayyat, a famous Kabbalist</note>, 1450 — circa 1508. Pupil of Abraham Zacuto and then of Isaac Aboab the younger. He was born in Spain and <unclear>left</unclear> in 1492 <note>the year of the Alhambra Decree and the expulsion of Jews from Spain</note> to go to Mantua. He was a leading Spanish <term>Kabbalist</term> <note>a follower of the Jewish mystical tradition known as Kabbalah</note> at the end of the 15th century who wrote several influential <unclear>treatises</unclear> on the symbolism of the Sefiroth.

The present work <unclear>was printed</unclear> by the famous printer Jacob ben Naphtali the Priest <note>original Hebrew: *ha-Kohen*</note>, who began his career as a printer in Riva di Trento in 1558. His activity began in Riva, and this press produced upwards of 30 books in Hebrew and <unclear>some</unclear> in English <note>original: "Latin"</note>; on the back of the leaf <note>original: "verso"</note> of the title is a short preface by the learned printer. Not in the British Museum or the Bodleian Library. Italian paper. Some notes on titles slightly water-stained.

<meta>This page continues the bibliographic description of a rare 16th-century edition of Joseph Gikatilla's "Gates of Justice," expanding on the author’s historical importance and the specific history of the printing press in Riva di Trento.</meta>

<insert>
Joseph ben Abraham Gikatilla: *The Book of the Gates of Justice* <note>original: "Sepher Sche-are Zedek"</note> (in Hebrew).
A <term>Kabbalistic</term> <note>relating to Kabbalah, the ancient Jewish tradition of mystical interpretation of the Bible</note> work on the ten <term>Sefiroth</term> <note>the ten attributes or emanations in Kabbalah through which the Infinite reveals itself</note> and the names of God.
The author lived in Castile <note>original: "Castillia"</note>, Spain, and died about 1305. The first three lines are printed in large Hebrew type, and the title is taken from the first word of the main text.
First edition, and the first book printed by Israel ben Cornelio Adelkind in Riva di Trento, 1561.
36 unnumbered leaves. Small <term>quarto</term> <note>a book size where each sheet is folded twice to create four leaves</note>. 16th century.
(Binding slightly rubbed, corners worn).

*Steinschneider, Catalogus Librorum Hebraeorum* <note>original: "CB"</note> 2023, 11. First edition, I. 147 (under Gikatilla).
*Zedner* 230; *Roest* 394. *Amram, Makers of Hebrew Books in Italy* <note>original: "Amram Hakko-en of Hebrew books in Riva"</note> 205. Rare first edition of this fundamental and important Kabbalistic work.

Gikatilla, Joseph ben Abraham (1248–c. 1305), was a pupil of Abraham Abulafia and a teacher of Moses de Leon, the man credited with the origin of the <term>Zohar</term> <note>the foundational work of Jewish mysticism</note>, a text with which Gikatilla was deeply familiar. He was a leading Spanish <term>Kabbalist</term> at the end of the 13th century, who wrote several influential treatises on the symbolism of the <term>Sefiroth</term>. The present work is highly esteemed by the famous physician Jacob <unclear>Emden</unclear> <note>a renowned 18th-century rabbi and scholar</note> and other Kabbalists.
The printing press was established in Riva di Trento in 1558; its activity was <unclear>limited</unclear> to only five years, during which it produced upwards of 30 books under the direction of Jacob Marcaria (as noted by the historian Amram). This is mentioned on the <term>verso</term> <note>the back of a page</note> at the end of the short preface by the learned printer. This edition is not in the British Museum Catalogue of Italian books. — Old handwritten notes on the title page; slightly waterstained.
</insert>

<summary>This page provides a detailed bibliographic record of a 1561 first edition of Joseph Gikatilla's "Sha’are Tzedek," highlighting its rarity and its author's connection to the origins of the Zohar.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the introductory address to the reader, establishing the necessity of proper guidance before entering the study of the Ten Sephirot. It transitions from a personal address to the cosmological origins of Israel versus the seventy nations.</meta>

א ב ג ד ה ו ז ח

# You Asked

From me, my soul’s friend <note>A term of endearment for a close student or colleague</note>, to illuminate like one of the inner attainments—to know which path the light of the Lord <note>original: "יי", a common abbreviation for the four-letter Name of God</note> follows regarding the matter of the <term>Ten Sephirot</term> <note>The ten creative attributes or emanations through which the Divine interacts with the universe</note>. And behold, I have looked upon you to fulfill your requests, and may God find you favorable. 

The beginning of the path through which a person enters into the unity of the Creator, blessed be He, is the knowledge of the Gate. For do you not know that if a person wishes to see the face of the King and labors for many days, but after the labor he does not merit to know the location of the Gate, what benefit is there in all the labor he labored and all the path he walked, when he does not know the Gate through which to enter to see the face of the King? 

And in this way, most people burden themselves to enter the <term>Pardes</term> <note>Literally "Orchard," a term referring to the deepest mystical study of the Torah</note>, but they do not know and do not understand; they walk in darkness. There is no profit in their toil. Therefore, I have seen fit to illuminate a path before you in the way you should go and the gate by which you should enter. Then you will be desired and accepted before the Lord <note>original: "יי"</note> and the Hand of the Blessed One, and you will be among those who merit to behold the beauty of the Lord <note>original: "ידוד", the Tetragrammaton</note> and to visit His temple. 

->This is the gate of the Lord; the righteous shall enter through it.<- 

Know that the Lord, blessed be He, chose Israel when He divided all the languages in the <term>Generation of the Division</term> <note>Refers to the biblical story of the Tower of Babel</note>, each to his own language, according to their families and their nations. He gave to every single nation a Prince <note>A guardian angel or celestial power</note> and a ruler over them, and He also gave to every nation a portion of the land. In this way, the nations and the lands were distinguished, each to a known Prince. 

And concerning this, it is said: "From these the maritime nations spread out into their territories by their clans within their nations, each with its own language." <note>Genesis 10:5</note> This is the secret of the matter of the Division: all seventy nations remained delivered into the hands of the seventy Princes above, and those seventy Princes surround...

<summary>The author explains that mystical study requires knowing the correct "Gate" or methodology to avoid wasted effort, then begins a cosmic history explaining how God divided the seventy nations under celestial Princes while choosing Israel.</summary>

# And You, the Reader

It shall be a merit <note>The Hebrew word "Tzedakah" means both "charity" and "merit/righteousness," playing on the title of the book</note> for us that we bring to light the mysteries of wisdom, **The Gates of Righteousness** <note>original: "Sha'arei Tzedek," a foundational 13th-century Kabbalistic text by Rabbi Joseph Gikatilla</note>, since we already have light from the book **The Gates of Light** <note>original: "Sha'arei Orah," also by Gikatilla, which Marcaria printed in 1558</note> previously printed here. It shall be counted to us as righteousness; "and through righteousness, may you behold His face," for you will understand and know the secret of the <term>Kabbalah</term> <note>The tradition of Jewish mystical interpretation and the study of divine emanations</note>. As it is said: "For one who comes to purify himself, they open the way for him."

The gates have not been locked. This is "a ladder set on the ground, and its top reaches heaven," and the "Work of the Chariot" <note>original: "Ma'aseh Merkavah," a term referring to the earliest form of Jewish mysticism centered on the vision of the Divine Throne</note>. "Lift up your heads, O gates!" and "Righteousness, righteousness shall you pursue." "Turn it and turn it, for everything is in it" <note>An Aramaic idiom from the Ethics of the Fathers (Pirkei Avot) suggesting that all wisdom is contained within the Torah; original: "Hafoch bah v'hafoch bah d'kula bah"</note>. "Enter His gates with thanksgiving," then my tongue shall sing. "Open for me the gates of righteousness; I will enter them and thank the Lord."

->The words of the physician, though not for himself<-
<note>A humble literary signature used by Jacob Marcaria, who was a physician by profession as well as a printer</note>

->Jacob Marcaria<-

He links the book to his previous printing of "Sha'arei Orah," suggesting they form a complete path for the student of Kabbalah.</summary>

# A Question

A friend of my soul asked of me to illuminate one of the inner insights <note>The Hebrew "Hassagot" refers to intellectual or prophetic attainments</note>, to know in what way the light of the Lord travels regarding the matter of the **Ten Sefirot** <note>The ten creative powers or emanations through which the Infinite God interacts with the universe</note>. Behold, I have favored you to fulfill your requests, and may God grant you favor.

The beginning of the path through which a person enters into the **Unification of the Creator** <note>original: "Yichud HaBoreh," the meditative practice of declaring and understanding God's absolute oneness</note>, blessed be He, is the knowledge of the Gate. For do you not know that if a person desires to see the face of the King and toils for many days, and after all that toil he does not merit to know the location of the Gate, what benefit is there in all the toil he toiled and all the way he walked? When he does not know the Gate through which to enter to see the face of the King?

And in this way, most people burden themselves to enter the **Orchard** <note>original: "Pardes," a term for the four levels of Torah interpretation, specifically the highest level: the mystical secrets</note>, but they do not know and do not understand, and they walk in darkness; and there is no benefit in their labor. Therefore, I saw fit to illuminate a path before you, the way in which you should walk and the gate through which you should enter. Then you shall be pleasing and accepted before the Lord God, blessed be He, and you will be among those who merit to behold the pleasantness of the Lord and to visit His Temple. "This is the gate of the Lord; the righteous shall enter through it."

Know that the Lord God, blessed be He, chose Israel when He divided all the languages in the **Generation of Dispersion** <note>original: "Dor HaPalaga," referring to the biblical story of the Tower of Babel</note>, each to his own language, according to their families, in their nations. And He gave to each and every nation a **Prince** <note>original: "Sar," an angelic governor or celestial minister</note> and ruler over them, and He also gave to each nation its portion of the land. In this way, the nations and the lands were each unified under a specific Prince. And about this it is said: *From these the maritime nations branched out into their territories by their clans within their nations, each with its own language.* And this is the secret of the matter of the Dispersion. And all seventy nations remained delivered into the hands of **seventy celestial Ministers** <note>original: "Shiv'im Sarim Lemala," the traditional Jewish concept that there are 70 primary nations, each overseen by a unique angelic force</note> above, and those seventy Ministers surround

<summary>Rabbi Gikatilla begins his treatise by explaining that mystical knowledge requires finding the correct "Gate" to the King. He introduces the concept of the 70 Nations and their celestial Princes as a backdrop for the unique spiritual path of Israel.</summary>

<meta>This page serves as a printer's preface by Jacob Marcaria. # And You, the Reader

It shall be credited to us as a righteous deed <note>original: "Tzedakah." The printer is punning on the title of the book, "Sha'arei Tzedek" (The Gates of Justice/Righteousness)</note> that we bring to light the mysteries of wisdom: 
**The Gates of Justice** <note>original: "Sha'arei Tzedek," the title of this book</note>, following the light we gained in the book **The Gates of Light** <note>original: "Sha'arei Orah," another major work by the same author, Joseph Gikatilla, which this printer had already published</note> which has already been printed here. 

And may you consider our work a merit; and "in righteousness behold His face." For you shall understand and know the secret of **Kabbalah** <note>The Jewish mystical tradition</note>; as it is said of the one who comes to be purified: "the openings are opened for him." 

And the gates were not locked; a "ladder set on the ground, and its top reaching the heavens," and the **Work of the Chariot** <note>original: "Ma'aseh Merkavah," the ancient Jewish mystical study of the divine throne and the celestial realms</note>. 

"Lift up your heads, O gates," and "Justice, justice shall you pursue" <note>A biblical pun on the book's title "Tzedek" (Justice)</note>. Turn the book over and over, for everything is in it <note>A reference to a famous saying in Pirkei Avot regarding the depth of sacred study</note>. "Enter His gates with thanksgiving"; then shall my tongue sing for joy. "Open for me the gates of justice, I will enter them and thank the Lord."

->So says the physician of L’Ala<-

->Jacob Marcaria<-

--- Page 20 ---
<meta>This page continues the philosophical and mystical exposition of Joseph Gikatilla, moving from the printer's preface into the core teachings of the "Sha’arei Tzedek" (Gates of Justice). The text focuses on the spiritual lineage of the Patriarchs and the special status of Jacob as the "Middle Pillar" and the "Throne" of the Divine Presence.</meta>

<page-num>3</page-num>

Woe to the people who insult this verse! And concerning this, it is said: "The righteous is gathered in because of the evil" <note>Isaiah 57:1. The text suggests that the death of a righteous person is a protective measure or a consequence of the generation's sins</note>. 
And this is what the Torah says: "This is the Torah: when a man dies in a tent," and so on <note>Numbers 19:14. In Rabbinic tradition, this is interpreted to mean that the Torah is truly acquired only by one who "kills himself" over its study, meaning total devotion</note>.

**And let us return** to the intentions of the verse: **"This is the gate of the Lord; the righteous shall enter through it"** <note>Psalm 118:20</note>. This means we must seek the gate through which one enters into the presence of the Lord, may He be blessed, since four partitions surround His palace.

**And we shall say** that after Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob came, they repaired all the "Chariots" <term>Merkavot: the divine structures or "vehicles" that manifest God's presence in the world</term>, both the upper and the lower, the outer ones on the outside and the inner ones on the inside. It is found that Abraham and Isaac bound the outer Chariot on the outside through Ishmael and Esau <note>Ishmael and Esau represent the "husks" or outer shells that emerged from the holy Patriarchs</note>. But when Jacob came—who was perfect, without a trace of blemish, refined sevenfold like silver through the lineage of Seth, Enoch, Noah, Shem, Abraham, and Isaac—Jacob became the seventh. Regarding them, it is said: "Silver purged in a crucible of earth, refined seven times." Thus, his "bed was complete" <note>A traditional teaching that unlike Abraham and Isaac, all of Jacob's children remained within the covenantal faith</note>. Therefore, the Name [of God] chose him, for he is the **Middle Line** <term>Kav Ha-Emtza'i: the Kabbalistic path of balance and beauty (Tiferet) that harmonizes the extremes of Mercy and Judgment</term>. He unified the pure with the pure, and the pure rested upon the pure. Then, the Lord found a portion for him in prophecy, as it is written: "For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself, Israel for His treasure." And this is the secret of: "For the Lord’s portion is His people; Jacob is the lot of His inheritance," and so on. And the secret of: "And He brought him outside," and "He found him in a desert land... the Lord alone led him." 

Since He chose Jacob, who is the middle point in the Middle Line, Jacob became a **complete Throne** for the Name, may He be blessed. For Abraham and Isaac were only called "Thrones of the Patriarchs," as indicated by: "The God of Abraham, the God of Isaac, and the God of Jacob." Concerning this, it is said: "You have affirmed the Lord... and the Lord has affirmed you." The secret is that the twelve tribes correspond to the twelve names that are joined together from the Unique Name <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter name of God</note>, blessed be He. The verse proclaims and says: "All these are the tribes of Israel, twelve in number... and this is what their father spoke to them, and he blessed them." Then Jacob became a complete Throne for the Chariot—meaning the "Throne of the Body" and not just the "Throne of the Earth." For the Divine Presence <term>Shekhinah: the indwelling presence of God in the world</term> did not dwell below until the "Fourth Leg" arrived <note>Refers to the completion of the four-legged Divine Throne; Jacob is often identified as the fourth leg, completing the structure started by the three Patriarchs</note>—meaning in a state of rest. Previously, it was only temporary, even in the days of Moses our Teacher, peace be upon him. Concerning this, it is said regarding the Tabernacle of the Jubilee: "They shall go up to the mountain."

**And after** you know this, know that just as the Lord chose Jacob from the rest of the nations, so He chose Zion from all lands to dwell in, and Jerusalem from all places. And just as a portion fell to each celestial Prince among the nations and the lands during the "Generation of Division" <note>The Tower of Babel incident where nations were divided under various angelic rulers</note>, so did the portion of the Lord, may He be blessed, ascend: from the nations, Jacob; from the lands, the Land of Israel; and from the places, Jerusalem. Regarding this, it is said: "For Your name is called upon Your city and upon Your people." And just as Jacob is in the middle of the Patriarchs, so Jerusalem is in the middle of the inhabited world. Regarding this, it is said: "This is Jerusalem; I have set her in the midst of the nations and the countries round about her." Thus, the secret of "the Land and the outer areas" is explained. Regarding this, it is said: "You have affirmed the Lord, and the Lord has affirmed you." 

From this, you learn the secret of "And He brought him outside"—that Abraham had no root or visible place of possession except in the externals and the "shells" <term>Kelipot: the forces of concealment or "husks" that hide the divine light</term> called "uncircumcision." Therefore, the beginning of circumcision was for Abraham, which is the secret of the Covenant. When Abraham was uprooted from the place of his planting, the Holy One, blessed be He, returned and planted him in the inner place. The sign for this is: "And I took your father from across the river," etc. Guard this great principle, for it is an inner secret from the chambers of the Torah.

**And after** you know this, know that the Lord, may He be blessed—the Great Name of the Lord—is called upon Jacob. Since Jacob inherited the inheritance of the Great Lord, the Lord informed Jacob which gate he should enter when he needs to enter His chambers—which is not the case for any other nation or tongue. Regarding this, it is said: "He tells His words to Jacob, His statutes and judgments to Israel; He has not done so for any [other] nation," and so on. And from where do we know that the Name, blessed be He, informed Jacob how to come to the gate where he would enter His palaces? It is written: "And he encountered the place and stayed the night there..." <note>Genesis 28:11, referring to the "Gate of Heaven" at Bethel</note>

<summary>This page explains Jacob's unique status as the "Middle Line" and the "Throne" of God, contrasting his spiritual perfection with the earlier states of Abraham and Isaac. It establishes a mystical link between the people of Israel, the Land of Israel, and Jerusalem as the central points of divine contact.</summary>

--- Page 21 ---
<meta>continuity: This page continues the discussion of the spiritual architecture of the universe, focusing on how the Patriarchs—Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob—relate to the internal and external spiritual realms, and how the "seventy nations" surround the divine center.</meta>

...outside the Throne of Glory, and they are what are called the "Secret of the Husks" <term>Qelippot: literally "shells" or "husks," these are the forces of impurity or "the other side" that conceal the divine light</term>, and within them is caught the secret of the "Foreskin" <term>Orlah: a metaphor for a spiritual barrier or blockage that prevents holiness from being revealed</term>.

And when Abraham our father, peace be upon him, arrived in that category in which he existed, he provided a place of attachment for the "Crowns" <note>original: kheterim; here referring to spiritual emanations or powers</note> that were on the Right Side through Ishmael. This is like the parable of a thread that connects the pieces of a garment. Concerning this, it is said: "And as for Ishmael, I have heard you" <note>Genesis 17:20</note>. This is the secret of "His hand shall be against every man, and every man's hand against him" <note>Genesis 16:12</note> and "he shall dwell in the presence of all his brothers."

And when Isaac arrived in that category in which he existed, he provided a place of attachment for the Crowns that were on the Left Side through Esau. This follows the same parable of the thread we mentioned above. Concerning this, it is said: "The arrogance of your heart has deceived you, you who dwell in the clefts of the rock, whose habitation is high" <note>Obadiah 1:3; Esau is traditionally identified with the nation of Edom and the "Left Side," which represents strict Judgment</note>.

Thus we find that Ishmael and Esau represent the secret of the attachment of the Seventy Crowns that surround the Chariot <term>Merkavah: the Divine Chariot or Throne-structure described by the prophet Ezekiel</term>. All of these are called "Outer Husks" and "Foreskin." In Scripture, they are called "The Outside," as it is said: "While as yet He had not made the earth, nor the fields (the outside)" <note>Proverbs 8:26. The Hebrew word 'chutzot' (fields/streets) is interpreted here as 'the external realms'</note>. This is also the secret of "the dead body outside" <note>Numbers 19:11</note> and "the wife of the dead shall not be married outside" <note>Deuteronomy 25:5</note>.

And when Jacob our father, peace be upon him, arrived, he stood in the middle. Abraham was on the Right side, Isaac was on the Left side, and Jacob was in the middle. The secret of this is: "And Jacob was a simple man, dwelling in tents" <note>Genesis 25:27. In Kabbalah, "dwelling in tents" implies he integrated the qualities of the other two</note>. It is found that Abraham and Isaac repaired the entire Chariot, both the outer and the inner. They repaired the inner Chariot so that it might be attached to Jacob in the "Beauty" <term>Tiferet: the central attribute of harmony and truth</term> on the Middle Line. And they repaired the outer Chariot—the seventy Crowns—so they would be attached to the hand of the Lord, may He be blessed, through Ishmael and Esau.

Consequently, when Jacob arrived, he was pure and without dross. Therefore, he is in the middle. This is the secret of what Jacob said: "Perhaps, God forbid, there is a blemish in my bed?" <note>A reference to a Talmudic teaching where Jacob worries that one of his children might be spiritually unworthy, unlike Abraham who produced Ishmael and Isaac who produced Esau</note>. They all opened their mouths and said: "Hear, O Israel, the Lord is our God, the Lord is One" <note>Deuteronomy 6:4</note>. Thus Jacob and his sons are attached to the Middle Line, which is called the "Inner Point" and the "Inner Palace." And this Inner Palace, called the Middle Line, is what is called by the name of the Lord, may He be blessed; it is in the center, and seventy Crowns surround it. This is what is said in the *Book of Formation*: "The Holy Palace is set exactly in the center" <note>Sefer Yetzirah 4:4</note>. This is the secret of "The Earth and the Outside" mentioned above. This is the place called the Middle Line, and we must know in which place and through which gate one enters it.

### And we shall say:

->Know that since the seventy<-
->nations were divided into seventy<-

Crowns, every nation and its land under a known Prince <note>Referring to the 'Sarim' or guardian angels of the nations</note>, the Name (the Lord), may He be blessed, found no portion among them because He is pure and they are all impure. As it is written: "You who are of purer eyes than to behold evil" <note>Habakkuk 1:13</note>. Concerning this, it is said: "And it came to pass, as they journeyed from the east" <note>Genesis 11:2</note>—this means they surely journeyed away and separated themselves from the Ancient One of the world <note>A pun on 'Kedem' (East) and 'Kadmon' (Ancient/Original)</note>. The Divine Name had no portion in all the nations or all the lands because they were all impure, until Jacob came, who is positioned between Abraham and Isaac. He is the portion of the Lord, may He be blessed. For the Lord, may He be blessed, dwells in the Inner Palace, and the Princes surround His throne, like the relationship between the fruit and the husks.

And this is the great secret that includes everything: "It is better to take refuge in the Lord than to trust in man. It is better to take refuge in the Lord than to trust in princes. All nations surrounded me... in the name of the Lord I will cut them off. They surrounded me, indeed they surrounded me... in the name of the Lord I will cut them off. They surrounded me like bees, they were extinguished like a fire of thorns; in the name of the Lord I will cut them off" <note>Psalm 118:8-12</note>. The word "I will cut them off" (*amilam*) is said three times, and "surrounded me" is said four times. This is the secret of the four partitions that Ezekiel saw, and that Elijah saw <note>1 Kings 19:11-12</note>. The fourth is the "still small voice." I am hinting here at the "case" called the Outer Throne, which is called "Man" (*Adam*), while the Inner Throne is called "Lord" (*Adonai*).

And the secret of all these things is: "When you come into the land... you shall count the fruit thereof as forbidden" <note>Leviticus 19:23; the word for 'forbidden' is 'orlah' or foreskin</note>. (The secret of three times 70 is 210, the years of the Egyptian exile). "Three years it shall be forbidden to you... and in the fourth year... all the fruit shall be holy... and in the fifth year you may eat the fruit thereof." When you merit to understand what we have written, you will grasp the secret of "the earth and the outside," and you will grasp "It is better to take refuge in the Lord than to trust in princes." This secret is hinted at in the image of the Throne: "the likeness of the appearance of a man" <note>Ezekiel 1:26</note> and the secret of "when the plague of leprosy is in a man, he shall be brought to the priest" <note>Leviticus 13:9</note>.

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic concept of the "Middle Line" represented by Jacob, who mediates between the qualities of Abraham and Isaac to create a pure space for the Divine. It contrasts the inner holiness of Israel with the "seventy nations" and "husks" (Qelippot) that surround the Divine Palace like a peel around a fruit.</summary>
<keywords>Jacob, Abraham, Isaac, Middle Line, Tiferet, Qelippot, Ishmael, Esau, Seventy Nations, Inner Palace, Sefer Yetzirah, Merkavah, Foreskin (Orlah)</keywords>

--- Page 22 ---
<meta>continuity: This page continues the discussion of the spiritual lineage of the Patriarchs, moving from the external "husks" or surrounding nations discussed on the previous page to the internal "Gate" of the Divine Presence and the unique status of Jacob and Moses.</meta>

"And I will make you a great nation..." and so on, "and you shall be a blessing" <note>Genesis 12:2</note>. 
What is the meaning of **"and you shall be a blessing"**? It means: "In you I shall rest the **Divine Presence** <term>Shekhinah: the indwelling presence of God, often associated with the feminine aspect of the Divine</term>," for she is the "Blessing." She is the **tenth emanation** <term>Sefirah: one of the ten attributes or channels through which the Divine is revealed</term> which receives a flow of light from the nine **illuminating mirrors** <term>Aspaklaria: a philosophical and mystical term for a lens or mirror through which a prophet perceives divine truth</term>. And even though God transmitted the "Blessing" to Abraham, He did not transmit **the Gate** to him. The same was true for Isaac, because of the "waste" <note>original: pesolet; referring to spiritual impurities or unrefined elements</note> within him—namely Ishmael and Esau. But Jacob, in whom there was no waste, had the Gate transmitted to him, as we have explained. 

And as for what the Blessed Lord said to Abraham, "and in you all the families of the earth shall be blessed" <note>Genesis 12:3</note>, it is all explained by the fact that the seventy families of the world are attached to Abraham and Isaac. This is based on the linguistic connection between "blessing" <note>original: ha-berakhah</note> and "grafting" <note>original: ha-vrakhah; a play on words suggesting the nations are grafted onto the holy root</note>. Were it not for Abraham and Isaac, the seventy nations would have no attachment to the **Divine Chariot** <term>Merkavah: the conceptual structure of the divine realm as seen in Ezekiel's vision</term>. This is the secret of the seventy bulls offered during the Festival <note>Sukkot; seventy bulls were traditionally sacrificed for the welfare of the seventy nations</note>.

## And After
you have understood this, know that the Gate called **Righteousness** <note>original: Tzedek; also associated with the Sefirah of Malkhut</note>, even though Abraham is the root of that emanation, he did not inherit the Gate until Jacob arrived. Regarding this Gate, it was said of Abraham: "Who raised up the righteous man from the east, and called him to his feet?" <note>Isaiah 41:2</note>. This is the secret of the "Gates of Righteousness."

## And Know
that from the time Jacob knew the Gate and took the **Key**, no creature in the world may enter unto the Blessed Lord except through Jacob and his sons—meaning, until they convert and are circumcised and come "under the wings of the Divine Presence." Through that same gate, they enter the "Gates of Righteousness." For this reason, true converts are called **"Righteous Proselytes"** <term>Gerei Tzedek: converts who join the Jewish faith out of sincere conviction</term>. This means the converts enter the Palace of the Blessed Name (YHVH) through the Gate of Righteousness. Guard this principle well: because the root of Abraham is the emanation of Righteousness, all converts are called "Righteous Proselytes" in his name. The secret of "they shall be blessed in you" is that they are literally "grafted" in you.

## And After
you have understood this, we must illuminate a path before you regarding this emanation called the **Gate to the Lord**. We say that not every person is worthy to enter this gate as a permanent resident, but rather only as a temporary visitor, to enter and depart. No one may remain inside as a permanent dweller—not even Abraham, Isaac, or Jacob, nor all the prophets. They did not merit to remain always inside; they would enter and exit. No one maintained a permanent dwelling within this Gate except for **Moses our Teacher**, peace be upon him, alone. 

Concerning this, it was said to the people: "Go say to them: Return to your tents," but to Moses: "But as for you, stand here with Me" <note>Deuteronomy 5:27-28</note>. This refers to the mirror that is not luminous, whereas Moses gazed through the **Luminous Mirror**. The reason is that he entered *within* this Gate; he entered with permission and established his residence there. This is the secret of: "And Moses was not able to enter the Tent of Meeting because the cloud dwelt upon it... and the glory of the Lord filled the Tabernacle" <note>Exodus 40:35</note>—until the Blessed Lord called to him and he entered with permission. Regarding this it is said: "And He called to Moses, and the Lord spoke to him from the Tent of Meeting" <note>Leviticus 1:1</note> and it is written, "But as for you, stand here with Me." 

Since he established his residence there, he never ceased. Regarding this, it is said: "Let him kiss me with the kisses of his mouth" <note>Song of Songs 1:2</note>. Because Moses entered within the Gate and established his residence there, he was always looking through the **Luminous Mirror**. But the other prophets, whose residence was not permanent within the Gate, even in the moment of their vision, they were looking from outside the gatehouse. They saw from behind the "Tenth Partition," which is the **Mirror that is not Luminous**. This is the secret of: "Hear now my words: If there be a prophet among you..." but "My servant Moses is not so; he is faithful in all My house" <note>Numbers 12:6-7</note>. "In all My house" literally means he entered and established his residence within the Gate. Thus, he was found faithful with all the King's property—which are the chambers called the "House of the King." He is faithful in all My house.

## And After
you have understood this, know that this attribute called the **Gates of Righteousness**, which Jacob inherited, he delivered all of its **Keys** into the hands of his sons. This Gate has thirteen keys: twelve for the twelve chambers <note>representing the twelve tribes</note>, and the inner key, which completes the thirteen, he delivered its path to **Levi**. This is the secret of the Priestly Blessing, for from there the blessings originate.

<summary>This page explains that while Abraham and Isaac provided the spiritual root for the world's nations, Jacob alone inherited the "Gate" to the Divine. It distinguishes Moses as the only prophet to dwell permanently within this divine realm, looking through a "Luminous Mirror," while others viewed from the outside.</summary>

<meta>continuity: This page continues the exploration of Jacob’s unique spiritual status compared to his predecessors, Abraham and Isaac, specifically focusing on his vision of the ladder and his access to the "Gate" of the Divine Presence.</meta>

"And he stayed there and dreamed, and behold, a ladder was set on the ground" <note>Genesis 28:12</note>. Jacob knew of the Kingdom of the Lord, may He be blessed, but he did not know through which gate he should enter it. Therefore, the Blessed Name showed him the way of entry. From where do we know this? As it is written: "And he dreamed, and behold, a ladder was set on the ground... and behold, the Lord stood over him" <note>Genesis 28:12-13</note>. He said: "Behold, see the gate through which you shall enter the ladder," for it is written of it, "and behold, the Lord stood over him." And what is written after it? "And Jacob awoke from his sleep and said: Surely the Lord is in this place... and this is the **gate of heaven**" <note>Genesis 28:16-17</note>. Thus, Jacob merited to perceive the gate through which one enters the courts of the Lord, may He be blessed, as it is written: "This is the gate to the Lord; the righteous shall enter through it" <note>Psalm 118:20</note>. And since he perceived this gate, he merited to enter the **twelve diagonal boundaries** <term>Gevulim Alakhsoniyim: a kabbalistic concept referring to the twelve edges of a cube that define the dimensions of the spiritual and physical cosmos</term>, which are the secret of the upper and lower worlds. Everything is held together by the secret of the **thirteen attributes** <note>original: middot; the thirteen attributes of Divine Mercy</note> through which the Torah is interpreted, and the secret of the twelve tribes which are thirteen <note>referring to the division of Joseph into Ephraim and Manasseh</note>, and the secret of the twelve months which are thirteen in the secret of the **intercalation** <term>Ibbur: the practice of adding a leap month to the Hebrew calendar to align the lunar year with the solar seasons</term>.

### And Know

When Jacob came and perceived the place of the gate, he ascended to the height of the ladder—something that Abraham and Isaac did not merit or perceive. For Abraham and Isaac only reached the place of the "shoulders," and this is the secret of "beneath the everlasting arms" <note>Deuteronomy 33:27</note>. They did not ascend further. Were it not for this, the seed of Jacob would have no standing among the nations, between Ishmael and Esau. Those shoulders are called the "high places of the earth." The great secret is "the God of Abraham, the God of Isaac," but higher than them is "the God of Jacob." He is the **Central Pillar** <term>Kav Ha-Emtza'i: the middle line of balance on the Tree of Life that connects the highest and lowest points</term> that ascends until it takes hold of the **Crown** <term>Keter: the highest and most hidden of the ten Sefirot, representing the Divine Will</term>. "This, your stature, is like a palm tree," but higher still: "and your breasts like clusters" <note>Song of Songs 7:8</note>. And the secret of "He made him ride on the high places of the earth" <note>Deuteronomy 32:13</note> and "you shall tread upon their high places" <note>Deuteronomy 33:29</note>. "The eternal God is a dwelling place, and underneath are the everlasting arms; and He drove out the enemy from before you and said, 'Destroy!'" <note>Deuteronomy 33:27</note>. "And Israel shall dwell in safety, alone, the eye of Jacob" <note>Deuteronomy 33:28</note>—specifically the "eye of Jacob" and not the "eye of Abraham or Isaac." This is because Abraham and Isaac are the "arms," and they are "underneath," for within them Ishmael, Esau, and the **seventy crowns** <note>original: keterim; the spiritual roots of the seventy nations of the world</note> take hold. 

But Jacob, who merited to perceive the gate and ascend by way of the ladder, took hold of the Central Pillar until the height of the ascent, reaching the **Infinite** <term>Ein Sof: the Boundless or Infinite aspect of God prior to self-manifestation</term> by way of the "neck." Therefore, regarding Abraham and Isaac it is written, "Lift up your eyes... westward and northward and southward and eastward" <note>Genesis 13:14; implies boundaries</note>, and likewise for Isaac. But regarding Jacob it says, "And you shall burst forth westward and eastward and northward and southward" <note>Genesis 28:14</note>. This is an **inheritance without boundaries**. Because no ministering angel of any of the nations ascends to that place, it is said: "For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself, Israel for His own treasure" <note>Psalm 135:4</note>. And it is written: "Then you shall delight in the Lord... and I will cause you to ride on the high places of the earth, and I will feed you the heritage of Jacob your father" <note>Isaiah 58:14</note>—the heritage of Jacob specifically, and not the heritage of Abraham and Isaac.

### And After

Now that we have informed you of these general principles, know that the secret of this gate which Jacob merited to perceive is the secret of the **tenth emanation**, which is called:

->**Divine Presence**<-
<note>original: Shekhinah</note>

The sign of this is "And Israel shall dwell in safety, alone, the eye of Jacob." And when Jacob perceived this gate, which is the secret of the Divine Presence—also called **Righteousness** <note>original: Tzedek</note>—then the scripture proclaims and says: "Open for me the gates of righteousness; I will enter them and praise the Lord" <note>Psalm 118:19</note>. For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself, and how far shall I enter? Until the place called the "neck"—"I will enter them and praise the Lord." This is the secret of "As for me, I shall behold Your face in righteousness" <note>Psalm 17:15</note>, meaning: "Since I have merited to find the gate, I shall then enter to see the face of the King."

### And After

After you have known this, know that this gate through which one enters to perceive and see the face of the Blessed King is the secret of the **Unity** of our Blessed Creator. It is the secret of the **blessing** <note>original: berakhah</note> that the Blessed Name transmitted to Abraham. For all types of abundance and emanation that flow from the **Upper Eden** <note>referring to the Sefirah of Wisdom or Crown</note> called "Crown," by way of the **Beauty** <note>original: Tiferet</note> called "River," all enter into the **Tenth Pool** <term>Bereikhah: a play on words between 'blessing' (berakhah) and 'pool' or 'reservoir' (bereikhah)</term>, which is the secret of the **Kingdom** <note>original: Malchut</note>. Regarding this, it is written: "the wells of Abraham and Isaac." The Blessed Lord transmitted this "Pool" (Blessing) to Abraham so that all the nations would be blessed through it, and this is what he meant when he said—

<meta>This page continues the discussion of Jacob’s spiritual superiority over his forefathers. "And I will make of you a great nation... and you shall be a blessing" <note>Genesis 12:2</note>.
What is the meaning of "you shall be a blessing"? It means: "In you, I cause the **Divine Presence** <term>Shekhinah: the indwelling presence of God, often viewed as the feminine aspect of the Divine</term> to rest," for she is the **Pool** <note>original: bereikhah; a play on the word "blessing" (berakhah)</note>. She is the **Tenth Emanation** <term>Sefirah: one of the ten attributes through which the Infinite reveals Himself</term>, which receives the flow of light from the nine **shining mirrors** <term>Aspaklaria'ot: mirrors or lenses through which divine light is filtered</term>. Even though God gave the "Pool" to Abraham, He did not give him the "Gate." The same was true for Isaac, because of the spiritual impurities <note>original: pesoret</note> of Ishmael and Esau. But Jacob, in whom there was no impurity, was given the Gate, as we have explained.
And as for what the Blessed Lord said to Abraham, "And in you all the families of the earth shall be blessed" <note>Genesis 12:3</note>—this is all clarified by the fact that the seventy nations of the world are attached to Abraham and Isaac through the process of refinement and composition. If not for Abraham and Isaac, the seventy nations would have no attachment to the **Divine Chariot** <term>Merkabah: the structure of the celestial realms</term>. This is the secret of the seventy bulls offered during the Holiday <note>Sukkot; seventy bulls were traditionally offered for the seventy nations of the world</note>.

## Now that you know this, know that the Gate 
called **Righteousness** <note>original: Tzedek</note>, even though Abraham inherited the Sefirah, he did not inherit the Gate until Jacob came. Concerning this Gate, it was said of Abraham: "Who raised up the one from the east, whom **Righteousness** <note>Tzedek</note> called to his feet?" <note>Isaiah 41:2</note>. This is the secret of the **Gates of Righteousness**.

## Know that from the time Jacob knew the Gate 
and took the key, no created being in the world has the power to enter before the Blessed Name except through Jacob and his children—meaning, until they convert and are circumcised and come under the wings of the Divine Presence. Through that same Gate they shall enter the "Gates of Righteousness." Because of this, true converts are called **Righteous Converts** <term>Gerei HaTzedek: proselytes of righteousness</term>. This means the converts enter the Palace of the Blessed Lord through the Gate of Righteousness. Guard this principle: because Abraham inherited the Sefirah of Righteousness, all converts are called by his name, "Righteous Converts." And the secret...

...Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob, and all the prophets did not merit to remain inside the inner sanctum at all times; rather, they would enter and exit. No one remained in a permanent dwelling before Him within this Gate except for **Moses our Teacher**, peace be upon him, alone. Regarding this, it was said: "Go, say to them: Return to your tents. But as for you, stand here with Me" <note>Deuteronomy 5:27-28</note>. This is what the Sages meant when they said: "All the prophets looked through a **mirror that is not clear**, but Moses looked through a **clear mirror**" <term>Aspaklaria Metzuchtzachet: a "polished" or "clear" lens of prophecy</term>. The reason is that he entered inside this Gate; he entered with permission and established his dwelling there. This is the secret of "And Moses was not able to enter the Tent of Meeting because the cloud dwelt upon it... and the Glory of the Lord filled the Tabernacle" <note>Exodus 40:35</note>, until the Blessed Lord called to him and he entered with permission. Concerning this it is said: "And He called to Moses, and the Lord spoke to him from the Tent of Meeting" <note>Leviticus 1:1</note>, and it is written, "But as for you, stand here with Me." Since he established his dwelling there, it never ceased. Regarding this it is said: "Let him kiss me with the kisses of his mouth" <note>Song of Songs 1:2</note>. Because Moses entered inside the Gate and established his dwelling there, he was always seeing through the **clear mirror**. But the other prophets, whose dwelling was not permanent inside the Gate, could only look momentarily from outside the gateway; they saw from behind the Tenth Partition, which is the **mirror that is not clear**. This is the secret of "Hear now my words: If there be a prophet among you... not so My servant Moses; in all My house he is trusted" <note>Numbers 12:6-7</note>. "In all My house" literally means that he entered and established his dwelling inside the Gate. Thus he was found trusted with all the King's property, which are the chambers called the "King's House." "In all My house he is trusted."

"And he stayed there and dreamed, and behold, a ladder was set on the ground..." <note>Genesis 28:11-12</note>
Jacob knew the **Kingdom** <term>Malchut: the tenth and final Sefirah, representing the manifest presence of God</term> of the Blessed Lord, but he did not know through which gate he should enter it. The Blessed God showed him the way of entry, as it is written: "And he dreamed, and behold, a ladder was set on the ground... and behold, the Lord stood over it." "And Jacob awoke from his sleep and said: Surely the Lord is in this place... and this is the **gate of heaven**" <note>Genesis 28:16-17</note>.

And after he grasped this gate, he merited to enter the **twelve diagonal boundaries** <term>Gvulei Alachson: a concept from the Book of Creation (Sefer Yetzirah) referring to the fundamental axes of the universe</term>, which are the secret of the upper and lower worlds. Everything is held together by the secret of the **thirteen attributes** <note>The Thirteen Attributes of Mercy</note> by which the Torah is interpreted, and the secret of the twelve tribes which are actually thirteen <note>referring to the division of Joseph into Ephraim and Manasseh</note>, and the secret of the twelve months which become thirteen in the secret of the **intercalation** <note>The Hebrew leap year (Ibbur), which adds a 13th month</note>.

### Know this:
When Jacob came and grasped the location of the gate, he ascended to the top of the ladder—something that Abraham and Isaac did not merit or achieve. For Abraham and Isaac only reached the "place of the shoulders," which is the secret of "underneath are the everlasting arms" <note>Deuteronomy 33:27; original: "mitachat zro'ot olam"</note>. They did not ascend further. Were it not for this, the seed of Jacob would have no survival among the nations, between Ishmael and Esau. Those "shoulders" are called the "heights of the earth."

The great secret is "the God of Abraham, the God of Isaac," but above them is "and the God of Jacob." He is the **Middle Line** <term>Kav Ha-Emtza'i: the central pillar of the Sefirotic tree that balances the extremes of Mercy and Judgment</term> that ascends until it takes hold of the **Crown** <term>Keter: the highest Sefirah, representing the Divine Will</term>. "This your stature is like a palm tree" <note>Song of Songs 7:8</note>, but higher up, "and your breasts like clusters." This is the secret of "He made him ride on the **heights of the earth**" <note>Deuteronomy 32:13</note>, and "you shall tread upon their high places" <note>Deuteronomy 33:29</note>. "The eternal God is a dwelling place, and underneath are the everlasting arms... and He drove out the enemy before you and said: Destroy!" "And Israel shall dwell in safety, alone, the **eye of Jacob**" <note>Deuteronomy 33:27-28</note>. 

It does not say "the eye of Abraham and Isaac," because Abraham and Isaac are the "arms" and are "below," since Ishmael, Esau, and the seventy crowns <note>The seventy spiritual princes of the nations</note> take hold of them. But Jacob, who merited to grasp the Gate and ascend by way of the ladder, is held by the Middle Line unto the highest height, unto the **Infinite** <term>Ein Sof: the Boundless God prior to self-manifestation</term> by way of the **Neck** <note>Tzavar: in Kabbalistic anatomy, the transition between the higher intellect and the lower body</note>. 

Therefore, regarding Abraham and Isaac it is written: "Lift up your eyes westward and northward and southward and eastward" <note>Genesis 13:14</note>, but regarding Jacob it says: "And you shall burst forth to the west and to the east and to the north and to the south" <note>Genesis 28:14</note>. This is an "inheritance without boundaries" <note>original: nachalah bli meitzarim</note>. Because no prince of any of the nations' ministers ascends to that place, it is said: "For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself, Israel for His own treasure" <note>Psalm 135:4</note>. And it is written: "Then you shall delight in the Lord" certainly, "and I will cause you to ride on the heights of the earth, and I will feed you the heritage of Jacob your father" <note>Isaiah 58:14</note>—the heritage of Jacob, and not the heritage of Abraham and Isaac.

And the tent is not spread in its proper order. Therefore, the **Shekhinah** <term>the Divine Presence manifest in the world</term> does not dwell upon a flawed thing. And this is the secret of: *A tent that shall not be taken down; its stakes shall never be removed, nor shall any of its cords be broken* <note>original: "אהל בל יצען..." Isaiah 33:20</note>. It is found that the whole body [is comprised of] **one hundred blessings**, as if one is spreading a tent. And he who knows this knows the secret of the **Tribute** <term>Terumah: a portion set aside as holy</term> and the secret of the "Tribute of the Hundred," for if there is no "Hundred," there is no "Tribute" <note>A wordplay in Hebrew where "Terumah" (תרומה) is interpreted as "Trei-Me'ah" (two from a hundred)</note>.

**And after** we have explained this, know that this **Gate** is called everywhere in the Torah:
**ADONAI** <note>The Name of God traditionally translated as "Lord"</note> and it is the palace of the Blessed **Y-H-V-H** <note>The four-letter Name of God, representing the higher, emanated light</note>. And because this **Sefirah** <term>Divine attribute or emanation</term> corresponds to the Earth, its secret is: *The man, the lord of the land, spoke...* <note>Genesis 42:30</note>. And because the first Adam caused [the Shekhinah] to depart from him below, it did not return until **Noah** came and brought it back for one hour, but no more. Then it was said: *Noah, Noah, a righteous man, perfect in his generations; Noah walked with God...* <note>Genesis 6:9</note>. And this is the secret of why he was called "Noah," from the language of *the spirit of Elijah rested (nachah) upon Elisha* <note>2 Kings 2:15</note>. *And he called his name Noah, saying: This one shall comfort us* <note>Genesis 5:29</note>. And it says: *But Noah found grace in the eyes of the Lord* <note>Genesis 6:8</note>. *And Noah built an altar... and the Lord smelled the sweet savor (nihoach)* <note>Genesis 8:20-21</note>. *And the Lord said to His heart: I will not again...* <note>Genesis 8:21</note>. All this was done, yet nevertheless, [the Earth] was not blessed until **Abraham** came and established for them the sign of the places of blessings. *And he built there an altar to the Lord... and he called upon the name of the Lord* <note>Genesis 12:7-8</note>. And Abraham our father, peace be upon him, prepared himself and made his "Right Hand" <term>the attribute of Chesed or Loving-kindness</term> a **throne for the Chariot** <term>Merkavah: the vehicle of Divine manifestation</term>, and this attribute [the Shekhinah] dwelt upon him. Therefore it is said: *And you shall be a blessing* <note>Genesis 12:2</note>. And when he entered the Land, he established the places for the Shekhinah and began to call upon the name of the Lord by the name of the Land, which is its portion. And regarding this, our sages of blessed memory said: *Cause Your face to shine upon Your sanctuary which is desolate, for the sake of the Lord (Adonai)* <note>Daniel 9:17</note>—for the sake of the one

who called You "Lord" (*Adonai*), and that is Abraham our father, peace be upon him. And when you understand these matters, many **Gates of Light** will open before you, and you will know and gain insight from the source of the matter. You will see it in every place you find in the Torah where the name **Y-H-V-H** is written as **ADONAI**, for it is the secret of the **Tenth Sefirah**. It is the **mirror that does not shine** <term>Aspaklaria she-eina me'irah: a vessel that has no light of its own but reflects the light of the higher realms</term>, and it requires illumination from that which is above it. Therefore, you find that Abraham established a place for it like one who takes hold of a thing. However, it was not established as a permanent dwelling in the ground until the **Fourth Leg** <term>referring to King David, who completes the "legs" of the Divine Throne</term>. And regarding that time it was said: *This is my resting place forever; here I will dwell, for I have desired it* <note>Psalm 132:14</note>, which is the secret of *for Your name is called upon Your city and upon Your people* <note>Daniel 9:19</note>; understand this very well.

**And after** we have explained this matter to you, know that this attribute is called in the Song of Songs: **Bride** <term>Kallah</term>. And the secret is: *As a bridegroom decks himself with a priestly crown, and as a bride adorns herself with her jewels* <note>Isaiah 61:10</note>; *With me from Lebanon, my bride* <note>Song of Songs 4:8</note>; and *He is like a bridegroom coming out of his chamber* <note>Psalm 19:6</note>.

**Know** and understand that this attribute is called everywhere the **Oral Torah** <term>Torah she-be’al peh</term>, because it is the mate to the **Written Torah**. For this reason, it is called among those who know the secret: **The Moon**. The reason for these two matters is the same: just as the Moon has no light of its own, only from the Sun, so too the Oral Torah has no foundation except from the Written Torah. Therefore, the Oral Torah was not given at Sinai to be written—so as not to establish a [fixed] place for it—except upon the Written Torah, because of the **secret of the Kiss**. This is the secret of the reason for: *Let him kiss me with the kisses of his mouth* <note>Song of Songs 1:2</note>. It was written down later only as a decree, lest the Torah be forgotten from Israel due to the persecutions.

**And behold**, I hint at the beginning and the end: in every place, the word "Speech" <note>original: "Dibur"</note> is the secret of the Written Torah, and "Saying" <note>original: "Amirah"</note> is the secret of the Oral Torah. This is what is said throughout the entire Torah: *And the Lord spoke (va-yedabber) to Moses, saying (le-emor)*. This [Oral Torah] is beautiful within that [Written Torah], and this one needs...
