<meta>This page continues the discussion of the "Hundred Sockets" and "Hundred Blessings," specifically how these spiritual currents are distributed through the twelve tribes and maintained through the priestly and levitical service, ultimately linking the human soul to the Divine.</meta>

The blessings spread out and go forth into **twelve rooms** belonging to **twelve keys** found within each and every tribe. This is the secret of: *All these are the twelve tribes of Israel... this is what their father spoke to them and blessed them, each according to his blessing he blessed them* <note>original: "כל אלה שבטי ישראל..." Genesis 49:28</note>. When Moses our Teacher—peace be upon him—came and blessed Israel, he made it known that the **master key**, which includes all other keys, is entrusted to the hands of the **Tribe of Levi**. As it says: *And of Levi he said: Your Thummim and your Urim be with your holy one* <note>original: "וללוי אמר תמיך ואוריך..." Deuteronomy 33:8</note>.

The secret of the **Urim and Thummim** <term>the oracular breastplate worn by the High Priest</term> is the secret of all the **Gates**. I will now hint at the secret of "Light" (*Orah*) and "Jacob was a simple (*Tam*) man" <note>A wordplay connecting "Urim" to "Orah" (light) and "Thummim" to "Tam" (simple/complete/wholesome)</note>. Therefore, it says in the Torah: *Speak to Aaron and his sons, saying, "In this way you shall bless the children of Israel..." and "They shall put My Name upon the children of Israel, and I will bless them"* <note>Numbers 6:23, 27</note>. Therefore, the Scripture says:

->*Therefore Levi shall have no portion or inheritance with his brothers; the Lord is his inheritance*<- <note>Deuteronomy 10:9</note>

And guard this great principle: for the Blessed Name is certainly his inheritance.

### And after
I have informed you of this, know that the **Tent of Meeting** <term>Ohel Moed: the portable sanctuary in the desert</term>, which is the secret of the **Shekhinah** <term>the Divine Presence manifest in the lower worlds</term>, is the root of Jacob; it is his portion and inheritance, a legacy for his children as a possession. Concerning this, it is said:

->*I will set My Tabernacle among you... and I will be your God*<- <note>Leviticus 26:11-12</note>

And it is written:

->*Who dwells with them in the midst of their impurity*<- <note>Leviticus 16:16</note>

Literally in their impurity! Happy is the people for whom it is thus; *happy is the people whose God is the Lord.*

### And behold
This **Attribute** <term>Midah: referring to the Sefirah of Malchut/Kingship</term> has **one hundred corners**, and every single corner has a receptacle. From every single corner, springs of blessing flow out to the world. From there, our forefather Isaac—peace be upon him—drew the **one hundred blessings** through which he became wealthy. Concerning this, it is said:

->*And Isaac sowed in that land and found in that year a hundredfold*<- <note>Genesis 26:12</note>

Literally "one hundred gates" <note>The Hebrew word "She'arim" can mean both "fold/measures" and "gates"</note>. Therefore: *And the Lord blessed him.* Therefore: *The man became great...* and therefore the Philistines and kings envied him because of the abundance of his blessings. These are the one hundred gates that Isaac found through which he was blessed; they are the one hundred gates and springs surrounding the **Gates of Justice** <term>Sha'arei Tzedek: a title for the Shekhinah</term>, from which blessing, abundance, and emanation go forth to all created beings by means of Israel.

Therefore, you will find in the Tent of Meeting **one hundred sockets** for the one hundred talents [of silver], a talent for each socket <note>Exodus 38:27</note>. There was not a single socket among them that did not have a receptacle for a column. The secret of the **Columns** is known through the **Hooks** <note>original: "Vavim"; the Hebrew letter Vav (ו) represents the number 6 and is shaped like a hook or a column</note>, as it is symbolized: *the hooks of the columns.*

The secret is the **Middle Line** <term>the central pillar of the Sefirotic tree that balances mercy and judgment</term> and "The Lord is Jacob" <note>Linking the name of God to the patriarch Jacob, who represents the central pillar</note>. Because this is the path by which every single person in Israel is blessed with one hundred types of blessings, abundance, and emanation, every person in Israel must recite **one hundred blessings** every day. That is to say, every single blessing has a spring and a source from which it is poured and emanated.

If, God forbid, a person is missing even one of the hundred blessings each day, he creates a flaw in the **entire Torah**. He is found to be damaging all the "pipes" or channels of divine flow. Your mnemonic sign for this is:

->*The Soul, Spirit, and Super-soul shall return to their Creator*<- <note>A wordplay on the three levels of the soul: Nefesh, Ruach, and Neshamah</note>

The secret is: *To the children of Levi I have given every tithe in Israel... in return for their service* <note>Numbers 18:21</note>. And it is written: *Speak to the Levites... when you take from the children of Israel the tithe... you shall set aside from it a gift for the Lord, a tithe of the tithe* <note>Numbers 18:26</note>.

It is found that Israel gives to the Levite ten out of every hundred, and the Levite gives to the Priest one out of every ten. If Israel does not have a hundred, the Levite does not have ten. If the Levite does not have ten, the Priest does not have one. Thus, the "bundle" is pulled apart, and there is no longer a vessel to pour out His blessing.

In this way, if the **Nefesh** <term>the lower, vital soul</term> does not bless one hundred blessings every day, then ten from the "tithe" will not reach the **Ruach** <term>the middle, emotional spirit</term>. When the *Ruach* does not receive ten, then the one will not reach the **Neshamah** <term>the highest, intellectual soul</term>. Behold, we are obligated every day to bind the *Nefesh* to the *Ruach*, and the *Ruach* to the *Neshamah*, so that the *Neshamah* cleaves to the Blessed **Y-H-V-H**. This is the secret of:

->*And the soul of my lord shall be bound in the bond of life with the Lord your God*<- <note>1 Samuel 25:29</note>

And this is the secret of: *Bless the Lord, O my soul* <note>Psalm 103:1</note>. And it is written: *Let every soul praise the Lord* <note>Psalm 150:6</note>.

In this way, we say that any person who does not complete one hundred blessings every day flaws his [divine] form and "cuts the plantings" <term>a Talmudic idiom for spiritual sabotage or heresy</term> of his soul. Woe to him for his deficiency, for he shall not enter the **Gates of Justice**, because the sockets for the columns are not arranged, and the tent is not spread in its proper order.

<summary>This page explains the mystical significance of the 100 daily blessings, linking them to the 100 silver sockets of the Tabernacle and the spiritual economy between the three levels of the soul (Nefesh, Ruach, Neshamah) and the tribes of Israel.</summary>
<keywords>hundred blessings, Shekhinah, twelve tribes, Levi, Isaac, soul levels, Nefesh, Ruach, Neshamah, Tabernacle sockets, Middle Line, Gates of Justice</keywords>

--- Page 28 ---
<meta>This page continues the technical discussion of how the Divine flow is channeled from the Infinite to the lower worlds. While the previous page focused on the tribal "keys" and the priestly role in maintaining these connections, this page explains how human action—specifically the observance or neglect of the commandments—affects the "channels" of the Divine Attribute of Malchut (the Shekhinah), which manifests alternatively as "Dry Land," "Sea," or the "New Moon."</meta>

This is also the nature of the Attribute [of the Shekhinah]: for through the weight of sins and the neglect of the commandments, the channels break and the flows are cut off. For transgressions shatter the **channels**. Thus Scripture says: *Go down, for your people have corrupted themselves* <note>original: "לך רד כי שחת עמך" - Exodus 32:7</note>; *He is a companion to a destroyer* <note>Proverbs 18:9</note>; and *He spilled [it] on the ground* <note>Genesis 38:9</note>. Through the neglect of the commandments, the flows cease, as it says: *Why is the land ruined and laid waste like a wilderness?* <note>Jeremiah 9:11</note>, and it continues: *Because they have forsaken My Torah,* etc. <note>Jeremiah 9:12</note>. It also says: *My soul thirsts for the living God; my flesh longs for You* <note>Psalm 63:2</note>. For this reason, it says in the Torah: *If you walk in My statutes... then I will give your rains in their season, and the land shall yield its produce* <note>Leviticus 26:3-4</note>. This means that by performing the commandments, you repair the **channels**, and the blessings flow from the **Infinite** <term>Ein Sof: the Boundless Divine source</term>. Thus, both the upper and lower beings are blessed, as it is said: *He who blesses himself in the earth shall bless himself by the God of Truth* <note>Isaiah 65:16</note>.

But if, God forbid, they transgress the Torah, it is written: *If you loathe My statutes... I will also do this to you... and I will make your heavens like iron and your earth like copper* <note>Leviticus 26:15-19</note>. For this reason, this Attribute is sometimes called **Dry**, meaning that when the channels are broken and the blessings and flows are cut off. This is hinted at in the verse: *And God called the dry land "Earth"* <note>Genesis 1:10</note>. And it is written: *And God saw the children of Israel, and God knew* <note>Exodus 2:25</note>; this "seeing" is the secret of the Divine Remembrance <term>Pakuda: a focused visitation or accounting of someone's state</term>. Sometimes this Attribute is called:

<column-break/>

### Sea

And this is when all the channels and flows run toward her from the depth of the **Infinite**. This is the secret of: *All the rivers flow into the sea, yet the sea is not full* <note>Ecclesiastes 1:7</note>. This is because she is the **mirror that does not shine** <term>Aspaklaria she-einah me’irah: the moon-like state of the Shekhinah, which has no light of its own but reflects the light of the higher Sefirot</term>. She requires illumination from the side of **Foundation** <term>Yesod: the ninth Sefirah, which funnels light into the Shekhinah</term>, as we hinted regarding the verse: *Blessed be the Lord from Zion, who dwells in Jerusalem* <note>Psalm 135:21</note>. This is the secret of: *And God called the dry land "Earth" and the gathering of waters He called "Seas"* <note>Genesis 1:10</note>. This is also the secret of the filling of the moon and the increase of the tides with the waxing of the moon, and their decrease with its waning. For when the **Assembly of Israel** <term>Knesset Yisrael: a mystical name for the Shekhinah as the spiritual collective of the Jewish people</term> receives the flow, becoming filled and saturated, then all creatures—upper and lower—are blessed through her blessing. And this Attribute is called in the Torah:

### New [Moon]

The sign for this is: *This is the burnt offering of the new moon in its month* <note>Numbers 28:14</note>. The word "**this**" (*zot*) certainly refers to this offering. The reason for the goats offered on the New Moon is that it says: *for a sin offering to the Lord* <note>original: "לחטאת ליהוה" - Numbers 28:15</note>. This is the secret of the waning of the moon and its fullness, and the secret of the **Moon’s complaint against the Sun**. The logic is that *two kings cannot share one crown* <note>A midrashic explanation for why the moon was diminished: it complained that it and the sun were equal</note>. I will hint to you: if they both used one crown, there would be no "offspring" in this world. This is the reason for the command: *Go and diminish yourself*.

One who understands this reason will understand why the lights were hung on the **fourth day** <note>of creation</note>. Initially, they were *two great lights*, but ultimately they became *the great light to rule the day and the small light*, etc. <note>Genesis 1:16</note>. Therefore, the activity of the Sun and Moon among the lower beings is by God’s decree. This is the meaning of: *And God set them in the firmament of the heaven to give light upon the earth and to rule over the day and over the night* <note>Genesis 1:17-18</note>; and the secret of *the precious fruits of the sun and the precious things put forth by the moon* <note>Deuteronomy 33:14</note>. For this reason, the **Shabbat** belongs to the Sun and the **Festivals** to the Moon.

The great secret is: *Give thanks to the Lord, for He is good... Give thanks to the God of gods... Give thanks to the Lord of lords* <note>Psalm 136:1-3</note>. This is the secret of: *From month to month and from Shabbat to Shabbat all flesh shall come to worship* <note>Isaiah 66:23</note>. One who knows this reason knows the secret of the **Sanctification of the New Moon** by the High Court and the Small Court, and the sign of **Perez and Zerah** <note>The twins of Tamar (Genesis 38), representing the moon/sun dynamics</note>. The essential point is: *The light of the moon shall be as the light of the sun, and the light of the sun shall be sevenfold* <note>Isaiah 30:26</note>. The secret of **sevenfold** refers to the **forty-nine gates** of understanding, the forty-nine days until the Giving of the Torah, and the forty-nine years until the **Jubilee** <term>Yovel: the fiftieth year when slaves were freed and land returned</term>. Then: *You shall grant redemption to the land... it shall have redemption... and in the Jubilee he shall go out* <note>Leviticus 25:24-33</note>.

He who understands these great reasons we have hinted at here will understand the secret of the union between the "Upper Beauty" and the "Lower Beauty": *Fair as the moon, clear as the sun* <note>Song of Songs 6:10</note>; and the secret of: *He made the moon for the seasons* <note>Psalm 104:19</note>; and the secret of the *round goblet* <note>Song of Songs 7:3</note> and the *Gates of Righteousness*.

<summary>This page discusses the kabbalistic dynamics of the Shekhinah (the Divine Presence), explaining how it shifts between states of "Dryness" (when human sin blocks the flow) and "Fullness" (when merit repairs the channels). It explores the relationship between the Sun and Moon as symbols of higher and lower spiritual forces and the eventual restoration of the Moon's light through the Jubilee cycle.</summary>
<keywords>Shekhinah, Malchut, Channels (Tzinorot), Ein Sof, Diminishment of the Moon, Sun and Moon, Jubilee (Yovel), Forty-nine Gates, Sanctification of the Moon, Shabbat vs Festivals</keywords>

--- Page 29 ---
<meta>The previous page discussed how human transgressions disrupt the divine channels, causing the "rains" of blessing to cease. It established that performing commandments repairs these channels, allowing the Infinite (Ein Sof) to bless both the upper and lower worlds. This page continues that discussion by exploring the various names and states of the Divine Attribute of Malchut (the Shekhinah) based on Israel's actions and the flow she receives from above.</meta>

In this [unity]—and one is not separated from the other—he who reads the Bible without Mishnah and Talmud is like one who has no God. *A whisperer separates the Chief* <note>Proverbs 16:28; kabbalistically interpreted here as one who causes a separation between the Divine attributes</note>. *And God spoke to Moses and said to him: I am the Lord [YHVH]* <note>Exodus 6:2</note>. And this is what is called in every place:

## Crown
The sign in the Torah is: *You shall be a crown of glory in the hand of the Lord* <note>Isaiah 62:3</note>, and *the crown with which his mother crowned him on the day of his wedding* <note>Song of Songs 3:11</note>. One who understands the law *Do not take the mother with the children* <note>Deuteronomy 22:6</note> will understand the secret of the crown with which his mother crowned him <note>In Kabbalah, the "Mother" represents the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding), which "crowns" or bestows light upon the lower Sefirah of Malchut (the Shekhinah)</note>. This is surely the secret of the Divine Unification on the day of His wedding. Sometimes she is called:

## Justice
The secret is "Lower Justice" and "Upper Justice," as it is written: *Justice, justice shall you pursue, that you may live and inherit the land* <note>Deuteronomy 16:20</note>. Because she is the fourth level from below <note>referring to the position of the Shekhinah in certain kabbalistic configurations</note> and she is the one who justifies the Omnipresent, it is written: *Righteousness [Tzedek] lodged in her, but now murderers* <note>Isaiah 1:21</note>—it is either one or the other <note>meaning the attribute fluctuates between mercy/justice and harsh judgment based on human conduct</note>. *As for me, I will behold Your face in righteousness* <note>Psalm 17:15</note>, for the "Lower Justice" is called "His back," and surely there is an "Upper Justice" called "Face." Moses did not reach the attainment of His "Face" because it is the secret of attaining the Upper Faces. This attribute is called:

## Jerusalem
For Zion and Jerusalem are the secret of the Foundation <term>Yesod: the sixth of the lower Sefirot, often associated with Zion</term> and the Kingdom <term>Malchut: the tenth and final Sefirah, associated with Jerusalem</term>. The "City of David" is Zion, and there is no sitting in the Temple courtyard except for the royalty of the House of David alone. The Scripture proclaims and says: *Blessed be the Lord out of Zion, who dwells in Jerusalem* <note>Psalm 135:21</note>, for it is the Gate of Heaven. This attribute is called:

## Eagle
Because when she is united with Israel, and they are righteous and upright lovers of the Most High, then this attribute—which is their portion—is renewed like the renewal of the moon. Concerning her it is written: *Your youth is renewed like the eagle* <note>Psalm 103:5</note>. But if, God forbid, Israel transgresses the Torah, changes the statute, or breaks the everlasting covenant, then this attribute is "plucked" and shaken off. Concerning this, the feathers fall, and she is surely called an "Eagle" <note>original: נשר (Nesher), which can mean eagle or vulture; here it refers to the shedding or "dropping" (nosherin) of feathers as a metaphor for the loss of divine light</note>. In every place, this attribute is called **Bathsheba** <note>literally "Daughter of Seven"</note>. She is the partner of David. The reason is that she draws and suckles from the *Sefirah* of Understanding <term>Binah</term> by means of the six middle *Sefirot*. The sign is: *For in six days the Lord made the heavens and the earth... and on the seventh day He rested* <note>Exodus 31:17</note>. In the Midrash *Bereishit Rabbah*, it is said: Since the Sabbath had no partner, God said to her, "The Congregation of Israel shall be your partner." This is the secret of David and Bathsheba, through which David merited the kingship. Our sages said: "Bathsheba was destined for David since the six days of Creation, but he ate her as unripened fruit" <note>Babylonian Talmud, Sanhedrin 107a; a reference to David taking Bathsheba before the divinely intended time</note>. Behold, this attribute is called:

## Land
The "Coveted Land." The reason is that when the true, perfect Unification is found on the Sabbath through the principles of "Remember" and "Observe," it follows the secret of: *Every man shall fear his mother and his father, and keep My Sabbaths* <note>Leviticus 19:3</note>. So it says: *And on the seventh day God finished* <note>Genesis 2:2</note>. The Aramaic translation of Jonathan renders this as: "And He coveted [it], the most coveted of days." *And the tree was desirable [coveted] to make one wise* <note>Genesis 3:6</note> with good intellect and grace. Sometimes she is called:

## The Land
Specifically the "**Good** Land"—meaning, when this attribute is called "Good." The one who dwells within her is called by His name "Good," as it says: *And God saw the light, that it was good* <note>Genesis 1:4</note>. When the Light—which is "Good"—is within her, she is called "Good." Also: *The Lord shall give that which is good; and our land shall yield her increase* <note>Psalm 85:12</note>. Likewise regarding Saul: *And has given it to a neighbor of yours, who is better than you* <note>1 Samuel 15:28</note>. She is called "Light," but when the Light that is "Good" is not within her, she is called "Darkness" and "Night." Sometimes this attribute is called:

## Land
The "**Broad** Land." The reason is that when she suckles from the full flow from the "Streets of the River" <note>a reference to the expansive emanations coming from the higher Sefirah of Binah</note>, she expands and grows. The sign is: *And there was an enlarging, and a winding about still upward* <note>Ezekiel 41:7</note>. This is the secret of how the worshippers in the Temple were *standing crowded together but bowing with ample space* <note>Pirkei Avot 5:5</note>. Also: *There shall yet old men and old women dwell in the streets [broad places] of Jerusalem* <note>Zechariah 8:4</note>; *Enlarge the place of your tent* <note>Isaiah 54:2</note>. Contemplate this great principle: this *Sefirah* is called in many places by the name of the attribute from which she is suckling at that moment. Understand this very well, for it is of great necessity for you in many places. Sometimes she is called:

## Land
The "**Dry** Land"—meaning, when Israel slackens their hands from the commandments and the attributes of Good depart from her, then she is found dry...

<summary>This page lists and explains the symbolic epithets of the Sefirah of Malchut (the Shekhinah), such as "Crown," "Jerusalem," and "The Good Land," showing how her state changes from "broad" and "good" to "dry" or "dark" based on the spiritual conduct of Israel.</summary>
<keywords>Malchut, Shekhinah, Sefirot, Jerusalem, Zion, Bathsheba, David, Binah, Sabbath, Divine Unification, Justice, Eagle</keywords>

--- Page 30 ---
<meta>The previous page concluded by discussing the attributes of Justice and the Shekhinah, explaining how the Divine Presence (Shekhinah) justifies the world and acts as the bridge between the upper realms and the physical land. This page deepens that connection, explaining how Israel’s reception of the Torah and the construction of the Ark served to anchor the Divine Presence on Earth, unifying the celestial emanations.</meta>

Furthermore: *And my servant David shall be king over them, and they shall all have one shepherd* <note>original: "ועבדי דוד מלך עליהם ורועה אחד יהיה לכולם"; Ezekiel 37:24</note>. Understand this very well: the reason for this is that the script which the Lord wrote for the Assembly of Israel was given to the kings of Judah to guard within the Holy of Holies. Through the people of Israel, who received the Torah and the Tablets and placed them into the Ark, the nine upper <term>Sefirot</term> <note>The ten creative attributes or emanations through which the Infinite interacts with the world</note> were bound to the tenth—the <term>Shekhinah</term> <note>The Divine Presence, the tenth and final Sefirah, representing the feminine aspect of God dwelling on Earth</note>. This is the secret of the Tablets placed within the Ark that Israel built.

Therefore, it is through the agency of Israel that all the Sefirot are unified. If they had not received the Torah here below, there would be no unification of the Sefirot above, for the primary dwelling place of the Shekhinah was meant to be among the lower beings in this world. This is what the Midrash <note>Genesis Rabbah (Bereshit Rabbah)</note> means by saying: "The essence of the Shekhinah was originally among the lower realms."

If the <term>Middle Line</term> <note>original: "קו האמצעי" (Kav Ha-Emtza’i); the central axis of the Sefirotic tree (usually the attribute of Tiferet) that balances Mercy and Judgment</note> does not extend downward to reach the place of the Shekhinah, there is no unity at all. For when the Shekhinah is not in her proper place below, there is no "True Unity."

Now, when the world was first created, the line of <term>Tiferet</term> <note>The attribute of Beauty or Harmony</note> reached down from above to pull divine abundance into the place of the Shekhinah. At that moment, Heaven and Earth were "clamped" together. This is the secret of the verse: *And the heaven and the earth were finished [Vayechulu], and all their host* <note>Genesis 2:1</note>. The word *Vayechulu* shares a root with the words for "Groom and Bride" <note>Hebrew: Chatan and Kallah</note>. The Aramaic translation renders this as "and they were perfected," meaning that the lower realms became a "Chariot" <note>a physical vehicle or throne</note> for the upper realms. They entered one into the other and were joined together; then, the Blessed Lord was truly "One" in a perfected world.

However, when the First Man <note>Adam</note> came and "corrupted the channels" <note>caused a spiritual disconnection through sin</note>, the Shekhinah withdrew, the "package" was unraveled, and the world was left in ruins. This continued until Israel arrived and received the Written Torah—which is the secret of the Middle Line—and they drew it back down. They fashioned the Ark as a physical model for the Shekhinah. Thus, through Israel, "the Lord is One and His Name One" <note>original: "יד וד אחד ושמו אחד"; a coded reference to the Tetragrammaton YHVH</note>. As it is written: *Who is like Your people Israel, one nation on earth?* <note>2 Samuel 7:23</note>. This is also the secret of the plea: *What will You do for Your great Name?* <note>Joshua 7:9</note>. As it were, through Israel, the Lord is made "One."

The secret lies in the two Tablets placed in the Ark. One who understands this secret will understand the immense power inherent in receiving the Torah. This is the secret of: *From heaven You made judgment heard; the earth feared and was still* <note>Psalm 76:9</note>, and *The heaven is My throne, and the earth is My footstool* <note>Isaiah 66:1</note>, and *I will honor the place of My feet* <note>Isaiah 60:13</note>.

One who understands these great principles we have hinted at will understand the reason why *Your Name is called upon Your city and upon Your people* <note>Daniel 9:19</note>, and the secret of: *You shall call me "My Husband" [Ishi] and no longer call me "My Master" [Ba'ali]* <note>Hosea 2:18</note>. And it is said, and the secret is: *And I will betroth you to Me in righteousness [Tzedek]* <note>Hosea 2:21</note>, and the secret of: *For the Lord your God is in your midst* <note>Deuteronomy 7:21</note>. The secret of *I will not come in your midst* <note>Exodus 33:3</note> means "literally within you." And the secret of *The Holy One in your midst; I will not enter the city* <note>Hosea 11:9</note> refers to what the Sages said: "I will not enter the Upper Jerusalem until I enter the Lower Jerusalem" <note>A famous Talmudic teaching (Taanit 5a) suggesting that God's presence in the heavenly realm is dependent on His presence in the earthly realm of Israel</note>.

One who understands this will understand the secret of the "Hiding of the Ark" <note>The *Genizah*, referring to the tradition that the Ark was hidden away before the destruction of the Temple</note> and the secret of: *I will lay your sanctuaries waste* <note>Leviticus 26:31</note>. Regarding this, the Sages of blessed memory said: "Even though they are desolate, they retain their holiness." This means that the Ark and the Covenant—the Ark of all the earth—and the Tablets are hidden there. This is the secret of the "Lord of all the Covenant, Lord of all the earth." All of this is hinted at and explained to anyone whom the Blessed Lord has granted knowledge. This attribute is sometimes called:

# Elohim
<note>Hebrew: אלהים; God, specifically in the aspect of Judgment</note>

I am now hinting at the eighth attribute <note>Counting upward from Malchut, the eighth is often associated with Binah/Understanding or, in other counts, Gevurah/Judgment</note>, written as "the Lord" <note>original: "יד וד"</note> but read as *Elohim*. This is the secret of the "Might of the Lord" <note>Gevurah of YHVH</note>, which pulls judgments downward. From it is emanated the attribute of <term>Gevurah</term> <note>Might, Strength, or Judgment</note>, which is called *Elohim* in every instance.

One who understands the secret of *Elohim* will understand the secret of: *In the beginning [Bereshit] God [Elohim] created* <note>Genesis 1:1</note>. To which "beginning" does this refer? It is said in this matter: *The Lord of Hosts [Elohim Tzevaot]* <note>Psalm 80:8</note>. From the attribute of Gevurah, the attribute of Judgment is emanated toward <term>Hod</term> <note>Splendor/Majesty, the eighth Sefirah</note>, and this is why it is called *Elohim Tzevaot*.

When all the upper Sefirot are perfected from the side of <term>Chesed</term> <note>Loving-kindness</note> and pour their influence into the tenth Sefirah, it is called "The Lord" [YHVH]. But when the attributes of Judgment and Might are perfected and pour into it, it is also called *Elohim*. This is the meaning of: *For My Name is in him* <note>Exodus 23:21</note>. These matters are even deeper; from them, you may enter the secret of the Torah saying, *In the beginning God [Elohim] created*. For the word *Bereshit* [In the beginning] includes all types of "beginnings," each according to its subject. The word *Elohim* also includes every mention of the name *Elohim* in the Torah, each according to its context.

When the Torah says *In the beginning God [Elohim] created*, it hints at the expansion of <term>Binah</term> <note>Understanding</note> from <term>Chokhmah</term> <note>Wisdom</note>, because that is where the name *Elohim* begins in the "World of Judgment." Behold, Binah is the "beginning" of the seven lower Sefirot, and it hints at the creation of the world.

<summary>This page explains that the unification of God’s attributes (Sefirot) depends on the spiritual actions of Israel and the presence of the Torah and Ark on Earth. It identifies the name "Elohim" with the attributes of Binah and Gevurah, representing the flow of Divine Judgment into the created world.</summary>

<keywords>Shekhinah, Sefirot, Ark of the Covenant, Tablets of the Law, Tiferet, Binah, Gevurah, Elohim, Unification, Creation, Bereshit, Jerusalem</keywords>

--- Page 31 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the unification of the Sefirot through Israel’s actions. It specifically explores the tenth Sefirah (the Shekhinah) under the names "Acharit" (the End) and "The Ark of the Covenant," emphasizing how the Divine Presence remains with Israel even in exile and sin.</meta>

How could this temperament and attribute be lacking? 
At times it is called:
### The End
The reason is that it is the "end" <note>original: "Acharit" (אחרית), meaning the final part or the future</note> of the <term>Sefirot</term> <note>The ten divine emanations</note>. The sign and proof of this is the verse, *And you shall see my back* <note>Exodus 33:23; the Hebrew word for "back" (achorai) shares a root with Acharit</note>. One who understands even a little of this will understand the verse: *And it shall come to pass in the end of days, that the mountain of the Lord's house shall be established at the top of the mountains* <note>Isaiah 2:2</note>. Understand this with great insight: at the end it is written, *For out of Zion shall go forth the Torah* <note>Isaiah 2:3</note>, which is the essence of the redemption and the hope of Israel.

Because the <term>Shekhinah</term> <note>The Divine Presence, viewed as the feminine aspect of God</note> is always with Israel, both in the Land and outside the Land, as it is written: *Who dwells with them in the midst of their impurity* <note>Leviticus 16:16</note>. This is what Rabbi Shimon bar Yochai said: "Beloved are Israel, for in every place they were exiled, the Shekhinah went into exile with them. They were exiled to Egypt, the Shekhinah was with them; they were exiled to Babylon, the Shekhinah was with them."

One who understands the secret of the exile in Egypt will understand the secret of the verse: *And I will pass through the land of Egypt* <note>Exodus 12:12</note>; it did not say "And I will descend." *A king is held captive in the tresses* <note>Song of Songs 7:6; interpreted here as God being "bound" to His people’s fate</note>. And at Mount Sinai, it says to Moses: *And the Lord descended in the cloud, and stood with him there, and proclaimed* <note>Exodus 34:5</note>, and afterward: *And the Lord passed by before him, and proclaimed* <note>Exodus 34:6</note>.

Since the Shekhinah is with Israel, if they are righteous, she is like *a joyful mother of children* <note>Psalm 113:9</note>, and it is written: *Let your father and your mother be glad, and let her that bore you rejoice* <note>Proverbs 23:25</note>. But if, Heaven forbid, Israel sins, it is a *grief to his father* and *a foolish son is the heaviness of his mother* <note>Proverbs 10:1</note>. It is also written: *A wise son makes a glad father, but a foolish son etc.*

The Sages alluded to this with a parable of a child who soils his clothes. The King says: "Let his nursemaid come and wipe away the filth." The secret of this is: *And for your transgressions was your mother sent away* <note>Isaiah 50:1</note>. Because the Assembly of Israel <note>Knesset Yisrael, another name for the Shekhinah</note> suffers the "filth" caused by Israel's actions, what is written in the Torah? *In your distress, when all these things have come upon you in the end of days*—and therefore—*you shall return to the Lord your God* <note>Deuteronomy 4:30</note>.

### Behold, I
am opening for you the "Gates of Light" <note>original: "Sha'are Orah" (שערי אורה); likely a reference to the famous Kabbalistic work by Rabbi Joseph Gikatilla</note>. 
Know that because this attribute is called "The End," we are destined to return to Zion by way of repentance. I am now hinting at the attribute called "The End" <note>Acharit</note>, which is attached to the <term>Middle Line</term> <note>The central axis of the Sefirotic tree, balancing Mercy and Judgment</note> by

<column-break/>

way of the "hope of the scarlet thread" <note>Joshua 2:18; the word "hope" (Tikvah) also means a cord or line</note>. The Scripture cries out and says: *And there is hope for your future [Acharit], says the Lord, and the children shall return to their own border* <note>Jeremiah 31:16</note>. By virtue of the "Hope" <note>Tikvah/Cord</note> that is tied to the "End" <note>Acharit</note>, it is tied to Israel. Therefore, *the children shall return to their own border*. Every instance of "Hope" (*Tikvah*) is a term for a "Line" (*Kav*). 

Similarly, they alluded to this through the "Inner Foundation" in the building of Ezekiel: *and a line of thirty cubits* <note>1 Kings 7:23</note>, where the word for "line" is written *Kaveh* <note>The written text (Kethiv) is קוה (Kaveh), while the reading (Qere) is קו (Kav/Line)</note>. And it is written in the later prophecies: *and a line shall be stretched forth over Jerusalem* <note>Zechariah 1:16</note>. One who understands these great principles will understand the secret of "Hope" in every place. Through this Hope, we shall return to the Middle Line, as in: *and a line shall be stretched forth over Jerusalem*.

This attribute is also sometimes called:
### The Ark of the Covenant
One who knows this secret knows the secret of the Tablets that were placed in the Ark. This is the secret of: *Behold, the Ark of the Covenant, the Lord of all the earth* <note>Joshua 3:11</note>. This [attribute] is called *Adonai* <note>Lord</note>, while the [Sefirah of] *Yesod* (Foundation) is surely called "Lord of all the earth." When the Tablets are in the Ark, then the Shekhinah dwells within Israel as an eternal inheritance. Wherever they go, they are the inheritance of the Omnipresent, and they can never be separated from Him. There is no "bill of divorce" there, which is the secret of: *Where is the bill of your mother's divorcement, whom I have sent away?* <note>Isaiah 50:1</note>.

Now, it is written afterward: *And I saw, when for all the causes whereby backsliding Israel committed adultery I had sent her away, and given her a bill of divorce...* <note>Jeremiah 3:8</note>. Heaven forbid! This refers to the Kingdom of Jeroboam <note>The Northern Kingdom of Israel</note> which has no return, and similarly the Kingdom of Samaria. But Judah, which is the secret of the Assembly of Israel, where the Shekhinah dwells in Jerusalem and Judah through David—regarding her, the verse says: *Yet her treacherous sister Judah feared not* <note>Jeremiah 3:8</note>. 

And this is the secret of *Where is the bill of your mother's divorcement, etc.* Keep this very great principle well. Through Judah, Samaria shall return, but she shall not return to being a [separate] kingdom, for God gave her a bill of divorce regarding the monarchy. This is the secret of: *And I will make them one nation in the land upon the mountains of Israel; and one king shall be king to them all; and they shall be no more two nations, neither shall they be divided into two kingdoms any more* <note>Ezekiel 37:22</note>.

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic significance of the Shekhinah as the "End" (Acharit) and the "Ark of the Covenant," emphasizing her enduring bond with Israel despite sin and exile. It interprets the biblical "bill of divorce" as applying only to the Northern Kingdom's sovereignty, promising a future unification under the House of David.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the divine attributes of the tenth Sefirah, the Shekhinah. Having previously discussed her as "The End," the text now shifts to her manifestation as "Shaddai" (The Almighty) and her role as the "lens" through which prophets perceive the Divine.</meta>

*And my breasts* <note>original: "Ve-Shadai" (ושדי); quoting Song of Songs 8:10. The author uses a wordplay between "breasts" and the Divine Name "Shaddai"</note> *are like towers; then was I in his eyes as one that found peace.* It is a strong cord, like the verse that follows. When Israel defiled the Sanctuary and the Temple, bringing forces of impurity into it, then through the attribute called **Shaddai**, they brought into that same place the forces of impurity known as **demons** <note>original: "shedim" (שדים); the author links the name Shaddai to the word for demons to show how holiness can be displaced by impurity</note>. As it is written: *Destruction* <note>original: "shod" (שוד)</note> *that wastes at noonday* <note>Psalm 91:6</note>, and *the tents of robbers* <note>original: "shodedim" (שודדים)</note> *prosper* <note>Job 12:6</note>. 

Woe to that hour! For then the Sanctuary was defiled. *The kings of the earth did not believe, nor any of the inhabitants of the world, that the foe and the enemy should enter the gates of Jerusalem* <note>Lamentations 4:12</note>. For *there shall no more come into you the uncircumcised and the unclean* <note>Isaiah 52:1</note>, and *I will also cause the idols and the unclean spirit to pass out of the land* <note>Zechariah 13:2</note>. 

Observe that the spirit of impurity and the demons are called "spirits," as is mentioned in the tractate <term>Chagigah</term> <note>A volume of the Talmud dealing with Temple offerings and mystical visions</note> and in several other places. The secret is this: *Is not a day of destruction for David coming as destruction from the Almighty?* <note>original: "ke-shod mi-Shaddai" (כשוד משדי); Isaiah 13:6. This is another wordplay on Shaddai/Destruction</note>. The conclusion of the matter is that when the flow of nourishment from the hand of **Shaddai** is severed, woe! She is "destroyed." 

Therefore, she is called the **Unpolished Lens** <note>original: "Aspaklaria she-eina me'ira" (אספקלריא שאינה מאירה); literally "a mirror that does not shine," referring to a dimmed or indirect spiritual vision</note>. She does not shine of her own accord, but only from the abundance that comes to her through the <term>conduits</term> <note>original: "tzinnorot" (צנורות); the spiritual channels that connect the Sefirot</note>. Note that when she is filled, she is called **Shaddai**, for then there is "enough" <note>original: "dai" (די)</note> for all the world. But when the conduits are cut off, then "destruction" <note>shod</note> is found. 

As we said, she is called the lens that does not shine on its own, but only by the letter *Yod* when she is called **Shaddai**. Know that all the prophets did not actually enter into her; they only reached the "gatehouse." She was the **curtain** <note>original: "pargod" (פרגוד)</note> up to which the prophets reached, and from behind it, they saw what they saw. They heard the voice of *The Lord of Hosts* <note>original: "Y-H-W-H Tzeva'ot"</note> in a vision of prophecy. Therefore, things were not entirely clear to them, because she was a partition standing before them. This is the secret of *the mirrors of the serving women who crowded* <note>original: "tzov'ot" (צובאו)</note> *at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting* <note>Exodus 38:8</note>. Regarding this, it is said: *Hear now my words: If there be a prophet among you, I the Lord will make myself known unto him in a vision, and will speak unto him in a dream* <note>Numbers 12:6</note>. 

But Moses, our teacher—may he rest in peace—entered within. He attained the vision through the **Polished Lens** <note>original: "Aspaklaria ha-me'ira"; a clear or "shining" mirror, representing direct, unclouded prophecy</note>. He went inside, beyond the lens that does not shine. What is written regarding Abraham? *And the Lord appeared to him by the oaks of Mamre... and he sat at the tent door in the heat of the day* <note>Genesis 18:1</note>. But regarding Moses, it is written: *And He called to Moses, and the Lord spoke to him out of the Tent of Meeting, saying...* <note>Leviticus 1:1</note>. Therefore, the prophecy of Moses is distinct from all other prophets; for all other prophets saw from the *entrance* of the tent, but Moses saw from *inside* the tent.

---

And this is what the verse means: *Hear now my words: If there be a prophet among you, I the Lord will make myself known unto him in a vision... My servant Moses is not so, who is faithful in all my house* <note>Numbers 12:6-7</note>. You already know that the "house" of the Sefirah <term>Tiferet</term> <note>The central Sefirah, "Beauty," often associated with the written Torah and Moses</note> is **Shaddai**, and that is the Tent of Meeting. Therefore, the Scripture said to Moses: *And I appeared to Abraham, to Isaac, and to Jacob, as El Shaddai, but by my name Y-H-W-H was I not known to them* <note>Exodus 6:3</note>. This means: they saw my name *Y-H-W-H* through the lens of *El Shaddai*, which is the unpolished lens. Therefore, I was not known to them with ultimate clarity, for they did not attain my true essence. 

All this is from behind the "curtain" and the secret of "the pillars with their sockets." One who understands this secret of what we have explained regarding the attribute of **Shaddai** will understand the secret of: *Can you find out the deep things of God? Can you find out the Almighty* <note>Shaddai</note> *unto perfection?* <note>Job 11:7</note>. Regarding the verse *And I appeared to Abraham... but my name Y-H-W-H etc.*, open your eyes and you will see wonders if you look within it.

This Torah is called:
### The Bed
The reason is: *Behold the bed of Solomon; sixty valiant men are round about it, of the valiant of Israel* <note>Song of Songs 3:7</note>. This is because all types of accusers and destroyers in the world surround it, and there are many types of destruction and punishment around it. This is the secret of: *As the lily among thorns* <note>Song of Songs 2:2</note>. This is **Jerusalem** in the midst of the nations, her inhabitants in the land and the streets. It takes hold of the "middle point"—that is, from the **Tree of Life** in the midst of the Garden. Its upper part is caught among the thorns and torn. 

But when Israel is worthy and perfect, and they perform the "peace offerings" properly, then the verse is fulfilled: *And I will give peace in the land* <note>Leviticus 26:6</note>. Then the **bed of Solomon** was truly "whole" <note>original: "sheleimah" (שלימה); a pun on Solomon's name, Shlomo</note>. 

**And Know** that this "bed" is the inheritance of our father Jacob, entirely and above all, because he was the perfect firstborn, as it is said: *Israel is my son, my firstborn* <note>Exodus 4:22</note>. Therefore, Jacob inherited this "bed." Abraham did not inherit it because of the flaw of Ishmael, and Isaac did not inherit it because of the flaw of Esau. For the "thorns and briers" <note>Ishmael and Esau</note> surround the bed along with all other external forces, while **Shaddai** is in the center. Jacob inherited this bed, as it is said: *Israel is my son, my firstborn*. Therefore, this bed returned to him as it was at the beginning of the world's creation. This is why *Israel bowed himself upon the bed's head* <note>Genesis 47:31</note>. What is the reason? As it is written: *And his soul was whole...* <note>The author concludes by linking Jacob's "wholeness" to the restoration of the Divine Bed/Shekhinah</note>.

It contrasts the direct prophecy of Moses with the indirect visions of other prophets and the Patriarchs, while also describing the Shekhinah as "Solomon's Bed" which must be protected from external "thorns."</summary>

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the divine attributes of the tenth Sefirah, the Shekhinah. Having previously discussed her as "The End," the text now shifts to her manifestation as "Shaddai" (The Almighty) and her role as the "lens" through which prophets perceive the Divine.</meta>

## [Right Column]

The yoke of the angels and all other worlds, and the Sovereignty <note>original: "Malchut" (מלכות); the tenth and final divine attribute in the Sefirotic tree</note> which is called "Beginning" <note>original: "Reshit" (ראשית)</note> and is called "God" <note>original: "Elohim" (אלהים)</note>. When you understand this, you will be able to understand all that our Sages of blessed memory <note>original: "drzl" (דרז"ל)</note> interpreted regarding the word "In the beginning" <note>original: "Bereshit" (בראשית); the first word of the Bible</note>: that there is no "Beginning" but Wisdom <note>original: "Chokhmah" (חכמה)</note>; there is no "Beginning" but the Torah; there is no "Beginning" but Israel; and all the other things that are called "Beginning."

You will also understand what was said: that for the sake of Moses the world was created, as it is said, *And he saw a beginning for himself, for there the portion of the lawgiver was hidden* <note>Deuteronomy 33:21</note>. Everything is stored away in the treasuries of the Torah, and everything is clear to those who have eyes to see. For through the revealed things, those with insight may enter into the hidden secrets. And the sign <note>original: "siman" (וסי'); a mnemonic or biblical proof-text</note> is: *Apples of gold in settings of silver* <note>Proverbs 25:11</note>.

Reflect on this: when the attribute of Sovereignty is called "God" <note>Elohim</note>, it is at the time when the attribute of Sovereignty is filled from the Power <note>original: "Gevurah" (גבורה); the attribute of strict judgment</note>. Ultimately, in every instance, she is named after the thing by which she is filled. The sign for this is the *Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil*. 

You must guard this great principle: in every place in the Torah where this attribute is named after the thing she receives from, it is because *My Name is within him* <note>Exodus 23:21</note>. Therefore, when Israel is pure and righteous, they fill her with the "one thousand lights" <note>original: "elef kallim" (אלף קלי')</note> that are upon her, all of which are the channels for the <term>613 commandments</term> <note>The total number of laws in the Torah</note>. But after this, if Israel turns from the straight path, sins, and defiles the world, then she becomes filled with those "external things" which are the forms of transgression. At that time, she is called *a sword full of blood* <note>Isaiah 34:6</note>, and it is written: *I was full of justice; righteousness lodged in her, but now murderers* <note>Isaiah 1:21</note>. 

The sign <note>siman</note> is that a thing is never empty; it is either *full of justice* or *murderers*. This is what our Sages of blessed memory said: *From the day the Holy Temple was destroyed, even though the four death penalties were abolished, their judgments were not abolished.* This is the reason for either "righteousness" or "murderers." Guard this principle well, for from it you will be able to grasp how much power is held by the one who fulfills the commandments, purifies the world, and gains merit for himself and the public by guiding them in the straight path. Conversely, consider those who commit transgressions, defile the world, sin, and cause others to sin—to what extent does their power reach to damage this [divine] attribute and cause a defect in the <term>Assembly of Israel</term> <note>original: "Knesset Yisrael"; a mystical term for the Shekhinah/Sovereignty</note>? This is the meaning of: *A foolish son is a grief to his father and bitterness to her who bore him* <note>Proverbs 17:25</note>, and *A wise son makes a glad father, but a foolish son is the grief of his mother* <note>Proverbs 10:1</note>, and *Because of your transgressions was your mother sent away* <note>Isaiah 50:1</note>. The secret is:

<column-break/>

## [Left Column]

*My son, if your heart be wise, my heart will be glad, even mine* <note>Proverbs 23:15</note>. The secret of Israel is a throne for the "Mother of the King" <note>referring to the Sefirah of Understanding/Binah or the Shekhinah in her motherly aspect</note>. Guard this principle, as it is written: *A joyful mother of children* <note>Psalm 113:9</note>, and *Let your father and your mother be glad, and let her who bore you rejoice* <note>Proverbs 23:25</note>.

Now that we have explained these great principles to you, know that this attribute is called **Shaddai** <note>שדי; traditionally translated as "Almighty"</note>. The reason is that from her, the abundance flows to all existing things in the world. She is the sustainer of all creatures, and from her every creature receives strength, existence, stability, life, and livelihood. This is because she is the gate to all the <term>Sefirot</term> <note>The ten emanations of God</note>, and there is no way for anything in the world to receive anything from the other Sefirot except through her. 

Regarding the verse: *And a river went out of Eden to water the garden* <note>Genesis 2:10</note>—up until "the Garden" is the place of abundance, emanation, and nurturing; from there and below is the "World of Separation." Thus, from Sovereignty <note>Malchut</note> downward, all creatures branch out according to their kinds. Each one receives abundance and strength according to its type. From the attribute of Sovereignty, each one receives joy, existence, and life, each "according to his eating" <note>Exodus 16:21</note> and according to its kind. 

Therefore, this attribute is called **Shaddai**, because in her there is **sufficiency** <note>original: "dai" (די); the author links the Name "Sha-ddai" to the phrase "She-dai" (That which is enough)</note> of livelihood, life, existence, and stability for all beings in the world, and nothing is lacking in her; everyone nurses from her. This is what was said in the tractate <term>Chagigah</term> <note>A volume of the Talmud</note> in the name of Him who said "Enough!" <note>original: "Dai"</note> to the world. That is to say, in this attribute there is sufficiency, repair, stability, and existence for all beings, each according to its matters. 

This is the secret of how the prophets prophesy through "rectification" <note>original: "tikun" (תיקון)</note>. When Israel does the will of the Omnipresent, then all the channels come to her from above in a perfect filling, and the blessings flow into Israel first. From there onward, the blessings are drawn to all other creatures and beings in the world, each according to its kind. The analogy is like a breast <note>original: "shad" (שד); another wordplay on the name Shaddai</note> from which an infant nurses. 

But after this, if they sin and provoke anger, then the channels are cut off from above, and no blessing is found among the creatures. The secret of this is: *We have a little sister, and she has no breasts; what shall we do for our sister in the day when she shall be spoken for? If she be a wall, we will build upon her a turret of silver* from the Upper King; *but if she be a door*—meaning she is impoverished <note>original: "dalah" (דלה); a pun on "delet" (door)</note>—*we will enclose her with boards of cedar* <note>Song of Songs 8:8-9</note>, and not even a cedar tree. *I am a wall, and my breasts are like towers* <note>Song of Songs 8:10</note>—I am strong in the commandments. 

And the sign <note>siman</note> is:

<summary>This page explains the Sefirah of Malchut (Sovereignty) as the "Beginning" and the divine name "Shaddai," illustrating how human conduct determines whether this divine channel flows with holy abundance (like a nursing breast) or becomes a vessel of harsh judgment.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the tenth Sefirah, the attribute of Sovereignty (Malchut). The previous page ended by explaining how this attribute is named based on the divine energy filling it; here, the text explores its manifestation as "The End" (Suf) and "The Storm" (Sufah), linking the Exodus from Egypt to the final redemption from Edom.</meta>

### Page 9

Egypt is to the Land of Israel like a garden of the letter <term>Yod</term> <note>The letter Yod represents the initial point of creation or the attribute of Wisdom; here it suggests Egypt was a perversion or a "shell" surrounding a holy spark</note> in the land of Egypt. When this attribute finds the wicked, it consumes them.
Therefore, the Blessed Name gave signs and wonders from the world, and the secret is because of its **End** <note>original: "sofah" (סופה); a play on the word "storm" (sufah) and "her end" (sofah)</note>. There is no wickedness
in that great Prince of Egypt <note>The spiritual guardian or "angel" of the nation</note>, so that people should not say: "The righteous one is the foundation of the world, for behold, the Lord comes in fire."
There is a great Prince on high whom the Blessed Name [overcomes], and "in the **storm** <note>original: "sufah"</note> are His chariots, to return His anger in fury."
Therefore, "Strike the capital of the pillar so the thresholds shake" <note>Amos 9:1</note> and His rebuke is with flames of fire. Therefore, it was found during the Exodus:
The Torah says, "So that you may remember the day of your departure from the land of Egypt all the days of your life," in order
that you should be confident that there is no prince or ruler among all the nations whom the Holy One, blessed be He, cannot overcome.
As it is written: "I will execute judgments against all the gods of Egypt."
Therefore, the Blessed Name Himself executed judgments against all the gods of Egypt, for there is no prince or ruler who could
redeem them except the Name alone. Therefore: "I will pass through the land of Egypt—I, and not an angel; and against all the gods of Egypt
I will execute judgments—I, and not a messenger." Therefore, the striking of the firstborn and the striking of the zodiac sign of **Aries** <note>original: "mazal taleh" (מזל טלה); the ram, which was the Egyptian deity</note>, which is
the head of the twelve signs, and the striking in the month of **Nisan**, which is
the head of the twelve months. It is written: "Observe the month of **Abib**" <note>The spring month</note>. The meaning of Abib is "Father" (*Av*) of twelve (*yb*). And behold, Egypt—which is the "Narrowness" <note>original: "Meitzar" (מיצר); a pun on "Mitzrayim" (Egypt)</note>
adjacent to the Land of Israel—pursued Israel until they reached the **Sea of Reeds** <note>original: "Yam Suf" (ים סוף)</note>. When they reached that
place, that attribute called the "Sea of Reeds" doubled itself, for it is the secret of the Sea called the **End** <note>original: "Suf" (סוף)</note> of the levels in the
Ten Sefirot. The secret is: "And I will set your border from the Sea of Reeds <note>the End</note> to the Sea of the Philistines." Open your eyes and see! Therefore,
this attribute is called **Storm** <note>original: "Sufah"</note>, as it is written: "The Lord is in the **Storm** and in the **Whirlwind** <note>original: "Se'arah" (סערה)</note> is His path." This is a great secret alluded to: "Storm" (*Sufah*) is
the **End** (*Sof*) of the Ten Sefirot. The sign is: "End" (*Sof*) and adjacent to it
is "Whirlwind" (*Se'arah*), the place that separates the boundaries. The sign is: "and around Him it is very **stormy**" <note>Psalm 50:3</note>. "And the Lord answered Job from the **whirlwind**."
"He who crushes me with a **whirlwind**." "And he walked in the **whirlwinds** of the south."
Therefore: "In the storm and in the whirlwind is His path." This is what our Sages of blessed memory meant when they said:
"The Holy One is as meticulous with the righteous as a **hair's breadth**" <note>original: "khut ha-sa'arah" (חוט השערה); a pun on "storm" (se'arah) and "hair" (sa'ar)</note>.
For surrounding the righteous is the secret of the **hair**.
The sign is: "His hand took hold of Esau’s heel" <note>Esau is associated with "hair" (Se'ir)</note>, and he is the "hairy crown."
This is the place where the boundaries separate. "They shall no longer offer their sacrifices to the **goat-demons**" <note>original: "se'irim" (שעירים); related to hair and the region of Seir</note>. For when the attribute of
**Storm** passes, it examines every place.
It brings forth the "hairy crown that walks in its guilt." As long as

<vocab>Egypt, Sea of Reeds, Storm, Whirlwind, Seir, Edom, Amalek, Redemption, Righteous, Hair's breadth, Hairy crown</vocab>

Egypt pursued Israel until the Sea, and on the day of the Sea, the miracle of miracles was performed for them. There, retribution was exacted from the Egyptians,
and the miracle was done for Israel: "Your right hand, O Lord, is glorious in power; Your right hand, O Lord, shatters the enemy."
It sinks them and saves you. The reason it is called **Sufah** (Storm/End) is because it has a front and a back
in equal measure: from the inside, mercy; from the outside,
retribution. So it is destined to be with **Edom** <note>Traditionally identified with Rome or the later enemies of Israel</note> at the time of
Redemption, which will come to consume the "Seir" <note>The hairy one/goat</note> around it—
which is Edom, which is **Seir**. From that point onward,
"the hairy crown" above shall be revealed, etc., and nothing shall remain
of their power below. "The Lord will punish the host of heaven on high, and the kings of the earth on the earth."
"Who is this coming from Edom..." etc. Then it is written: "Enlarge the site of your tent," and the Lord shall be revealed with a "shaved razor."
It is written: "But God will crush the head of His enemies, the hairy crown of him who walks in his guilt." When no
hair remains in Seir, then: "Saviors shall ascend Mount
Zion to judge Mount Esau, and the kingdom shall be the Lord's." Then "The Lord shall be King over all the earth."
This is "The war of the Lord against **Amalek**." "Who is this coming from Edom?"
Just as there was the war of Egypt, which was a "narrowing"
to the Land of Israel, so too there will be the war of
Seir, the "narrowing" to the Land of Israel. It is written: "The southern side shall be for you from the Wilderness of Zin to Edom." This is
"His hand taking hold of Esau's heel." Therefore, "As in the days of your departure
from the land of Egypt, I will show him wonders." Then he shall reveal
the hairy crown, and his mischief shall return upon his own head, and his violence shall descend upon his own scalp. Therefore, it says regarding the final redemption:
"For behold, the Lord comes in fire, and in the **storm** His chariots, to return His anger in fury and His rebuke in flames of fire."
And next to it: "For by fire the Lord will judge, and by His sword,
all flesh." This is: "For My sword is sated in heaven, behold [it descends upon Edom]."

<summary>This page uses linguistic puns to connect the "End" of the divine emanations (the Sefirah of Malchut) with the "Storm" (Sufah) of judgment. It explains that the same divine power that crushed Egypt at the "Sea of Reeds" (Yam Suf) will ultimately destroy Edom and Amalek in the final redemption.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the tenth Sefirah (Malchut), here personified through the figure of Jacob and the concept of "wholeness." It expands on the previous page's discussion of the "End" by linking it to the death of the righteous and the crossing of the Sea of Reeds.</meta>

It is written: "And Jacob was a whole man, dwelling in tents" <note>Genesis 25:27; the Hebrew term "Tam" (תם) is translated here as "whole" or "complete"</note> and we translate this into Aramaic as "a complete man." Therefore, "Behold the bed of Solomon" <note>Song of Songs 3:7; in Kabbalah, "Solomon’s bed" represents the Divine Presence or the perfected lineage</note>. And if you should say that regarding Abraham it is written: "And be whole" <note>Genesis 17:1</note>, which is also translated as "be complete," it is written regarding him in the context of "walking" <note>original: "hahalika"</note> and not in the context of "sitting." He did not sit upon the throne of <term>The Lord</term> <note>original: "Yad Yad," a scribal shorthand for the Tetragrammaton, the four-letter Name of God</note>. 

But regarding Jacob, it is written "a whole man, *dwelling* in tents," referring to "sitting," for Jacob sat upon the throne of <term>The Lord</term>. The sign for this is: "Israel, in whom I will be glorified" <note>Isaiah 49:3</note> with the "glory of a man to dwell in a house" <note>Isaiah 44:13</note>. Therefore, it is written of him: "a whole man, dwelling in tents"—actual tents. This is the meaning of "This is the Torah: when a man dies in a tent" <note>Numbers 19:14</note>. 

Therefore, he said of Abraham that his "bed" was not complete <note>Meaning his lineage included those who went astray</note>, and likewise regarding Isaac, of blessed memory. But Jacob, who said: "Heaven forbid there should be any flaw in my bed as there was with Abraham, from whom came Ishmael, or with Isaac, from whom came Esau," they all answered and said: "Hear, O Israel, <term>The Lord</term> our God, <term>The Lord</term> is One." 

Thus, his "bed" was found to be complete. Therefore, at the time of his passing, he attached himself to it entirely, and this is the meaning of: "He gathered his feet into the bed, and he expired, and was gathered to his people" <note>Genesis 49:33</note>. In every instance regarding the righteous, "gathering" <note>original: "asifa"</note> refers to the place of the "bed," which is the place of purity. 

Therefore, He said to Moses: "Be gathered to your people" <note>Numbers 27:13</note>, and it is written of the sons of Jacob: "Gather yourselves that I may tell you" <note>Genesis 49:1</note>, and "All Israel were gathered to the city as one man, comrades" <note>Judges 20:11</note>. This refers to the purification of all Israel during the Pilgrimage festivals. 

Therefore, wherever you find "gathering" in the Torah regarding the righteous, it refers to the matter of their gathering into the "bed," which is the place of purity. For outside of this "bed," all places are side-places of impurity. Therefore: "If there be among you any man who is not clean by reason of a nocturnal accident, then he shall go outside the camp; he shall not come inside the camp" <note>Deuteronomy 23:11</note>. And it is written: "That they send out of the camp every leper and every one that has a discharge" <note>Numbers 5:2</note>. And regarding Miriam, it is written: "Let her be shut out from the camp seven days, and after that she shall be gathered in" <note>Numbers 12:14</note>. 

Open your eyes and see wonders! Regarding Naaman, it is written: "That I may gather him from his leprosy" <note>2 Kings 5:3</note>. The great secret is that "the righteous is gathered from before the evil" <note>Isaiah 57:1</note>; "Gather My pious ones to Me" <note>Psalms 50:5</note>; and "Men of kindness are gathered" <note>Isaiah 57:1</note>. The wise understand this secret.

<column-break/>

This is the secret of "And he gathered his feet into the bed," and it continues. Therefore, every man must be careful at the time of his death that none of his limbs remain outside of this "bed." For concerning anyone of whom a part remains outside this "bed," it is said: "He shall not be gathered, nor shall he be buried; he shall be for the beasts of the field" <note>Jeremiah 8:2</note> and "I have given them as food for the birds of the heaven" <note>Jeremiah 7:33</note>. This is because surrounding this "bed" are all kinds of destroyers and agents of retribution. 

Any of a person’s limbs that remain outside this "bed" die in great pain. This is the secret of what we said: "Behold the bed of Solomon, sixty heroes surround it, of the heroes of Israel; they all hold swords, expert in war; every man has his sword upon his thigh because of the fear in the nights" <note>Song of Songs 3:7-8</note>. This means the fear of Hell <note>original: "Gehinnom"</note>, which is compared to night. 

Therefore, it is written regarding Jacob: "And he gathered his feet into the bed." For this reason, every man must prepare himself for "gathering," for without this, he would be in great pain. These preparations are great principles I have opened for you in this place, and from there you will see many wondrous secrets. As we have said: "There is nothing between the Garden of Eden and Hell but a hair's breadth," and the sign for this is: "and it shall be very tempestuous [round about Him]" <note>Psalms 50:3; a play on the word "tempestuous" (nish'arah) and "hair" (se'arah)</note>. 

Therefore, a man must not remain outside, but must be "gathered to his people." For this reason, He prepared all types of "gathering" mentioned in this context regarding the righteous.

### **This Attribute is Called the Sea of Reeds**
<note>original: "Yam Suf"; also translates as "The Sea of the End"</note>

Therefore, it was split to receive its children and to save them from the hand of its enemies. Did I not tell you that surrounding the "bed" are thorns, briers, and thistles? Behold, the land of Egypt is the "second" land <meta>catchword: "not"</meta> and therefore it is called *Mitzrayim* <note>Egypt</note>, meaning the language of "straits" <note>original: "meizarim," meaning narrow straits or boundaries</note> adjacent to the inheritance. For the "strait" acts as a divider between borders. 

Behold, the scripture proclaims: "And Hebron was built seven years [before Zoan in Egypt]" <note>Numbers 13:22</note>, meaning that they are joined together. "Built seven years" refers to the "Daughter of Seven" <note>Bat-Sheva, a reference to the Sefirah of Malchut</note>, and Zoan in Egypt is adjacent to her. This is why they said that the land of Egypt is second to the Land of Israel, for the Prince of Egypt is second in the Chariot. "And he made him ride in the second chariot which he had" <note>Genesis 41:43</note>. Therefore, the Blessed Name had to perform signs and wonders in the land of Egypt, because its Prince is supreme over all the princes—second only <meta>catchword: "Egypt"</meta>

<summary>This page discusses the spiritual perfection of Jacob ("the whole man") and explains that his "bed" represents a state of purity that protects the soul from external "destroyers" at the moment of death. It further links the attribute of Malchut to the "Sea of Reeds" and explains the metaphysical status of Egypt as a spiritual boundary or "strait" adjacent to holiness.</summary>

--- Page 36 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of the tenth Sefirah, Malchut (Kingdom), here transitioning from the persona of Jacob to the symbolic meanings of the "Tree of Knowledge," the "Bride," and the mystical name "This" (Zot).</meta>

The name for the gates of Jerusalem is surely from <term>The Lord</term> <note>original: "יי'", shorthand for the Tetragrammaton</note>; it refers to those barriers that cling from the outside to the Chariot <note>The "Chariot" (Merkavah) refers to the divine structure of the Sefirot</note>, about which You commanded: "they shall not enter into your congregation." Therefore, this attribute is called the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil**.

Observe this principle carefully: because both [good and evil] cling to it, it is written: "Behold, I have set before you life and good, and death and evil" <note>Deuteronomy 30:15</note>—blessing and curse. Everything depends on the "Israel of <term>The Lord</term>"; whichever thing they desire, they draw down through this attribute, whether good or evil. Everything is by their hand, as it is written: "Let your father and your mother be glad" <note>Proverbs 23:25</note> and it is written: "A foolish son is the grief of his mother" <note>Proverbs 10:1</note>. And in the chapter "Where is the bill of your mother’s divorcement?" <note>Isaiah 50:1</note>, it says "for your transgressions was your mother sent away." 

Therefore, you will find regarding the **Covenant of Circumcision** that good and evil are both attached; the foreskin (*Orlah*) surrounds the good. When the foreskin is removed, the good appears and is revealed. This is the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil**. Keep this principle very carefully. 

Behold, the First Man (Adam) was commanded to make use only of the Kingdom of <term>The Lord</term>, which is the Good, and that is the meaning of "avoiding evil." But he brought an "idol into the Temple" <note>A metaphor for sinning within the holy realm</note>; he defiled the sanctuary of <term>The Lord</term>. The <term>Shekhinah</term> <note>The Divine Presence, the feminine aspect of God</note> departed, the "bundle" was separated, and man was cursed and blinded. Their sins were so great they "withheld the good from you" <note>Jeremiah 5:25</note>—they surely withheld the Good, and thus Evil was found clinging to the earth, and a curse was sent upon the ground. 

For this reason, even a thought can bring defilement, as when the Priest enters on the Day of Atonement to sanctify the Holy of Holies. Therefore, "he shall not come at all times into the Holy Place" <note>Leviticus 16:2</note>, for there are times for Good and there are times for Evil. But it is written: "With **this** (*Zot*) shall Aaron come into the Holy Place" <note>Leviticus 16:3</note>. "**This**" is Jerusalem; "**This**" is the Torah; "**This**" is the Blessing; "**This**" is the heritage of the servants of <term>The Lord</term> and their righteousness. 

Because they failed in this, the Elders were punished when "they saw God, and did eat and drink" <note>Exodus 24:11; interpreted here as an inappropriate vision</note>. And it is written regarding the sons of Aaron: "And they offered strange fire before <term>The Lord</term>" <note>Leviticus 10:1</note>—this is to teach you and guard you from "strange fire." 

The end of the matter is: as long as the foreskin clings to the Good, the Good remains hidden and concealed. Therefore: "When you come into the land and shall have planted all manner of trees for food, then you shall count the fruit thereof as uncircumcised (*Orlah*); three years it shall be as uncircumcised unto you, it shall not be eaten" <note>Leviticus 19:23</note>. These represent three "shells" (*Klipot*) of the foreskin, and they are called a "sickly evil." The fourth year is an intermediate stage. Therefore, "And in the fifth year"—this is the secret of the Kingdom (*Malchut*), which is the secret of the final letter *He* of the Name; then everything is pure. 

And it is permitted "to eat the fruit thereof, that it may yield unto you the increase thereof" <note>Leviticus 19:25</note>, for then everything is good and there is no evil in it. Thus, some of the wonders regarding the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil have been explained to you, and from this, you may enter many chambers. This attribute is also called in the *Song of Songs*:

### The Bride

The secret of the **Bride** (*Kallah*) is when the evil is distanced from her and all the shells (*Klipot*) are removed. Then she is complete in her sovereignty; she has no flaw or blemish at all. For she comes from the attribute that is the "Foundation of All" (*Yesod*). She "visits the earth and waters it" <note>Psalm 65:10</note>, and to her come types of flow, emanation, and blessings from the head of the **Crown** (*Keter*). 

She is called *Kallah* (Bride) from the word *Kol* (All), because all flow, emanation, blessing, and life are within her. This is the meaning of: "A garden locked is my sister, my bride" <note>Song of Songs 4:12</note>—meaning that the forces of impurity shall not enter her. Then she is sanctified and adorned with all kinds of jewelry that come to her: nine "mirrors" <note>Refers to the nine Sefirot above Malchut</note>. "As a bridegroom decks himself with a priestly crown, and as a bride adorns herself with her jewels" <note>Isaiah 61:10</note>. When the bridegroom truly decks himself with a priestly crown, then she, like a bride, puts on her jewels. 

Therefore: "I will greatly rejoice in <term>The Lord</term>." "You shall no more be termed Forsaken; neither shall your land any more be termed Desolate" <note>Isaiah 62:4</note>. Contemplate the secret of: "I have come into my garden, my sister, my bride" <note>Song of Songs 5:1</note>—my literal garden. Therefore: "As the bridegroom rejoices over the bride, so shall your God rejoice over you" <note>Isaiah 62:5</note>. 

But if, Heaven forbid, she is defiled by sins, then she is "divorced" from her place, and "the sword of <term>The Lord</term> is filled with blood" <note>Isaiah 34:6</note>. May the Blessed Name save us from this punishment! 

I am hinting here: when the shells (*Klipot*) go outside, and everything is unified in the wedding canopy (*Chuppah*) and "no stranger is with them in the house," then all types of foreskins and shells remain outside. It is written: "Spread therefore your wing over your handmaid; for you are a near kinsman" <note>Ruth 3:9</note>. The sign of this is "redemption shall be for it" and "in the Jubilee he shall go out" <note>Leviticus 25:24, 28</note>. 

The **Jubilee** (*Yovel*) is the fiftieth year, when all types of flow and drawings-down that come to the "Foundation of the World" (called *Kol* or "All") are distributed. And when she is unified with the King (the letters *Vav* and *He* of the Name), which is the secret of the final *He*, then she is called the **Bride** (*Kallah*)—one unity. <term>The Lord</term> is His Name and His memorial. Hear this and know it for yourself. This attribute is called:

### This

-> "And **this** (*Zot*) is the blessing, wherewith <-
-> Moses the man of God <-
-> blessed..." <note>Deuteronomy 33:1</note> <-

<summary>This page discusses the dual nature of the Sefirah Malchut as the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil, which reflects the moral state of Israel. It further explains the transition of this attribute into the "Bride" (Kallah) when purified from the "shells" of evil.</summary>
