<meta>The previous page concluded with the discussion of the "Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil" and how human actions draw down either blessing or curse. It specifically mentioned the "idol in the Temple" as a metaphor for spiritual defilement within the Kingdom (Malchut). This page continues by identifying the geographical and spiritual boundaries of this attribute, focusing on Edom and the transition from judgment to mercy.</meta>

"My sword shall descend upon Edom" <note>Isaiah 34:5</note>; "The sword of <term>The Lord</term> <note>original: "יי'", the Tetragrammaton</note> is filled with blood," etc. "Who is this that comes from Edom, with crimsoned garments?" <note>Isaiah 63:1</note> "Why is your apparel red?" <note>Isaiah 63:2</note> Behold, here are explained to you wondrous secrets regarding the boundaries of the Land of Israel, all of which are destined to be purged. And then, "when <term>The Lord</term> your God shall enlarge your border" <note>Deuteronomy 12:20</note>; "Enlarge the place of your tent" <note>Isaiah 54:2</note>. And then <term>The Lord</term> shall be King over all the earth.

From this place, you shall understand the secret of the **two goats** on the Day of Atonement: "And the goat shall bear upon him all their iniquities unto a land of cutting <note>Eretz Gezeira—often translated as a 'solitary land' or 'precipice'</note>... and he shall let go the goat in the wilderness" <note>Leviticus 16:22</note>. And your "South side shall be from the wilderness of Zin along by the side of Edom" <note>Numbers 34:3</note>. "I loved Jacob, but Esau I hated; I made his mountains a desolation and his heritage a home for desert jackals" <note>Malachi 1:2-3</note>. 

Behold the secret of what our Sages, of blessed memory, said: "The Holy One, blessed be He, is as meticulous with the righteous as a **hair's breadth**" <note>original: "כחוט השערה" (*k'chut ha-sa'arah*)</note>. This is because the word for "Goat/Hairy" (*Se'ir*) shares a border with the attribute called **Justice** (*Tzedek*). For this reason, Jacob and Esau were born as one. And therefore it is written: "You are to pass through the border of your brethren the children of Esau, who dwell in Seir... and they shall be afraid of you; take good heed... do not contend with them, for I have given Mount Seir unto Esau for a possession" <note>Deuteronomy 2:4-5</note>. 

The reason is that the Name, may He be blessed, left them there to rule over Israel and to be meticulous with them "to a hair's breadth"—for "round about Him it is very tempestuous" <note>Psalm 50:3; the Hebrew word for 'tempestuous' (*nish'arah*) shares a root with 'hair' (*sa'ar*) and 'Seir'</note>. "These are the nations which <term>The Lord</term> left, to prove Israel by them" <note>Judges 3:1</note>. But in the end: "Saviors shall come up on Mount Zion to judge the mount of Esau; and the kingdom shall be <term>The Lord's</term>" <note>Obadiah 1:21</note>. "Though you exalt yourself as the eagle, and though you set your nest among the stars, thence will I bring you down, says <term>The Lord</term>" <note>Obadiah 1:4</note>. 

Behold, we have explained to you great principles regarding the **Red Sea** <note>original: "ים סוף" (*Yam Suf*)</note> and the matter of the **Storm** <note>original: "סופה" (*Sufah*)</note>. Guard them, for they are hidden in several places in the Torah. The sign for this is: "Wherefore it is said in the book of the Wars of <term>The Lord</term>: Vaheb in Suphah (*Sufah*), and the valleys of Arnon" <note>Numbers 21:14</note>. The explanation of this verse is that at the Red Sea were the "Wars of <term>The Lord</term>," and in the valleys of Arnon is the secret of "partnership" <note>The author puns on *Arnon* and *Arnona*, a Roman crop tax or communal tribute</note>: the dough-tax of Arnon, the cattle-tax of Arnon. One who knows this secret knows the secret of the "fords of Arnon" and the great miracles performed there. Arnon is the secret of the boundary-place between Moab and Israel, as it is written: "For Arnon is the border of Moab,

<column-break/>

between Moab and the Amorites." And Israel inherited the land of the Amorites, as it is written: "And Israel dwelt in all the cities of the Amorites" <note>Numbers 21:25</note>. Therefore, regarding "the valleys of Arnon," it means the war that defined the kingdom of the Red Sea is called by this name. The "fords of Arnon" are called Arnon by the Amorites, and they fought on behalf of Israel in every appropriate place according to the Name. He sent what was appropriate for her: the Red Sea for the Egyptians, and Arnon for the Amorites. 

From here, you may enter the secret of the "Rose among the thorns" <note>Song of Songs 2:2</note>, and the secret of "This is Jerusalem; I have set her in the midst of the nations and countries that are round about her" <note>Ezekiel 5:5</note>. Also the secret of the "sixty mighty men round about her... all of them handling the sword" <note>Song of Songs 3:7-8</note>. In this way, you will understand many hidden things from the Torah. And this attribute is called 

## **Tree**
of Knowledge. This means the 
Tree which is the 
**Throne for Knowledge** (*Da'at*), 
and it draws life, flow, and dominion from Knowledge, which is "Beauty" (*Tiferet*). This is just as the light of the moon draws light from the sun. 

If the "pipes" of influence were to be cut off from reaching her from Knowledge, then those "thorns" and types of impurity and the "foreskin" (*Orlah*) surrounding her—those rebellious and stinging ones—would take hold of her. Then the "idol" <note>original: "הצלם" (*ha-tzelem*), referring to the image of an idol</note> stands in the Temple and the filth is cast therein. This is the meaning of "when the adversary and the enemy entered into the gates of Jerusalem" <note>Lamentations 4:12</note>, for "she has seen that the nations are entered into her sanctuary, whom You did command that they should not enter into Your congregation" <note>Lamentations 1:10</note>. 

Therefore, it is the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil**. As long as it draws from Knowledge, it dwells upon **Justice** (*Tzedek*), which is called "Good." "Say of the righteous that it shall be well (*tov*) with him" <note>Isaiah 3:10</note>. Then the moon is at its full and the Sanctuary is in purity, and the Tree of Knowledge is "Good." "She does him good and not evil all the days of her life" <note>Proverbs 31:12</note>. This means as long as the Sefirot draw from the "World of Life"—the **Tree of Life** which clings to the Kingdom (*Malchut*)—she bestows good and not evil all the days of her life. 

But once the pipes of Knowledge are cut off from the King to the Kingdom, then the attribute of Good departs and Evil arrives. This is the secret of "Also, that the soul be without knowledge (*Da'at*) is not good" <note>Proverbs 19:2</note>. Because one is attached to the other, when the impure forces, the "foreskins," and the filth flow in, then the "Righteous One" is gathered in, for "the righteous is taken away from the evil to come" <note>Isaiah 57:1</note>. For the hand of evil is from <term>The Lord</term>.

It explains that when the flow of divine "Knowledge" is interrupted, impurity and foreign nations (represented by Edom/Esau) gain power over the Sanctuary.</summary>

--- Page 38 ---
<meta>The previous page discussed the mystical borders of the Land of Israel and the spiritual significance of Edom, linking physical geography to the divine attribute of Judgment. It concluded by mentioning the concept of "hairy" (Se'ir) in relation to the scapegoat of Yom Kippur and the precision of divine justice, which is compared to a "hair's breadth." This page transitions into a discussion of the attribute of Malchut (Kingdom) as the "Book of Life" and the union of divine forces during the festivals.</meta>

...etc. The secret is "Let us make a name for ourselves" <note>Genesis 11:4, referring to the Tower of Babel</note>. Because of this, His name was called "In Everything" <note>Hebrew: *Bakol*. In Kabbalah, this often refers to the Sefirah of Yesod, the "Foundation," which gathers all divine light</note>. This is the "Wisdom of the People of the East."

Balaam the Wicked was an expert in this wisdom, as it is written: "From Aram, Balak the king of Moab has brought me, from the mountains of the east" <note>Numbers 23:7</note>. Know that there is a difference between the "mountains of the east" and the "head of the mountains of the east," a difference as great as the superiority of light over darkness. You will grasp more of this by the decree of the Blessed Name.

This divine attribute is called the **Book** of Life. It is the attribute that receives life from **Understanding** <note><term>Binah</term>: the third of the ten Sefirot, often associated with the "Divine Mother" or the source of life-flow</note>, which is the eighth attribute relative to it. **Understanding** is called "Life." When the attribute is filled from that life, it is called a "Book" and is called the "Tree of Life"—meaning the Earthly realm that receives the flow of life from **Understanding** through the "Living God" <note>Hebrew: *El Chai*</note>, which is the **Foundation** <note><term>Yesod</term>: the sixth Sefirah, the channel of procreation and flow</note>. Concerning this, it is said: "Let the earth bring forth a living soul according to its kind" <note>Genesis 1:24</note>. Our Sages of blessed memory said: "This refers to the soul of the Messiah."

When human beings purify themselves and fulfill the commandments—which extend from **Understanding**, the essence of life—they merit to fill this attribute with life and cling to it. Regarding this, it is said: "But you who held fast to the Lord your God are all alive today" <note>Deuteronomy 4:4</note>. This is the secret of "See, I have set before you life and good" <note>Deuteronomy 30:15</note>; meaning, life comes to this attribute through the "Righteous One" <note>the Sefirah of Yesod</note> who is called "Good." Therefore, it is called the **Book of Life**, meaning it is through the Righteous ones who "scribe" <note>Hebrew: *soferim*, a play on the word for Book, *Sefer*</note> the Torah and emanate "Good" unto it from **Understanding**.

The reason for this is the same as the verse: "Everyone who is recorded for life in Jerusalem" <note>Isaiah 4:3</note>. Behold, the righteous join the Tree of Life with the Tree of Knowledge and unify them; then they are inscribed in the Book of Life. Guard this principle well: "She does him good and not evil all the days of her life" <note>Proverbs 31:12</note>, for as long as life is flowed into her through the "Good." And "Benaiah son of Jehoiada, the son of a living man, great in deeds, from Kabzeel" <note>2 Samuel 23:20</note>—a wondrous and great matter is hinted at in this verse. This is the "Open Book of Life," in which the completely righteous are literally written and sealed, as it is said: "Everyone who is recorded for life in Jerusalem."

And this attribute is called **The Eighth** Day of Assembly <note>original: *Shemini Atzeret*</note>. I am now opening great gates before you; open your eyes and incline your ear, and you shall see intellectual concepts perceived through physical things in the Torah. Regarding Passover, it says: "Six days you shall eat unleavened bread, and on the seventh day there shall be an assembly (*Atzeret*) for the Lord your God" <note>Deuteronomy 16:8</note>. They said that the seventh day of Passover is an "Assembly," but it is "for the Lord" and not "for Israel." But regarding the Festival of Booths <note>Sukkot</note>, it says: "On the eighth day, there shall be an assembly for you" <note>Numbers 29:35</note>. This means that on the day of **Shemini Atzeret**, it is truly ours.

Now contemplate: the "seventh" is an assembly, and the day belongs to Him, for He is the "Righteous One," the "Foundation of the World" <note>Yesod</note>, and He is the seventh. He is among the nine "Upper Shining Mirrors" <note>original: <term>Ispaklaryot</term>, a term for the Sefirot as lenses of divine light</note>. But regarding the Festival of Booths, it says the "Eighth Assembly" is ours—and this is the **Kingdom** <note><term>Malchut</term></note>, which is the **Divine Presence** <note><term>Shekhinah</term></note> that dwells within Israel. She is "Bathsheba" <note>literally "Daughter of Seven"</note> who is actually the eighth. Thus, the Assembly of Passover is His, and the Assembly of Sukkot is ours. 

Know that His assembly is the seventh, and ours is the eighth. Before the Torah was given, they were unified above and below; after the giving of the Torah, the Eighth Day of Assembly is ours. Guard this principle well. You will find that the word "Assembly" (*Atzeret*) is a term for "Kingdom" <note>Kingship/Governance</note>, as it is said: "This one shall rule (*ya'atzor*) over my people" <note>1 Samuel 9:17</note>. Know that the Assembly of Passover and of Sukkot both stem from the One. 

Therefore, return to "Remember" and "Observe" <note>the two versions of the Sabbath commandment</note>: "Remember" the day and "Observe" the night. Consequently, the Sabbath has no partner, and the Community of Israel is its partner. This is the secret of the Festival of Weeks <note>Shavuot</note>, when the Upper Assembly and the Lower Assembly are unified as they were at the giving of the Torah—meaning, the **Foundation** (*Yesod*) with the **Kingdom** (*Malchut*).

Open your eyes and see the secret of the cantillation marks <note>musical notations for chanting Torah</note> that are among us in the Torah. Every word that has two marks upon it through the verse is a secret of the union of two things as one. It is written: "On the day of the first-fruits, when you offer a new meal-offering to the Lord in your Weeks (*Shavuot*)" <note>Numbers 28:26</note>. Here everything is explained: His and ours are unified as one. As it is said: "On the day of his wedding, and on the day of the joy of his heart" <note>Song of Songs 3:11</note>. "The day of his wedding"—this is the giving of the Torah. "The day of the joy of his heart"—this is the building of the Holy Temple. 

In this way, "And they said: 'Indeed, we will return with you to your people'" <note>Ruth 1:10</note>; one is the essence and the second is secondary. Likewise: "Then Rachel and Leah answered and said to him" <note>Genesis 31:14</note>. Remember this in every place where you find a word divided by two cantillation marks in this manner. 

And on the Festival of Weeks, the Torah was given...

<summary>This page explores the Kabbalistic significance of the "Book of Life," identifying it with the Sefirah of Malchut (Kingdom) as it receives flow from Binah (Understanding). It further explains the mystical differences between the holidays of Passover, Sukkot, and Shavuot, focusing on the union of divine attributes.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the divine attributes, specifically transitioning from the concept of the "Book of Life" and the attribute of Foundation (Yesod) to the attribute of Kingdom (Malchut), which the author identifies through the mystical interpretation of the Hebrew word "Zot" (This).</meta>

"And this is what their father spoke to them and blessed them" <note>Genesis 49:28</note>. It is a sign to "bestow a blessing into your house." I am here hinting at the verse: "This shall be called Woman, for from Man she was taken" <note>Genesis 2:23</note>. 

**This** <note>Hebrew: *Zot*</note> is a great secret from the foundation of faith. This secret is called **Kingdom** <note><term>Malchut</term>: the tenth and final Sefirah, representing the manifest world and the feminine aspect of the Divine, often called the Shekhinah</note>, and it is also called "This." It is called "This" because all the King's treasures are in her hand, from *Aleph* to *Tav* <note>The first and last letters of the Hebrew alphabet, signifying a complete range</note>. "This is the heritage of the servants of the Lord, and their vindication is from Me, says the Lord" <note>Isaiah 54:17</note>. "The Lord your God you shall fear." "This is the burnt offering of every month" <note>Numbers 28:14</note>. Judah inherited this and merited the **Kingdom**. Regarding Judah, it says: "And this for Judah, and he said..." <note>Deuteronomy 33:7</note>. David also declared: "**This** was mine, because I kept Your precepts" <note>Psalm 119:56</note>. 

Therefore, the righteous have cleaved to this attribute. For this reason, it is called "**This** is the Torah which Moses set" <note>Deuteronomy 4:44</note>. Furthermore, every High Priest who must enter the innermost sanctum <note>The Holy of Holies</note> on the Day of Atonement does not enter except through this attribute. The secret is found in the verse: "With **this** <note>Hebrew: *Be-Zot*</note> shall Aaron come into the holy place" <note>Leviticus 16:3</note>. For if this attribute does not enter with him, he will achieve nothing of all his needs, nor will he see the face of the Blessed King except through this attribute. This is the meaning of: "But let him who glories glory in **this**" <note>Jeremiah 9:23</note>.

The great secret is: "Where has your beloved gone, O fairest among women? Where has your beloved turned, that we may seek him with you?" <note>Song of Songs 6:1</note>. If we wish to seek the face of the Blessed Name, we cannot attain Him except through "you" <note>The Shekhinah/Malchut</note>. Thus, "everyone who sought the Lord went out to the tent of meeting" <note>Exodus 33:7</note>, and "I will meet with you there" <note>Exodus 25:22</note>, and "Let them make Me a sanctuary, that I may dwell among them" <note>Exodus 25:8</note>. "Who is **this** that looks forth like the dawn?" <note>Song of Songs 6:10</note>. "For **this** let every godly man pray to You in a time of finding" <note>Psalm 32:6</note>—meaning at the literal "time of finding."

And behold, the wise Kabbalists everywhere call this attribute the **Last Wisdom** <note><term>Chokhmah Acharonah</term>: a term for Malchut when it acts as a reflection or manifestation of the higher Wisdom</note>. The reason is that the Thought which cleaves to the **Supreme Crown** <note><term>Keter Elyon</term>: the highest Sefirah, the primal Divine Will</note> is the secret of the letter *Yod* of the Name of God, and it is called **Wisdom** <note><term>Chokhmah</term>: the second Sefirah, the flash of intuitive insight</note>. Within that **Wisdom**, no creature in the world can know its place or grasp it. Concerning this, it is said: "But where shall Wisdom be found?" <note>Job 28:12</note>. It is found from "Nothingness" <note>Hebrew: *Ayin*</note>—literally from the place called *Ayin*. That Wisdom is called the "Essence of Thought," and it is the beginning of **Emanation** <note><term>Atzilut</term>: the highest of the four spiritual worlds, where the divine light is still unified</note>. Of it, it is said: "Deep, deep; who can find it?" <note>Ecclesiastes 7:24</note>. Therefore, the void said, "It is not in me," and the sea said, "It is not with me." Destruction and Death said:

---

"We have heard a rumor of it with our ears. God understands its way, and He knows its place" <note>Job 28:14, 22-23</note>. God certainly understands its way and knows its place. Of this, it is said: "How very deep are Your thoughts" <note>Psalm 92:5</note>, and it is written: "Deep, deep; who can find it?" No creature can comprehend that attribute. Regarding it, they said: "The Torah is the 'fallen fruit' of the higher Wisdom" <note>original: "Novelet Chokhmah shel ma'alah Torah"</note>. 

The truth is that the channels and the flows that emanate from it, when they reach through the channels down to the **Kingdom**, she becomes filled by it, according to the secret of: "For the earth shall be full of the knowledge of the Lord, as the waters cover the sea" <note>Isaiah 11:9</note>. Then, that flow which comes to her from the **Supreme Wisdom** and fills her is also called by its name: "Wisdom." But we call it **Last Wisdom**. This is the "Wisdom" of which it is said: "And in the heart of all who are wise-hearted I have set wisdom" <note>Exodus 31:6</note>. This is the "Wisdom" that was built for Solomon.

However, the **First Wisdom** is such that no person in the world can comprehend it. Of it, Solomon said: "All this I have tested by wisdom; I said, 'I will be wise,' but it was far from me" <note>Ecclesiastes 7:23</note>. Rather, Solomon is saying: "Even though the **Last Wisdom** was given to me as a gift, I endeavored to enter further, perhaps to attain the **Supreme Wisdom**, but I saw that all the comprehensions and all the channels were closed before me." One cannot enter the **Supreme Wisdom**, for it is not surrendered to any creature. Of it, it is said: "God understands its way, and He knows its place." 

Thus, you find that Solomon only attained the **Last Wisdom**. This is the secret of the verse: "And Solomon sat on the throne of the Lord as king" <note>1 Chronicles 29:23</note>, and "Solomon’s wisdom excelled the wisdom of all the people of the East and all the wisdom of Egypt" <note>1 Kings 5:10</note>. You already know what we told you: that Egypt is second to the Land of Israel. As for the "people of the East," their wisdom was great because they received it during the generation of the Tower of Babel <note>original: *Dor Ha-Palagah*</note>, as it is said: "And it came to pass as they journeyed from the east" <note>Genesis 11:2</note>. They said: "Let us make a name for ourselves." See how far their wisdom reached! 
<margin>1</margin>
For in that same place, the vessels of the Holy Temple were exiled. He who understands this will understand the image that Nebuchadnezzar made, commanding all the nations to bow down before it. Concerning him, it is said: "And I will punish Bel in Babylon," etc. <note>Jeremiah 51:44</note>.

<summary>This page explores the mystical attribute of Malchut (Kingdom), identifying it as the "Last Wisdom" and the necessary gateway ("Zot") through which one accesses the Divine. It contrasts this with the "First Wisdom," which remains inaccessible even to King Solomon.</summary>
<keywords>Malchut, Chokhmah, Solomon, Zot, Shekhinah, First Wisdom, Last Wisdom, High Priest, Tower of Babel, Sefirot</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the attribute of Kingdom (Malchut/Shekhinah). Having previously established the Shekhinah as the destination for divine blessing, the author now explains how the three Patriarchs—Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob—worked to repair the spiritual damage caused by Adam and restore the flow of divine light to the Shekhinah.</meta>

...it extends all the way down to the "well" of the **Shekhinah** <note>The Divine Presence</note>, which dwells among the lower beings. As it is said: "The heavens are My throne and the earth is My footstool" <note>Isaiah 66:1</note>. Then, the Name was one and the world was one, and all things followed the order of the true celestial arrangement. 

This continued until the first man, **Adam**, arrived and damaged all the **channels** <note><term>Tzinorot</term>: the spiritual conduits that allow divine light to flow between the Sefirot</note>, and through him, the unity was dismantled. This lasted until **Abraham**, our father (peace be upon him), arrived and saw the ruin of these arrangements. He understood and realized how the Shekhinah had departed because of the sin Adam committed. 

Abraham began to reorganize the channels. From the **Right Side** <note>The attribute of Mercy or *Chesed*</note>, he repaired the arrangement on the right side of the Creator (blessed be He), to emanate and flow into the Shekhinah from the **Highest Crown** <note><term>Keter Elyon</term>: the first and highest of the ten Sefirot, representing Divine Will</note> down to the **Kingdom** <note><term>Malchut</term>: the tenth Sefirah</note>. 

This is the secret of the verse: "And he moved from there to the mountain east of Bethel" <note>Genesis 12:8</note>. "East" <note>Hebrew: *Mi-Kedem*, also meaning "from the ancient/primordial"</note> is the secret of the Highest Crown, extending down to **Bethel**, which is the secret of the Kingdom—for it is the "House of God" <note>Hebrew: *Beit-El*</note>. All of Abraham’s journeys were intended to repair the Shekhinah from the Right Side, because Adam had caused damage from the Left Side. 

Therefore, it says: "And Abram journeyed, going on still toward the South" <note>Genesis 12:9; the South represents the Right/Mercy</note>. Conversely, it is written: "Out of the north evil shall break forth" <note>Jeremiah 1:14</note>. Thus, Abraham began to fix the damaged channels and bring the abundance from the Right Side, as it is said: "His left hand is under my head, and his right hand embraces me" <note>Song of Songs 2:6</note>. 

When the Holy One (blessed be He) saw the deeds of our father Abraham, He said: "Sit at My right hand," and He made Abraham’s "Right" a throne for the Supernal Chariot. Thus, the Kingdom received abundance through Abraham by way of the "Right-side repair." This is the meaning of "He moved from there to the mountain east of Bethel."

Then **Isaac** arrived and fenced the breaches in the **North**. The "North wind" is the place of **Fear** <note><term>Pachad</term>: the attribute of Isaac, associated with Judgment and Strength</note>; he did this so the forces of impurity would have no place to enter, for Adam had breached it. This is the "Fear of Isaac"—meaning, just as one fears an enemy or oppressor. 

Then **Jacob**, our father (peace be upon him), arose <column-break/> and fenced all the breaches on the northern side against the forces of impurity. This is the matter of: "Behold the bed of Solomon, sixty warriors around it, of the warriors of Israel" <note>Song of Songs 3:7</note>. You already know that Isaac is the secret of **Strength** <note><term>Gevurah</term>: the attribute of Judgment</note> and Fear. 

Before Jacob and Esau were born, Isaac embraced sixty warriors toward the North—and "Isaac was sixty years old when she bore them" <note>Genesis 25:26</note>. When Isaac arrived and performed repairs through the "Warriors," it was then written: "They all handle the sword, expert in war; every man has his sword upon his thigh because of fear in the night" <note>Song of Songs 3:8</note>—this refers literally to the "Fear of Isaac."

So, while Abraham acted to draw the channels down from the Right, and Isaac fenced all the breaches on the Left side against the fear of impurity, Jacob arrived and built structures *inside* the wall that Isaac had made. Thus, Abraham and Isaac act as guards from the sides, while Jacob is in the center. 

Then Jacob established a permanent place for the Shekhinah, as it says: "And Jacob went out from Beer-sheba... and he encountered the place... and behold, a ladder was set on the earth... and behold, the Lord stood above it and said: I am the Lord, the God of Abraham your father and the God of Isaac." 

"And Jacob awoke from his sleep and said: How awesome is this place! This is none other than the house of God" <note>literally "House of Elohim," the attribute of Judgment</note>. "And Jacob rose up early in the morning and took the stone that he had put under his head... and he called the name of that place **Bethel**... and this stone, which I have set for a pillar, shall be God's house; and of all that You shall give me I will surely give a tenth to You" <note>Genesis 28:10-22</note>. 

By establishing his tribes, he prepared a place for the Shekhinah and established a resting place for her. He declared that he would draw down all **Ten Sefirot** and arrange the channels in order with perfect unity. 

What his grandfather Abraham began—moving from the "East" <note>Keter</note> to "Bethel" <note>Malchut</note>—Jacob completed. He declared that he would arrange all Ten Sefirot and create an order to repair all damages, until the Kingdom (which is the tenth, and is "Bethel") is unified with the Highest Crown (which is "Holy"). 

"And this stone which I have set as a pillar shall be the house of God, and all that You give me, I will tithe" refers to the ten ordered Sefirot. Thus, Jacob repaired the Shekhinah and arranged her all the way up to the Crown through the unity of the ten ordered Sefirot—something Abraham and Isaac could not fully do. For Abraham and Isaac ascended only to the "Crowns" <note>referring to the side-pillars of the Sefirotic tree</note> and no further; but Jacob ascended to the Crown itself through the secret of the **Middle Line**, via the secret of the **Ladder**.

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic roles of the Patriarchs in repairing the spiritual channels damaged by Adam, with Abraham fixing the Right (Mercy), Isaac the Left (Judgment), and Jacob unifying everything through the Center to the Highest Crown.</summary>
<keywords>Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, Shekhinah, Ten Sefirot, Keter (Crown), Malchut (Kingdom), Channels (Tzinorot), Fear of Isaac, Gevurah (Strength), Ladder, Bethel</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Sefirah of Malchut (Kingdom), transitioning from Abraham’s repairs to how the festivals and the act of prayer connect the divine attributes of Tiferet (Beauty/The Central Pillar) and Malchut (Kingdom/The Divine Presence).</meta>

There is a record
<meta>catchword: Continued</meta>

Gratitude and Beauty were joined with Kingdom on the day of his wedding, before the giving of the Torah—which is Passover—and on the eighth day, the Assembly for the Lord your God during Sukkot <note>The Feast of Tabernacles</note>, which is after the giving of the Torah: "there shall be an assembly for you" <note>Numbers 29:35</note>. Thus, what is His and what is His are all ours. "And let them make Me a sanctuary, that I may dwell among them" <note>Exodus 25:8</note>. It has already been said in a parable of a king who had an only daughter; he married her off, and then the king returned to dwell with her. So it is with "Let them make Me a sanctuary, that I may dwell among them." Since the Name (blessed be He) gave them the Eighth Day of Assembly <note><term>Shmini Atzeret</term>: the festival immediately following Sukkot</note>, which is the "Daughter of Seven" <note><gloss>Bat Sheva</gloss>: a kabbalistic reference to Malchut, the seventh of the lower Sefirot</note>, He comes to dwell with us. Behold, the week of Passover is also ours when it is unified with Kingdom, which is the attribute given to us. Guard this great principle, and you will find how great the power of the festivals and holidays truly is.

However, we must awaken a great matter regarding what they said: that the Eighth Day of Assembly is a festival in its own right. In truth, Passover is the festival of Abraham; his week <note>Shavuot</note> is the festival of Isaac; Sukkot is the festival of Jacob; and the Eighth Day of Assembly is a festival in its own right—it is the festival of the Lord, and it belongs to David <note>Representing the lineage of the Kingdom/Malchut</note>. This is the secret of the "Deputy of David." Guard this great principle. 

This attribute is called **Prayer** (**Tefillah**), and she stands before the Lord constantly to remove all types of thorns and thistles, to clear away the stones, and to prepare the highway for Israel. This is the secret of "O God, do not keep silent; be not silent and be not still, O God. For behold, Your enemies are in uproar" <note>Psalms 83:2-3</note>. This is also the secret of the plea: "In return for my love they are my accusers, but I am prayer" <note>Psalms 109:4</note>. Woe to the creatures for the insult to the Torah! 

I will hint to you why it is called **Tefillah** <note>original: "Tefil-lah"</note>. It is to say that she has no power, nor light, nor existence or joy from herself, except through the flow from **Beauty** (**Tiferet**). Your sign is: "Can that which is tasteless be eaten without salt?" <note>Job 6:6; the Hebrew word for "tasteless" is <term>Tafel</term>, a pun on <term>Tefillah</term></note>. It is as if she is nothing on her own, but rather she is "tasteless" <note>secondary/subordinate</note> next to the **Foundation** (**Yesod**), which is the salt that cuts the covenant. Therefore, the Kingdom of the House of David is a "Covenant of Salt." Just as salt provides flavor to all foods, so the Foundation provides flavor to all the powers of the Kingdom, all her springs, her elevations, and all her matters. This is the secret of the necessity and excellence of prayer.

For it is the secret of true unity, as one hears its true essence: she is secondary <note><gloss>tafel</gloss></note> to Beauty, like the shape of a letter in writing; the Oral Torah is like the light of the moon reflecting the sun. Therefore, Prayer is called "Service" <note><term>Avodah</term></note>: "He who tills <note><gloss>oved</gloss></note> his land shall be satisfied with bread" <note>Proverbs 12:11</note>, for it connects Kingdom <note>original: "Malchu"</note> to Foundation <note>original: "Yesod"</note>. Every person who prays testifies about himself that he has a Master who sustains and supports him, and he has no side of existence except through the Master. This is the secret of Prayer: she is "all tasteless <note><gloss>tafel</gloss></note> without salt." Anyone who does not pray is like one who denies the Root <note>God</note>, as if he has no Master and has no need to ask anything from His Divinity. Of this it is said: "And you have cast Me behind your back" <note>I Kings 14:9</note>. 

This is the secret of "Accepting the Yoke of the Kingdom of Heaven"—meaning, he makes himself secondary <note><gloss>tafel</gloss></note> and the Name (blessed be He) is the Essence. Regarding this, one who wishes to take upon himself the Yoke of the Kingdom of Heaven should wash his hands, put on Tefillin, recite the Shema, and pray. This is the complete Yoke of the Kingdom of Heaven. Thus we learn how great is the power of prayer, and how great is the power of the punishment for one who neglects it. 

Through the prayer that a person performs below, he awakens the supernal Chariots and opens several channels from the **Crown** (**Keter**) which come upon **Kingdom** (**Malchut**), and she is blessed through the one who prays. This is what is meant by "He who blesses himself in the land shall bless himself by the God of Truth <note>original: "Elohei Amen"</note>" <note>Isaiah 65:16</note>. That is to say: first, the attribute of Kingdom is blessed, and when she is blessed, she flows the power of the blessing onto the one who prays. This is the secret of "I will bring them to My holy mountain and make them joyful in My house of prayer" <note>Isaiah 56:7</note>. Deep secrets are hidden in this place regarding the saying: "From where do we know that the Holy One, blessed be He, prays?" All of this is hinted at for those worthy of attaining it. 

After we have stood upon these wondrous matters, know that this attribute is called:

# Beit El
<note>House of God</note>

The reason is: because it is the house of God <note><gloss>El</gloss></note>. I am telling you: know that when the world was created and all the upper and lower arrangements were perfected, and all the arrangements were paired on the seventh day—from the Giver to the Receiver—then the whole world became one for one Lord, and Beauty was above...

<summary>This page explains the kabbalistic significance of the festivals and prayer as mechanisms for unifying the divine attributes of Tiferet and Malchut. It emphasizes that prayer (Tefillah) is inherently subordinate to the divine flow, comparing it to "tasteless" food that requires the "salt" of the Foundation (Yesod) to have essence.</summary>

--- Page 42 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of the divine attributes (Sefirot), specifically contrasting the "Altar of Burnt Offering" (associated with the attribute of Kingdom and mercy) with "hewn stones" (associated with strict judgment). It then moves to explain the "Altar of Incense" as the attribute of Understanding (Binah) and the "Tree of Life" within the "Garden" of Kingdom.</meta>

And this is the secret of the life of the World to Come. Know that the Altar of
the Burnt Offering is the "Courtyard," and through it, the righteousness
of Israel is increased. And the Blessed Name commanded and said: "An altar of earth
you shall make for Me, and you shall sacrifice on it your burnt offerings and your
peace offerings" <note>Exodus 20:21</note>. "Your peace offerings" <note>original: *shelamecha*</note> literally, because it is the attribute
that completes <note>original: *hamashlemet*, a wordplay on the Hebrew for peace offering, *shelamim*</note> the peace between Israel and their Father in Heaven.
Furthermore, it is said: "With whole stones you shall build the
altar of the Lord your God, and you shall offer on it burnt offerings" <note>Deuteronomy 27:6</note>.
And this is a great secret: "you shall not build them of hewn stone" <note>Exodus 20:22</note>.
The reason is that this divine attribute desires the well-being of
Israel and is not overly exacting with them regarding the multitude of
their sins and transgressions. Therefore, He said: "whole stones
you shall build." Even though the stone
is not shaped into a precise form—but is wide on one side,
short on another, thick on one side, and
thin on another—so too with Israel: what they bring
is acceptable to the Altar of Burnt Offering. That is to say,
this divine attribute is not exacting with them when they come
to offer a sacrifice, even if the person is wicked or rebellious;
on the whole, everything is received with favor. But "hewn stones" <note>original: *avnei gazit*</note>
are made with great precision and known measurements,
and if they have uneven places, one strikes them
until they are level. Thus, He said that "hewn stones"
are not appropriate for the Altar of Burnt Offering. Therefore, He said:
"An altar of earth you shall make for Me; you shall not build them of
hewn stone." The reason is "hewn" <note>original: *gazit*</note>, because "hewn" is the name for
all the accusing forces that prosecute Israel
and scrutinize them for every iniquity and every
sin. Therefore, they are not appropriate for the Altar of
Atonement, because they prosecute Israel's guilt.
This is what the scripture says: "He has hedged up my ways with hewn stone" <note>Lamentations 3:9</note>, in the sense
that "your iniquities have separated you" <note>Isaiah 59:2</note>. But it says
the construction of the Temple was with hewn stones, because this attribute
is called the "House of God," which is the Lower Court of Law.
Therefore: "And the king commanded, and they brought great stones,
hewn stones" <note>1 Kings 5:31</note>. The reason is that they
sometimes represent the attribute of Stern Judgment when the
channels do not reach it from the world of Mercy; then it
destroys, kills, and uproots. And this is the secret of: "I will discipline

<column-break/>

you, even I, sevenfold for your sins" <note>Leviticus 26:18</note>. And this is
the secret of what is called the "sword that executes the vengeance of the covenant" <note>Leviticus 26:25</note>. Therefore,
its foundation is hewn stone. Likewise, the place where the
Great Sanhedrin <note>The supreme legal assembly of ancient Israel</note> sat was in the "Chamber of Hewn Stone" <note>original: *Lishkat HaGazit*</note>,
for they do not show mercy in judgment. The likeness of Judgment is like the likeness of
hewn stone: everything they could investigate and scrutinize
according to the law. Observe these principles well, for in
the place of Judgment, the opposite of the Altar of Burnt Offering occurs, because in Judgment
there is no mercy, but the Altar is entirely atonement and mercy.
Know that the attribute of the Altar of Incense is the secret of
Understanding <note><term>Binah</term>: the third of the ten divine Sefirot, often associated with the divine womb or source</note>, which is the secret of the connection of the entire Chariot <note>The structure of the divine realm</note>.
We will explain this further, with God's help. And this attribute is called
"Beer-sheba" <note>Well of Seven</note>, meaning the pool that is filled
from the seven <term>Sefirot</term> above it, in the secret of "Bathsheba" <note>Daughter of Seven</note>,
as was explained above. It is also called the Garden of Eden. Behold, I
will explain: "The Lord planted a garden in Eden" <note>Genesis 2:8</note>. From where was it planted? From the "East" <note>original: *kedem*, which also means "ancient" or "preceding"</note>,
meaning all the Sefirot emanated from the Crown <note><term>Keter</term>: the highest Sefirah</note>,
which is called *Kedem*; all of them are planted in the Kingdom <note><term>Malchut</term></note>. And behold,
Beauty <note><term>Tiferet</term></note>, which is the Tree of Life, is in the place where it is
planted in the Garden, for it is the secret of Kingdom; its sign is: "the work of His hands, that He may be glorified" <note>Isaiah 60:21</note>.
This is a great secret of His unified mystery.
And in this place, there is a deep matter: the secret of
"severing the saplings" <note><term>Kitzutz be-Neti'ot</term>: a kabbalistic term for heresy, specifically the act of separating a divine attribute from its source or treating it as an independent power</note>. For anyone who separates a sapling
from its place of connection is called one who "severs the saplings."
The reason is that Kingdom is the secret of the place
where all the Sefirot are planted to produce a branch
and bear fruit. I am hinting: "And the Tree of Life
was in the midst of the garden" <note>Genesis 2:9</note> truly, for Life is literally within the Garden.
This is "the sprout of God's planting, to be glorified." This is the secret of connection
and unity. But regarding the "Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil," it does not say
that it is *in* the garden. Understand this very well, for the Tree of Knowledge
and the Garden are a different matter, but the Tree of Life is planted within
the Garden. The sign for this is: "the hooks of the pillars" <note>Exodus 27:10; the Hebrew word for "hooks," *vavim*, also refers to the letter *Vav*, which represents the six Sefirot connecting to the Garden</note>. And behold, regarding one who severs
any of the saplings in the Garden, it is said of him: "He is a companion to a man
who destroys" <note>Proverbs 28:24</note>. He is a companion to Jeroboam ben Nebat, who brought destruction
to Israel before their Father in Heaven. "Go down, for
your people have corrupted themselves" <note>Exodus 32:7</note>, for "all flesh had corrupted its way upon the earth"
before Me. "And God saw the earth, and behold, it was corrupted" <note>Genesis 6:12</note>.
Likewise: "and it shall be, when he went in unto his brother's wife, that he spilled it on the ground" <note>Genesis 38:9</note>.
**GATES OF RIGHTEOUSNESS**
<sig>4 A 1</sig>

<summary>This page explores the symbolic distinction between "whole stones" (mercy) and "hewn stones" (strict judgment) in the construction of the Altar and Temple, eventually linking the Tree of Life to the unity of the Sefirot within the "Garden" of Malchut.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the kabbalistic exposition of the divine attributes (Sefirot), moving from the description of the Altar of Burnt Offering to the mystical ladder of Jacob. It explains how the Patriarchs represent different divine "arms" and how Jacob, as the "Middle Line," facilitates the ascent from the lowest realm (Kingdom) to the highest (Crown).</meta>

The "Ladder from bottom to top" <note>original: *ha-sulam mi-mata le-ma'ala*</note> ascends from the realm of **Kingdom** <note>the sefirah of *Malchut*</note> until it reaches the **Crown** <note>the sefirah of *Keter*</note>. It is found that Abraham and Isaac are called the "Arms of the World," <note>representing the attributes of Mercy and Judgment</note> but Jacob, who represents the **Middle Line**, ascended and took hold of the "Head." Thus, all the levels are found to be connected to him through Abraham and Isaac, who are the two "Arms." Consequently, Jacob is found to ascend as far as the **Crown**. This is the secret of the verse: "Then you shall delight in the Lord, and I will cause you to ride upon the high places of the earth, and I will feed you the heritage of Jacob your father" <note>Isaiah 58:14</note>. This "heritage" refers to the life of the **World to Come**, which issues from **Understanding** <note>the sefirah of *Binah*</note>. It is called the heritage of Jacob, and not the heritage of Abraham or Isaac. This is the secret of the three highest Sephirot: **Crown**, **Wisdom**, and **Understanding**. This is the meaning of "I will feed you the heritage of Jacob your father," which is the life of the World to Come, from **Understanding** and upward. Leah has no path in this specific place.

This is also the secret of the musical accents *Zarka, Sakef, Segol*. <note>Cantillation marks used in Torah chanting, interpreted here mystically</note>. The secret lies in the verse: "For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself" <note>Psalm 135:4</note>. This is the secret of the *Segulah* <note>meaning "treasure" or "special property," related to the vowel-point *Segol*</note>, as in "Israel shall be His treasure." Open your eyes and see wonders! This is the secret of: "The Lord alone guided him, and there was no foreign god with him" <note>Deuteronomy 32:12</note>. For the "supernal princes" who are called "foreign gods" have no power to ascend to the place of Jacob, which is from the "Arms" and upward. This is why the verse immediately adds: "He made him ride on the high places of the earth."

Understand these wonders! Jacob is found to unify all the Sephirot from bottom to top, and he draws influence from top to bottom. From bottom to top: "And he dreamed, and behold, a ladder set up on the earth, and the top of it reached to heaven; and behold, the angels of God ascending and descending on it. And behold, the Lord stood above it and said: I am the Lord..." <note>Genesis 28:12-13</note>. From top to bottom: "And Jacob rose up early in the morning, and took the stone that he had put under his head, and set it up for a pillar, and poured oil upon the top of it" <note>Genesis 28:18</note>. This is the secret of: "It is like the precious oil upon the head, running down upon the beard, even Aaron’s beard, that goes down to the skirts of his garments; as the dew of Hermon, which descends upon the mountains of Zion; for there the Lord commanded the blessing, even life forever" <note>Psalm 133:2-3</note>. You already know the secret of the phrase "descends upon the mountains of Zion." He calls it "Jacob" because he drew down influence like the pouring of oil and goodness into this place through the levels from the ancient source until the "House of God" <note>original: *Beit El*</note>. This is what is meant by: "Of all that You give me, I will surely give a tenth to You" <note>Genesis 28:22</note>.

Regarding why Jacob calls this place "House of God" <note>original: *Beit El*</note> in this portion sometimes, and other times "House of the Almighty" <note>original: *Beit Elohim*</note>—it is according to the order of the "Rectifications" <note>original: *Tikkunim*</note> that Abraham and Isaac performed for the **Kingdom** <note>the *Shekhinah* or Divine Presence</note>. When he mentions "House of El," it refers to the secret of the Rectification

---

that Abraham performed for the Divine Presence from the "Right Side," which is called *El*. Therefore, he called it by his name, "House of El" <note>the God of Mercy</note>, for "A King sits upon a throne of Mercy." Thus: "And he called there upon the name of the Lord, the Everlasting God." And when he called it "House of Elohim," he named it after the Rectification performed by Isaac, which is the attribute of **Fear** <note>the sefirah of *Gevurah* or Strength</note> within the secret of Might and Fear. Note these two names that Jacob called this place—*Beit El* and *Beit Elohim*—do not forget them, for you have a great need for this knowledge.

I have already written to you above regarding the attribute that is named after that which fills it, based on the verse "For My name is within him" <note>Exodus 23:21</note>; this is now clarified. When this attribute is joined via the "channels" and the "ascents" of **Understanding**, they are both called *Dar* and *Socheret* <note>terms from Esther 1:6 for types of marble, here used as technical kabbalistic terms</note>. **Kingdom** is called *Dar* because it is the "dwelling place" <note>original: *dirah*</note> for the Lord, and He dwells among us, as it is said: "Who dwells with them in the midst of their uncleanness" <note>Leviticus 16:16</note>, and "I will set My tabernacle among you." Understand this well.

The eighth level, which is **Understanding** (*Binah*), unifies the **Crown** and encompasses all the Sephirot and all the celestial arrangements. It is called *Socheret* from the language of "surrounding" and "circulating" <note>original: *sochur sochur*</note>. This is the reason Abraham (may he rest in peace) took the **Cave of Machpelah**: to ascend from there upward, from the "Arms" to the place of the **Crown**. This is the secret of "Four hundred shekels of silver, passing to the merchant" <note>original: *over la-socher*, Genesis 23:16</note>. Behold, this place is for entering from there into the World to Come, for it is the gateway that stands before the "Arms of the World," and from there one enters the life of the World to Come. This is the secret of "passing to the merchant" (*socher*) and the secret of *Dar* and *Socheret*. Open your eyes and see how great the level of the Field of Machpelah is, for it is the "Gatehouse" to the Upper Eden to enter the place of **Understanding**. Keep this and know it for yourself!

The secret of these "Two Stones" is the secret of: "And you shall take two onyx stones, and engrave on them the names of the children of Israel" <note>Exodus 28:9</note>. The secret is that he who merits to enter this **Kingdom** merits the life of the World to Come. He who merits *Dar* merits *Socheret*, and this is "Peace." All of Israel has a portion in the World to Come, and this is "Justice, justice shall you pursue, that you may live..." <note>Deuteronomy 16:20</note>. This is the secret of the "Two Stones" and the secret of *Dar* and *Socheret*. And this attribute is called:

# The Altar of the World
## And here I hint at
### The Way of the Altar of Burnt Offering

Through this, Israel merits to ascend to the Upper Realm, which is the secret of the ascent to the Gate of the House of the Holy of Holies.

<summary>This page explains Jacob's ladder as a mystical path for unifying the Sefirot, linking the Patriarchs to specific divine attributes and identifying the Cave of Machpelah as a gateway to the World to Come.</summary>
<keywords>Jacob's Ladder, Sefirot, Malchut, Keter, Binah, World to Come, Cave of Machpelah, Abraham, Isaac, Dar and Socheret, Altar of the World</keywords>

--- Page 44 ---
<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Sefirah of Malchut (Kingdom), connecting it to the architectural laws of the Temple and the spiritual mechanics of the Altar. It transitions from the festivals discussed on the previous page to the "Secret of the World to Come."</meta>

And this is the secret of the life of the World to Come. Know that the Altar of Burnt Offering <note>The outer altar in the Temple courtyard</note> represents the Courtyard <note>A symbolic reference to the Sefirah of Malchut, the gateway to the divine</note>, and through it, one is pardoned and released. The Name (blessed be He) commanded and said: "An altar of earth you shall make for Me, and you shall sacrifice upon it your burnt offerings and your peace offerings" <note>Exodus 20:21</note>. "Your peace offerings" <note>Hebrew: *Shlamim*</note> literally: because the altar brings peace <note>Hebrew: *mashlemet*</note> between Israel and their Father in Heaven. Furthermore, it is said: "You shall build the altar of the Lord your God of whole stones, and you shall offer burnt offerings upon it" <note>Deuteronomy 27:6</note>. And this is a great secret: "You shall not build them of hewn stone" <note>Exodus 20:22</note>.

The reason for this is that this divine Desire <note>The Sefirah of Malchut acting as the Altar</note> seeks the welfare of Israel and does not scrutinize them strictly for the abundance of their sins and transgressions. Therefore, He said: "You shall build of whole stones"—even if the stone is not perfectly shaped, but is wide on one side, narrow on another, thick on one side, or thin on another. Similarly, whatever Israel brings is acceptable to the Altar of Burnt Offering. This means that the Attribute of Judgment <note><term>Middat HaDin</term>: the divine quality of strict justice and law</note> does not scrutinize them when they come to offer a sacrifice; even if a person is wicked, reckless, or worthless, everything is accepted with favor.

However, hewn stones <note><term>Gazit</term>: stones cut to precise measurements with metal tools</note> are made with great precision and known measurements. If there are places in them that are not level, they are cut down until they are equal. Regarding such hewn stones, He said they are not fit for the Altar of Burnt Offering; therefore, He said: "An altar of earth you shall make for Me... you shall not build them of hewn stone." The reason is that "hewn stones" <note>original: *Gazit*</note> is the name for all the prosecuting forces that accuse Israel and scrutinize them for every iniquity and sin. Therefore, they are not fit for the Altar of Atonement, as they would act as accusers against Israel's merit. This is what the verse means: "He has hedged my ways with hewn stone" <note>Lamentations 3:9</note>, which is similar to: "But your iniquities have separated..." <note>Isaiah 59:2</note>. 

Nevertheless, it says that the building of the Temple House was done with hewn stones, because that attribute is called the "House of God," which is the Lower Court <note>The earthly Sanhedrin or the divine court of justice</note>. Therefore: "And the king commanded, and they quarried great stones, costly stones, hewn stones" <note>1 Kings 5:31</note>. The reason is that they sometimes represent the Attribute of Harsh Judgment; when the channels do not reach it from the World of Mercy, it binds, kills, and uproots. This is the secret of: "I, even I, will chastise 

you seven times for your sins" <note>Leviticus 26:18</note>. This is the secret called "the sword that executes the vengeance of the covenant" <note>Leviticus 26:25</note>, and therefore its foundation is hewn stone. Likewise, the place where the Great Sanhedrin <note>The supreme religious assembly in ancient Israel</note> sat to provide sustenance was in the Chamber of Hewn Stone <note><term>Lishkat HaGazit</term></note>, for they do not show [unearned] mercy in judgment. The nature of Judgment is like the nature of hewn stone: everything must be investigated and scrutinized according to the law. 

Keep these principles well, for the place of Judgment is the opposite of the Altar of Burnt Offering; in Judgment there is no mercy, while the Altar is entirely atonement and mercy.

Know that the attribute of the Altar of Incense <note>The inner altar</note> is the secret of Understanding <note><term>Binah</term>: the third Sefirah, associated with the divine mother and repentance</note>, which is the secret of the knot of the entire Chariot <note><term>Merkavah</term>: the structure of the divine realms</note>, as we will yet explain with God’s help. This attribute is called "Beer Sheva" <note>Well of Seven / Well of the Oath</note>, meaning the pool was filled from the sapphires above it, in the secret of "Bat Sheva" <note>Daughter of Seven</note>. This has already been explained above. It is also called the Garden of Eden. Behold, I will explain: "The Lord God planted a garden in Eden" <note>Genesis 2:8</note>—and from where did He plant it? From "the East" <note>Hebrew: *mi-Kedem*, also meaning "from the Ancient One"</note>, meaning all the Sefirot emanated from the Crown <note><term>Keter</term></note>, which is called *Kedem* (Ancient/East). All of them are planted in the Kingdom <note><term>Malchut</term></note>. 

Behold, Beauty <note><term>Tiferet</term></note> is the Tree of Life in the place where it is planted, for it is the secret of Kingdom, as symbolized by: "The work of My hands, that I may be glorified" <note>Isaiah 60:21</note>. This is a great secret from His unique mystery. In this place, the matter is deep; it is the secret of "Cutting the Shoots" <note><term>Katzitza BaNetiyot</term>: a Talmudic metaphor for heresy or spiritual separation</note>. For anyone who separates a "shoot" <note>a Sefirah</note> from its place of connection is called one who "cuts the shoots." The reason is that Kingdom is the secret of the place where all the Sefirot are planted to produce a branch and bear fruit. 

I will hint further: "The Tree of Life was in the midst of the garden" <note>Genesis 2:9</note>—truly, for the Life is within the garden itself. This is "the branch of My planting, that I may be glorified," and this is the secret of connection and unity. But regarding the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil, it does not say that it is "in the garden." Understand this well, for the Tree of Knowledge and the Garden are different matters, while the Tree of Life is planted *within* the Garden, following the secret of the "hooks of the pillars." 

Behold, the one who "cuts" is like one who destroys all the plantings like a garden; of him it is said: "He is a companion to a destroyer" <note>Proverbs 18:9</note>. He is a companion to Jeroboam son of Nebat, who corrupted Israel before their Father in Heaven. "Go down, for your people... have corrupted themselves" <note>Exodus 32:7</note>; "For all flesh had corrupted its way upon the earth" <note>Genesis 6:12</note>; "And God saw the earth, and behold, it was corrupted" <note>Genesis 6:12</note>; and likewise: "And it came to pass, when he went in to his brother's wife, that he corrupted it on the ground" <note>Genesis 38:9</note>. These are the Gates of Righteousness <note>Sha'arei Tzedek</note>.

<summary>This page explores the symbolic difference between the unhewn stones of the Altar (representing Mercy and the acceptance of all people) and the hewn stones of the Temple and Sanhedrin (representing strict Judgment). It also defines the "Tree of Life" as the union of divine attributes within the "Garden" of Malchut, warning against the heresy of "cutting the shoots" or separating these divine connections.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the divine attributes (Sefirot), moving from the nature of the Altar to the mystical ascent of the soul through the "Ladder of Jacob." It explains how Jacob, representing the "Middle Pillar," connects the lower realms to the highest divine crown (Keter), a level not fully accessed by Abraham or Isaac.</meta>

The ladder <note>Hebrew: *Sullam*</note> from bottom to top ascended from **Kingdom** <note>original: *Malchut*</note> to the **Crown** <note>original: *Keter*</note>. It is found that Abraham and Isaac are called the "Arms of the World" <note>representing the attributes of Mercy/Right and Strength/Left</note>, but Jacob, who is the **Middle Pillar** <note>the balancing central line of the Sefirot</note>, ascended and took hold of the Head. Thus, the entire nation of Israel takes hold of him through Abraham and Isaac, who are the "two arms."

Consequently, Jacob ascends all the way to the **Crown**, and this is the secret of the verse: *“Then you shall delight in the Lord, and I will make you ride on the heights of the earth, and I will feed you with the heritage of Jacob your father”* <note>Isaiah 58:14</note>. This "heritage" refers to the life of the World to Come, which flows from **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*</note>. It is called the "heritage of Jacob" and not the heritage of Abraham or Isaac. This is the secret of the "Three Sefirot," which are **Crown**, **Wisdom**, and **Understanding** <note>original: *Keter, Chokmah, Binah*</note>.

This is what is meant by *“I will feed you with the heritage of Jacob your father,”* for this is the life of the World to Come from **Understanding** and above, where the attribute of Leah <note>associated with the hidden, higher world</note> has no path in this place. This is also the secret of the cantillation marks *Zarqa, Qadma, Pashta, Segol* <note>original: *Zar' Qa' Pa' Segol*</note>. The secret is that *“the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself”* <note>Psalms 135:4</note>. The name of the Lord <note>Yah</note> and the letters *Aleph-Mem-Shin* <note>representing the primary elements</note> are the secret of the *Segulah* <note>the "treasure" or the Segol vowel/accent</note>. Therefore, "Israel is His treasure" <note>original: *segulato*</note>.

Open your eyes and see wonders! This is the secret of *“The Lord alone did lead him, and there was no strange god with him”* <note>Deuteronomy 32:12</note>. For the "Upper Princes" <note>angelic rulers of other nations</note> called "strange gods" have no ascent to the place of Jacob, which is from the "Arms" and above. This is why the verse immediately adds, *“He made him ride on the heights of the earth.”* Understand these wonders.

Thus, Jacob is found to unify all the **Sefirot** from bottom to top and to draw influence from top to bottom. From bottom to top, it says:

-> “And behold, a ladder set up on the earth, and its top reached to heaven; and behold, the angels of God were ascending and descending on it. And behold, the Lord stood above it and said, ‘I am the Lord...’” <note>Genesis 28:12-13</note> <-

And from top to bottom, it says: “And Jacob rose up early in the morning, and took the stone that he had put under his head, and set it up for a pillar, and poured oil upon the top of it” <note>Genesis 28:18</note>. This is the secret of:

-> “Like the precious oil upon the head, running down upon the beard, the beard of Aaron, running down over the collar of his robes. Like the dew of Hermon, which falls on the mountains of Zion, for there the Lord has commanded the blessing, life forevermore.” <note>Psalms 133:2-3</note> <-

You already know the secret of "falling on the mountains of Zion." After Jacob drew down the oil and the goodness into this place, it was "from the east to Beit El." This is why he said, *“I will surely give a tenth to You”* <note>Genesis 28:22</note>. Regarding why Jacob called this place "Beit El" <note>House of God</note> in this portion several times—specifically two or three times—it is according to the order of the two "rectifications" <note>term: *tikkunim*, mystical repairs or arrangements</note> that Abraham and Isaac made for the **Kingdom**. When he mentions "Beit El," it is the secret of the rectification Abraham made for the Divine Presence <note>original: *Shekhinah*</note> from the side of the Right <note>Mercy</note>, which is called *Yod*. Therefore, he called it by His name: "Beit El" <note>House of the Mighty God</note>, for *“A King sits on a throne of mercy.”* He called out there in the name of the *“Lord, the Everlasting God.”*

And when he calls it "Beit Elohim" <note>House of God/Judgement</note>, he names it for the rectification of Isaac, which is the attribute of "Man" <note>Adam</note> in the secret of **Strength** <note>Gevurah</note> and **Awe** <note>Pachad</note>. This is why there are two names that Jacob called this place: *Beit El* and *Beit Elohim*. Do not forget this, for it is a great necessity for you. I have already written to you above concerning the attribute called by the name of the thing that fills it, according to the secret of "My name is within him" <note>Exodus 23:21</note>, and this is clear.

When this attribute connects to the "channels" and the ascents of **Understanding**, they are both called *Dar* and *Soharet* <note>original: *dar ve-socharet*, terms from Esther 1:6 traditionally meaning marble or pearls</note>. **Kingdom** is called *Dar* because it is the "dwelling house" <note>from the Hebrew *dirah*</note> for the Lord; She dwells among us, as it is written:

-> “Who dwells with them in the midst of their uncleanness” <note>Leviticus 16:16</note> <-

And: “I will set My tabernacle <note>dwelling place</note> in your midst.” Understand this well. The "eighth" <note>attribute</note> which is **Understanding** <note>Binah</note> unifies the **Crown** and surrounds all the **Sefirot** and all the arrangements. It is called *Soharet* from the language of "surrounding" <note>original: *sechor sechor*</note> or "trading" <note>original: *sachar*</note>. This is why Abraham our father <note>peace be upon him</note> took the Cave of Machpelah—to ascend from there above the "Arms" to the place of the **Crown**. This is the secret of:

-> “Four hundred shekels of silver, passing to the merchant” <note>original: *over la-socher*; Genesis 23:16</note> <-

Behold, this place is for entering from there into the World to Come, for it is the doorway that stands by the "Arms of the World," and through it, one enters the life of the World to Come. This is the secret of "passing to the merchant" and the secret of *Dar* and *Soharet*. Open your eyes and see how great is the level of the Field of Machpelah, for it is the gatehouse to the Upper Eden to enter the place of **Understanding**. Guard this and know it for yourself!

The secret of these "two stones" <note>the onyx stones on the High Priest's shoulders</note> is the secret of:

-> “And you shall take two onyx stones and engrave on them the names of the sons of Israel” <note>Exodus 28:9</note> <-

The explanation is that whoever merits to enter the **Kingdom** merits the life of the World to Come. Whoever merits *Dar* merits *Soharet*, and this is "Peace." All Israel has a portion in the World to Come, and this is:

-> “Righteousness, righteousness you shall pursue, that you may live...” <note>Deuteronomy 16:20</note> <-

This is the secret of the "two stones" and the secret of *Dar* and *Soharet*. And this attribute is called:

# The Altar
of the Burnt Offering. And here I am hinting at the way of the Altar of the Burnt Offering: through it, Israel merits to ascend to the "Upper Chamber," which is the secret of the ascent to the Gateway of the House of the Holy of Holies.

<summary>This page explores the mystical significance of Jacob's Ladder and the Cave of Machpelah as conduits between the earthly realm (Kingdom) and the highest divine realms (Understanding and Crown). It explains how Jacob's central position in the Sefirotic tree allows for a unique unification of divine energies, facilitating the transition to the "World to Come."</summary>
<keywords>Jacob's Ladder, Sefirot, Keter, Binah, Malchut, Cave of Machpelah, Abraham, Isaac, Dar and Soharet, World to Come, Kabbalah, Altar of Burnt Offering</keywords>

--- Page 46 ---
<meta>This page continues the kabbalistic discussion of the Sefirot (divine emanations). While the previous page focused on Jacob as the "Middle Line" connecting the lower realm to the highest Crown, this page shifts to the architectural symbolism of the Tabernacle and Temple—specifically the Altars—to explain how Mercy and Judgment interact within the divine structure.</meta>

And this is the secret of the life of the **World to Come**. Know that the **Altar of Burnt Offering** <note>Hebrew: *mizbe'ach ha-olah*</note> is the "Courtyard," and through it, the ministry is composed. The Blessed Name commanded and said: "An altar of earth you shall make for Me, and you shall sacrifice on it your burnt offerings and your peace offerings" <note>Exodus 20:21</note>. "Your peace offerings" <note>Hebrew: *shelamecha*</note> literally, because it is that which "completes" or "brings peace" <note>original: *ha-meshallemet*</note> between Israel and their Father in Heaven.

Furthermore, it is said: "With whole stones you shall build the altar of the Lord your God, and you shall offer burnt offerings upon it" <note>Deuteronomy 27:6</note>. This is a great secret: "You shall not build them of hewn stone" <note>Exodus 20:22</note>. The reason is that this **Attribute** <note>the sefirah of *Malchut* or Kingdom acting in its receptive, earthly capacity</note> desires the well-being of Israel and does not scrutinize them for the multitude of their sins and transgressions. Therefore He said, "Whole stones you shall build." 

Even though the stone is not shaped in a precise form—but rather is wide on one side, short on another, coarse here, and thin there—so too with Israel: what they bring is acceptable to the **Altar of Burnt Offering**. This is to say that the **Attribute of Justice** does not scrutinize them when they come to offer a sacrifice, even if one is a sinner or lawless; in every case, it is accepted with favor. 

But **hewn stones** <note>original: *avnei gazit*</note> are made with great precision and according to a known measure. If there are uneven places in them, they are struck with a hammer until they are level. Thus, it is said that hewn stones are not fit for the **Altar of Burnt Offering**. Therefore He said: "An altar of earth you shall make for Me; you shall not build them of hewn stone." 

The reason for the term "hewn" <note>original: *gazit*</note> is because "hewn" is the name for all the accusing forces <note>the *Kitrugim*</note> against Israel, who are precise with them for every iniquity and sin. Therefore, they are not fit for the **Altar of Atonement**, as they prosecute Israel. This is what the scripture means: "He has walled up my ways with hewn stone" <note>Lamentations 3:9</note>, in the same sense as "But your iniquities have separated..." <note>Isaiah 59:2</note>. 

However, even the building of the **Temple** <note>original: *ha-Bayit*</note> was done with hewn stones, because this Attribute is called the "House of God," which is the "Lower Court of Justice." Therefore: "The king commanded, and they quarried great stones, hewn stones" <note>1 Kings 5:31</note>. The reason is that they sometimes represent the **Strict Attribute of Justice**. When the "channels" <note>divine flows of energy</note> do not reach it from the **World of Mercy**, it destroys, kills, and uproots. This is the secret of: "I also will chastise

---

you seven times for your sins" <note>Leviticus 26:24</note>. And this is the secret of what is called "A sword that shall execute the vengeance of the covenant" <note>Leviticus 26:25</note>. Therefore, its foundation is "hewn" <note>original: *gazit*</note>. Likewise, the place where the **Great Sanhedrin** <note>the supreme council of seventy-one sages</note> sat was the **Chamber of Hewn Stone** <note>original: *Lishkat Ha-Gazit*</note>. For they show no mercy in judgment, and the likeness of judgment is like the likeness of hewn stone—wherever they could investigate and scrutinize the law. 

Keep these principles, for the "Place of Judgment" is the opposite of the **Altar of Burnt Offering**; in judgment there is no mercy, whereas the Altar is entirely atonement and mercy.

Know that the attribute of the **Altar of Incense** is the secret of **Understanding** <note>the sefirah of *Binah*</note>, which is the secret of the connection of the entire **Chariot** <note>the *Merkavah*, the divine structure</note>, as we shall explain with God's help. This attribute is called **Beersheba**—meaning the "pool" <note>original: *breichah*</note> was filled from the seven Sefirot above it, in the secret of **Bathsheba**. This was already explained above. It is also called the **Garden of Eden**.

I shall now explain: "The Lord God planted a garden in Eden" <note>Genesis 2:8</note>. From where did He plant it? From "the East" <note>original: *mi-kedem*</note>, meaning all the Sefirot emanated from the **Crown**, which is called *Kedem* <note>meaning "ancient" or "preceding"</note>; they are all planted in **Kingdom** <note>the sefirah of *Malchut*</note>. See, **Beauty** <note>the sefirah of *Tiferet*</note>, which is the **Tree of Life**, in the place where it is planted within it <note>Malchut</note>, is the secret of sovereignty. The sign for this is: "The work of my hands, in which I glory" <note>Isaiah 60:21; the word "glory" or *le-hitpa'er* alludes to the sefirah of *Tiferet*</note>. This is a great secret of the Divine Unity.

In this place, there is a deep matter, the secret of **Cutting the Shoots** <note>original: *k'tzitzah ba-netiyot*—a Talmudic metaphor for heresy or causing separation within the divine unity</note>. For anyone who separates a "shoot" <note>an attribute</note> from its place of connection is called "one who cuts the shoots." The reason is that **Kingdom** <note>*Malchut*</note> is the secret of this place where all the Sefirot are "planted" to produce branches and fruit. I hint to you: "The Tree of Life was in the midst of the garden" <note>Genesis 2:9</note>—the Life is literally *inside* the Garden. This is the "shoot of My planting, the work of My hands, in which I glory," and this is the secret of connection and unity. 

But regarding the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil**, it does not say that it is "in the garden." Understand this well, for the Tree of Knowledge and the Garden are different matters, but the **Tree of Life** is planted *inside* the Garden, etc. 

"The hooks of the pillars" <note>Exodus 27:10</note>—behold, the one who "cuts" <note>separates</note> any of the shoots in the Garden, of him it is said: "is a brother to him who is a destroyer" <note>Proverbs 18:9</note>. He is a "brother" to **Jeroboam son of Nebat**, who "destroyed" <note>corrupted</note> Israel's relationship with their Father in Heaven. "Go down, for your people have corrupted themselves" <note>Exodus 32:7</note>, for "all flesh had corrupted its way upon the earth" <note>Genesis 6:12</note>. Before "God saw the earth, and behold, it was corrupt." Likewise: "And it came to pass, when he went in to his brother's wife, that he spilled it on the ground, to corrupt it" <note>Genesis 38:9</note>.

**Gates of Righteousness** <note>original: *Sha'arei Tzedek*</note>

<summary>This page explains the mystical difference between the Altar of Burnt Offering (associated with mercy and "whole stones") and the "hewn stones" of the Temple and Sanhedrin (associated with strict judgment). It then discusses the Garden of Eden as the union of the divine attributes Tiferet and Malchut, warning against the "cutting of the shoots" which represents spiritual separation or heresy.</summary>
