<meta>This page continues the Kabbalistic exploration of the Sefirot (divine emanations) through the figure of the Patriarchs. The previous page discussed the Altar and the Attribute of Justice; this page transitions into the "Ladder of Jacob" and how the Patriarchs represent the "Arms" and the "Middle Line" of the divine structure, leading toward the World to Come.</meta>

The Ladder ascends from below to above, rising from **Kingdom** <note>Hebrew: *Malchut*, the lowest of the ten Sefirot</note> up to the **Crown** <note>Hebrew: *Keter*, the highest Sefirah</note>. It is found that **Abraham** and **Isaac** are called the "Arms of the World," but **Jacob**, who is the "Middle Line" <note>the balancing path between Mercy and Judgment</note>, ascended and took hold of the "Head." Thus, all the people are attached to him through Abraham and Isaac, who are the two "Arms."

We find that Jacob ascends all the way to the **Crown**. And this is the secret of the verse: "Then you shall delight in the Lord, and I will make you ride on the heights of the earth, and I will feed you the heritage of Jacob your father" <note>Isaiah 58:14</note>. This "heritage" refers to the life of the **World to Come**, which stems from **Understanding** <note>Hebrew: *Binah*, the third Sefirah, often associated with the source of the World to Come</note>. This is the "heritage of Jacob" specifically, and not the heritage of Abraham or Isaac. This is also the secret of the three highest Sefirot: **Crown**, **Wisdom**, and **Understanding** <note>Hebrew: *Keter, Chokhmah, Binah*</note>. This is why it says "I will feed you the heritage of Jacob your father," for it is the life of the **World to Come**, from **Understanding** and above. **Leah** <note>Jacob's first wife, often representing the hidden world</note> has no path in this specific place.

And this is the secret of the cantillation marks *Zarka* and *Segol* <note>original: *Zarka, Sekef, Segol*—musical notations for chanting Torah</note>. The secret is that "the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself" <note>Psalm 135:4</note>. The name of God <note>original: *Yah*</note> is the secret of the "Treasure" <note>Hebrew: *Segulah*, playing on the vowel/accent name *Segol*</note>, as in "Israel is His treasure." Open your eyes and see wonders! This is the secret of: "The Lord alone guided him, and no foreign god was with him" <note>Deuteronomy 32:12</note>. This means that the "Upper Princes" <note>angelic regents of other nations</note>, who are called "foreign gods," have no ascent to the place of Jacob, which is from the "Arms" and above. This is why the verse continues: "He made him ride on the heights of the earth." 

Understand these wonders! It is found that Jacob unites all the Sefirot from below to above, and draws influence from above to below. From below to above, this is: "And behold, a ladder was set on the earth, and its top reached the heavens... and behold, the angels of God were ascending and descending on it... and behold, the Lord stood over him and said, 'I am the Lord...'" <note>Genesis 28:12-13</note>. From above to below, it is written: "And Jacob rose early in the morning, and took the stone that he had put under his head, and set it up as a pillar, and poured oil on its top" <note>Genesis 28:18</note>.

This is the secret of: "Like the precious oil upon the head, running down on the beard, the beard of Aaron, running down on the edges of his garments; like the dew of Hermon, which falls on the mountains of Zion; for there the Lord has commanded the blessing, life forever" <note>Psalm 133:2-3</note>. You already know the secret of the phrase "falling on the mountains of Zion." Jacob spoke later, after he had caused the oil and the goodness to flow into this place; it is the Tree of Life <note>original: *Etz*</note> that has existed from the beginning <note>original: *mi-kedem*</note> until the **House of God** <note>Hebrew: *Beit El*</note>. This is why he said: "I will surely give a tenth to You" <note>Genesis 28:22</note>. 

The reason Jacob calls this place **Bethel** <note>Hebrew: *Beit El*</note> at certain times in this portion, and at other times the **House of God** <note>Hebrew: *Beit Elohim*</note>, is according to the order of the two "Rectifications" <note>Hebrew: *Tikkunim*</note> that Abraham and Isaac performed for the **Kingdom** <note>Hebrew: *Malchut*</note>.

<column-break/>

The rectification Abraham performed for the **Divine Presence** <note>Hebrew: *Shekhinah*</note> was from the side of the Right <note>Mercy</note>, which is called *El*. Therefore, he called it by his name: **Bethel** <note>The House of the God of Mercy</note>, for "A King sits on a throne of Mercy." He "called there on the name of the Lord, the Everlasting God" <note>Genesis 21:33</note>. 

But when Jacob calls it the **House of God** <note>Hebrew: *Beit Elohim*</note>, he calls it so because of the rectification performed by **Isaac**, which is the **Attribute of Justice** <note>Hebrew: *Midat Ha-Din*</note>, within the secret of "Power" and "Fear" <note>Hebrew: *Gevurah* and *Pachad*</note>. Guard these two names that Jacob called this place—**Bethel** and **House of God**—and do not forget them, for they are of great necessity for you.

I have already written to you above regarding the **Attribute** that is named after the thing that fills it, according to the verse: "For My Name is within him" <note>Exodus 23:21</note>. This is the name *Yah* <note>original: *YH*</note>; this is clear. This attribute, when it connects through the "Channels" and "Passageways" of **Understanding** <note>*Binah*</note>, both are called *Dar* and *Socheret* <note>Two types of precious stone or pavement mentioned in Esther 1:6</note>. **Kingdom** <note>*Malchut*</note> is called *Dar* because it is the "Dwelling House" <note>Hebrew: *Beit Dirah*</note> for the Lord. It is the dwelling among us, as it is said: "Who dwells with them in the midst of their impurity" <note>Leviticus 16:16</note> and "I will set My Tabernacle in your midst" <note>Leviticus 26:11</note>. Understand this very well.

The letter *He* <note>the second 'H' in the Tetragrammaton</note>, which is **Understanding** <note>*Binah*</note>, unites the **Crown** which encompasses all the Sefirot and all the arrangements. It is called *Charut* <note>Engraved</note>, from the language of "surrounding/circuit" <note>Hebrew: *sachor sachor*</note>. This is why **Abraham**, our father (peace be upon him), took the **Cave of Machpelah**—to ascend from there above the "Arms" to the place of the **Crown**. 

This is the secret of the "four hundred shekels of silver, current money for the merchant" <note>Genesis 23:16</note>. Behold, this place is for entering from there into the **World to Come**, for it is the gateway that stands before the "Arms of the World," and from there one enters the life of the World to Come. This is the secret of "passing to the merchant" <note>Hebrew: *over la-socher*</note> and the secret of *Dar* and *Socheret*. Open your eyes and see how great the level of **Sarah** and the **Machpelah** is, for it is the gatehouse to the **Upper Eden**, through which one enters the place of **Understanding**. Guard this and know it for yourself.

The secret of these two stones is the secret of: "You shall take two onyx stones and engrave on them the names of the children of Israel" <note>Exodus 28:9</note>. The secret is that whoever merits to enter this **Kingdom** merits the life of the **World to Come**. Whoever merits the "Dwelling" <note>*Dar*</note> merits the "Merchant" <note>*Socheret*</note>. This is the "Peace of all Israel," for "all Israel has a portion in the World to Come." This is: "Justice, justice you shall pursue, that you may live..." <note>Deuteronomy 16:20</note>. This is the secret of the two stones and the secret of *Dar* and *Socheret*. And this attribute is called:

## The Altar
of the world. And here I hint at the
->Way of the Altar of Burnt Offering<-
Through which Israel merits to ascend to the Upper Chamber; it is the secret of the ascent of the Gate of the House of the **Holy of Holies**.

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Sefirot (divine emanations), moving from the higher "arms" of the divine structure toward the attribute of Kingdom (Malchut). It focuses on the concept of "Time" and how the physical world's cycles are rooted in divine will.</meta>

...provide food for them in its season <gloss>at its time</gloss> <note>Psalm 145:15</note>—this refers to our own time. Regarding one who asks at all times, what is written immediately after? "You open Your hand and satisfy the desire of every living thing" <note>Psalm 145:16</note>. This refers to a "time of favor" <note>Hebrew: *Et Ratzon*</note>. This is why the Scripture says: "The Lord will open for you His good storehouse, the heavens, to give the rain for your land in its season, and to bless all the work of your hands" <note>Deuteronomy 28:12</note>. 

It is also written: "He shall be like a tree planted by streams of water, which yields its fruit in its season" <note>Psalm 1:3</note> <gloss>meaning</gloss>. And this is the secret of **Remember** and **Keep** <note>Hebrew: *Zakhor* and *Shamor*, referring to the two versions of the Commandment to observe the Sabbath</note>. Our Sages said: "The designated time for the marital intimacy of Torah scholars is from Friday night to Friday night" <note>Talmud, Ketubot 62b</note>. Therefore, rain on Friday nights is a sign of blessing for the world. 

It is written: "Every man shall revere his mother and his father, and you shall keep My Sabbaths" <note>Leviticus 19:3</note>. When Solomon, peace be upon him, delved into the depths of "that which was" <note>Ecclesiastes 1:9</note>, he explained many things and said: "A time to be born, and a time to die; a time to plant, and a time to uproot" <note>Ecclesiastes 3:2</note>. He wrote and detailed all the "times" explained in those verses. Then, he came and explained one single verse to rectify all types of times, saying: "At all times let your garments be white" <note>Ecclesiastes 9:8</note>. This means drawing down the "Upper Whiteness" <note>The pure, merciful light of the higher Sefirot</note> through divine favor (*Ratzon*); then all "times" will be filled with goodness and abundance. 

And how is this flow drawn? It is like the oil from above that flows through the upper channels. At the end of that verse, it says: "and let not oil be lacking on your head." If it is like the oil that is never lacking, then it surely "descends upon the beard, the beard of Aaron" <note>Psalm 133:2</note>, which draws from the World of Kindness (*Chesed*) and the Divine Will. For this reason, the High Priest would enter the Holy of Holies on the Day of Atonement <note>Yom Kippur</note> in white garments. Because they appease and rectify all the "times," it is written: "He shall not come at all times into the Holy Place" <note>Leviticus 16:2</note>. Yet it is also written: "At all times let your garments be white," and "With this shall Aaron come into the Holy Place: with a holy linen tunic..." <note>Leviticus 16:3-4</note>. He shall put on linen breeches upon his flesh; these are holy garments. He shall bathe his body and put them on. Here, the secret of "At all times let your garments be white" is revealed. These are the great principles called the "Time of Favor."

After I have informed you of these great principles regarding the word **Kingdom** <note>Hebrew: *Malchut*</note>, know that it also has many names that testify to it, according to its many levels, for these names are its ornaments and its rectifications <gloss>meaning</gloss>. If we were to explain each and every one in detail, it would require great effort. However, through what I have written for you in this gate, you will enter into many inner chambers. Open your eyes and see how many secrets are hidden and sealed before you.

With the help of God, may He be blessed, and in remembrance of Him, we shall also mention in this composition the other levels of the "Chariot" <note>Hebrew: *Merkavah*, the divine structure</note> and the arrangement of the order of the **Sefirot** <note>the ten divine emanations</note>. We will also mention the names associated with the attribute of **Kingdom**. You already know that the unification <note>Hebrew: *Yichud*</note> of all the Sefirot, according to the Way of Truth, occurs when they all take hold of the attribute of Kingdom. The secret of this is: "And the Lord shall be King over all the earth" <note>Zechariah 14:9</note> <gloss>meaning</gloss>. This is what is said in the *Book of Formation* <note>Hebrew: *Sefer Yetzirah*, an ancient foundational Kabbalistic text</note>: "Ten and not nine." You already know that there are nine luminous mirrors, and one that does not shine on its own <gloss>meaning</gloss>. There is no rectification except through this one <note>The Tenth Sefirah, Malchut, which receives light from the others</note>. Its secret is the "flame bound to the coal." It is a throne for the assembly of a great, deep, and sealed glory.

Know that this Sefirah is the "Last Light" in the Unique Name <note>The Tetragrammaton, Y-H-W-H</note>. It represents the complete secret of the Unification of the Name. All the Sefirot are unified within it, and all the "channels" flow toward it. This is the secret of the final letter *He* (ה) of the Unique Name. 

Know and believe that all 613 commandments of the Torah are woven into the nine Sefirot, like a form made by a skilled designer or an embroiderer, each according to its kind. There are commandments that take hold specifically in the attribute of Kingdom, such as the blue thread (*Tekhelet*) in the ritual fringes (*Tzitzit*). There is enough for one thread of blue and seven threads of white. The secret of this is "Bathsheba" <note>literally "Daughter of Seven"</note> within the "Divine Seven" <gloss>meaning</gloss>. 

The secret of the Day of Atonement is also found here: "One and one, one and two, one and three, one and four, one and five, one and six, one and seven" <note>The High Priest's counting of the blood sprinklings in the Temple</note>. This is the "Preparation." This is the secret of how they descend and unify in the seven that ascend. This is the complete Unification through the seven attachments. Through the seven, they are attached to the nine; in the seventh, the three are attached through the secret of *Yah* <note>The first two letters of God's Name</note> and the secret of the *Segulah* <note>a "treasured" or special property</note>. The sign for this is: "For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself, Israel for His treasure (*Segulah*)" <note>Psalm 135:4</note>. And behold, I am hinting to you: "...to set you high above all nations that He has made, for praise, for fame, and for glory" <note>Deuteronomy 26:19</note>. Here, three places are included together.

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Sefirot, specifically focusing on the final attribute, Malchut (Kingdom), and its various symbolic names such as "Cup," "Tefillin of the Hand," "West," and "Time." It follows a discussion on the cycles of nature and divine favor.</meta>

And the comprehensive secret of the verse "you shall not destroy its trees by wielding an axe against them" <note>Deuteronomy 20:19; a prohibition against wanton destruction</note> is related to "destruction is his, not His children's, their blemish" <note>Deuteronomy 32:5</note>, "a seed of evildoers, children who deal corruptly" <note>Isaiah 1:4</note>. ## Cup

of "all is within it" <note>Hebrew: *Kol Bo*</note>. The reason is that this is the attribute that is filled by the divine flow and emanated from above through the unification of the **ten Sefirot** <term>Sefirot</term> <note>The ten divine attributes or emanations in Kabbalah through which the Infinite interacts with the physical world</note>. This is the secret of what the Sages said: "The cup of blessing requires ten things" <note>Talmud, Berakhot 51a</note>. He who understands this will understand the secret of the ten things. Know and believe that there is another cup outside the partitions of Divine Unity, which is the "cup of retribution." This is what the Scripture refers to: "For a cup is in the hand of the Lord, and the wine foams, fully mixed, and He pours from it" <note>Psalm 75:9</note>. 

Behold, the **Cup of Blessing** is on the inside, and it belongs to David <note>Symbolizing the attribute of Malchut/Kingship</note>. This is what the Scripture says: "You anoint my head with oil; my cup overflows" <note>Psalm 23:5</note> and "I will lift up the cup of salvations and call upon the name of the Lord" <note>Psalm 116:13</note>. However, the cup of retribution is like "the degenerate shoots of a foreign vine" <note>Jeremiah 2:21</note> which is planted on the outside. When Israel sins, they drink from that cup, as it is said: "You who have drunk from the hand of the Lord the cup of His fury, you have drunken the dregs of the cup of trembling" <note>Isaiah 51:17</note>. That wine is made from the "vine of Sodom," from the essence of the venom of the "heavens" <note>or "waters"</note> that dwell on the outside. This is the secret of "Their wine is the venom of serpents," and so on <note>Deuteronomy 32:33</note>. Know and believe that those who do not merit the Cup of Blessing wrong their own souls and lose many types of blessings. If we were to prolong this, we would explain many great matters regarding this subject. This attribute is also called

## Tefillin of the Hand

and the secret is "And it shall be for a sign upon your hand and for frontlets between your eyes" <note>Exodus 13:16</note>. Behold, the **Tefillin of the Head** is the secret of the connection of *Tiferet* <note>Beauty/the central Sefirah</note> to *Keter* <note>the Crown/highest Sefirah</note> by means of *Binah* <note>Understanding</note>. The **Tefillin of the Hand** is the secret of the connection of *Malchut* <note>Kingdom</note> to *Tiferet* <note>Beauty</note> by means of *Yesod* <note>Foundation</note>. With the help of the Exalted Name, when discussing the word *Tiferet*, I will inform you of great principles regarding the matter of Tefillin, their construction, and their purposes. This attribute is also called

## Entrance

<margin>They answer and call the "Tent" for great principles</margin>

---

that you will yet attain with the help of the Blessed Name. It is called the **Opening of the Eyes** <note>Hebrew: *Petach Einayim*</note> because of the providence of the "Entrance" and the "Tent" for the Torah. "And I saw something like the color of *Chashmal*" <note>Ezekiel 1:27; a mystical amber or electrum brilliance</note>, "These seven are the eyes of the Lord" <note>Zechariah 4:10</note>, and "This is the Torah: when a man dies in a tent" <note>Numbers 19:14</note>. It is also called

## West

The reason is that it is the secret of the place of setting and the growth of seeds. I will hint at this: "I will bring your seed from the east, and gather you from the west" <note>Isaiah 43:5</note>. I will add a hint regarding the sun and the moon. Our Sages, of blessed memory, have already said that the **Shekhinah** <term>Shekhinah</term> <note>The Divine Presence, associated with the Sefirah of Malchut</note> is in the West <note>Talmud, Bava Batra 25a</note>. Know that from the day the Holy Temple was destroyed, the taste was taken from fruit, and "there are no grapes on the vine, nor figs on the fig tree, and the leaf has withered" <note>Jeremiah 8:13</note>. In the Story of Creation, everything is hinted at: "And there was evening and there was morning, one day" <note>Genesis 1:5</note>—literally "one day." For this reason, the "Son of David" <note>the Messiah</note> does not come until all the souls in the "Body" <note>Hebrew: *Guf*, the celestial storehouse of souls</note> are exhausted. "And he stayed there overnight because the sun had set" <note>Genesis 28:11</note>. It is also called

## Time

And we must explain—know that we have already explained the reason it is called the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil**. When you understand that path, you will be able to understand the secret of "But as for me, may my prayer to You, O Lord, be at a **time of favor**" <note>Psalm 69:14</note>. For when Knowledge (*Da'at*) joins with Will (*Ratzon*), then all the worlds are rectified, the Sefirot are in unity, the Community of Israel is in perfection, and the moon is full. This is the secret of "But as for me, may my prayer to You, O Lord, be at a time of favor." 

Know and believe that at that hour, the Attribute of Judgment <note>Hebrew: *Midat HaDin*</note> does not rule, for all the worlds are in Mercy (*Rachamim*), and there is no adversary and no evil occurrence. According to this path, the Torah said: "Tell Aaron your brother that he should not come at **all times** into the Holy Place" <note>Leviticus 16:2</note>. Know that there are "times" that are not among the good ones, when impurity joins within the Holy Temple. Regarding this, it is said: "Therefore the prudent man keeps silent at that time, for it is an evil time" <note>Amos 5:13</note>. Therefore, the early pious ones used to "fix the channels" when they came to request of the Lord, until they reached a "time of favor." Know that when the Attribute of Good joins with the Kingdom (*Malchut*), then all kingdoms of every kind receive sustenance, existence, food, and life from the Kingdom. The secret of this is: "The eyes of all look expectantly to You, and You give them..." <note>Psalm 145:15</note>

the power of one who fulfills a commandment, or conversely, how corrupted is the one who commits a transgression. All of this is hinted at in the matter of the **menstruant** <term>Niddah</term> <note>A woman during her menstrual period, requiring ritual purification</note> and the **woman with a discharge** <term>Zavah</term> <note>A woman experiencing irregular bleeding, requiring a more complex purification process</note>. For the menstruant possesses a minor degree of impurity, and therefore she does not require seven [clean] days <note>According to the original biblical law, as opposed to later rabbinic stringency</note>, whereas the woman with a discharge has a flow that goes outside its place; therefore, "she shall count for herself seven days" <note>Leviticus 15:28</note>. Consequently, [the verse says]: "they shall cast out of the camp every **leper**, and every **one with a discharge**, and everyone **impure by the dead**" <note>Numbers 5:2</note>. Three levels of impurity are taught here, corresponding to the three camps <note>The camps of the Shekhinah (Divine Presence), the Levites, and the Israelites</note>. Woe to the creatures for the humiliation of the Torah! If only human beings knew and understood the place where they are corrupted, and the extent to which they are corrupted,

->the power of transgression,<-

->guarded<-

in their souls. Keep this great principle for the entire Torah. Now that we have informed you of these matters, you can know the power of holiness, which is the secret of purity and the divine flow and the addition of the holy; and what is the power of impurity, which is the secret of excess and distance. Impurity is the opposite of purity: one is distance and the other is closeness. One who understands this can understand the secret of the "nine hundred hymens" <note>original: "tet me'ot betuliah"; a Kabbalistic reference to spiritual veils or barriers</note> and the secret of "he has defiled the sanctuary of the Lord" <note>Numbers 19:13</note>. # The Second Sefirah is called
# El Chai <note>Living God</note>; and regarding it

->David, peace be upon him,<-
said: "My soul longs, even faints, for the Living God" <note>Psalm 84:3</note>, and it is written: "My soul thirsts for God, for the Living God," and so on <note>Psalm 42:3</note>. The reason is that when the world is in a state of Divine Will, then the **Crown** <term>Keter</term> <note>The highest Sefirah</note> reveals itself through the secret of the name **El** <note>The Hebrew name for God associated with the attribute of Mercy</note>, and then the entire world is under the influence of Mercy. This is the secret of "God [El], the King, sits upon the throne of Mercy." If you should say: "But is it not written, 'God [El] is indignant every day'?" <note>Psalm 7:12</note>—in truth, this refers to the "El" who is indignant toward all types of destroyers and punishments when it is revealed. It does not say "God is angry" but "indignant" <note>The author distinguishes between 'za'am' (indignation/rebuke) and 'ka'as' (anger)</note>. In this manner, it is the secret of "God Most High" [*El Elyon*] literally, and the secret of "Blessed be Abram of God Most High" <note>Genesis 14:19</note>. This is what draws down mercy and life from above, and when it draws down mercy and life, it is then called **El Chai** <note>Living God</note>. Therefore, the sages established in the prayer: "Living God [El Chai] and Shield"—a shield from all types of destroyers and punishments. Consequently, wherever you find the name **El**, it refers to the attribute of Mercy. This is the name **El** that is revealed at the hour when three hundred and ten <note>The numerical value of the Hebrew word 'Yesh' (Substance/Existence) is 310</note> classes of Judgment are aroused against the world. It is called *Yesh* in a great and hidden secret. When the name "El" is aroused over them, they are all turned into Mercy and are all subdued before Him. This is the secret of "May Your mercy subdue Your anger from upon us," for there is nothing in the entire arrangement of the prayers that does not contain many books worth of meaning. This is [the meaning of] "May Your mercy roll over Your attributes" <note>A liturgical phrase describing God's compassion overcoming strict justice</note>. I am hinting here: they are called "Attributes" [*Midot*] corresponding to what they measure from below; according to the measure, so is it filled. If it is a measure of purity, they fill it from the supernal mountains of holiness. If it is a measure of impurity, they fill it from the "External Ones" <term>Chitzonim</term> <note>Demonic or parasitic spiritual forces</note> and from the filth. This is what was meant by "With the measure a man measures, so they measure out to him" <note>Talmud, Sotah 8b</note>. Therefore, when the name **God Most High** [*El Elyon*] appears, all these [harsh] attributes flee and scatter and are revealed and depart. Then Judgment is turned into Mercy, and this is the secret of "The Lord is King, sitting on the throne of Mercy." And as for what was said, "When the world of Mercy appears, then Your mercy shall subdue Your anger and Your mercy shall roll over Your attributes." **Know** and believe that wherever the attribute of Life appears, [the other attributes] come with it. Behold, I am opening [this secret]: Abraham inherited the attribute of **Grace** <term>Chesed</term> <note>The Sefirah of Loving-kindness</note> and his attribute is called **El**. And he...

and believe that you should investigate

impure animals, birds, and fish—all of whose matters 
were commanded according to the order of the external Shells. 
For they are called "Thrones of Impurity" at the time 
when the strength of impurity is on the outside and the pure 
is on the inside. Then all things stand in their proper 
order. But if, God forbid, the order is reversed 
and the impure enter into the Courtyard, then all the pure 
exit to the outside, because both cannot enter 
into one room. And the secret is: "For the bed is too short 
for one to stretch out on, and the covering is too narrow when they wrap themselves in it" <note>Isaiah 28:20</note>. And how much 
did our sages, of blessed memory, go to great lengths to inform us 
of this verse and those like it!

<meta>This page continues the discussion on spiritual unification (Yihud), moving from the historical failings of the "Generation of the Division" to the restorative work of the Patriarchs—Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob. It specifically explains how each Patriarch corresponds to a specific Divine Name and how they repaired the spiritual "channels" damaged by the first human.</meta>

Jacob stood in the center <note>Jacob represents the "Middle Column" or Tiferet in the Sefirotic tree, balancing mercy and judgment</note> and prepared for himself a place of lodging. He took the stone that he had placed under his head, set it up as a monument, and poured oil on its top. Through this, he influenced the ten **Sefirot** <term>Divine emanations or attributes</term> and repaired the spiritual channels; they were all unified by his hand. This is as it is said: "of all that You give me, I will surely give a tenth to You" <note>Genesis 28:22</note>. He called them **Beth-El** <note>House of God</note> and the **House of Elohim** in the names of Abraham and Isaac. Then the Prophets came and called it the **House of YHVH** <note>The four-letter Name of God, often translated as Lord</note> in the name of Jacob, for Jacob is the middle line, and he inherits the name **YHVH** from every side. Regarding this, the Prophet said: "In the end of days, the mountain of the **House of YHVH** shall be established at the top of the mountains... and let us go up to the **House of the God of Jacob**" <note>Isaiah 2:2-3</note>. **Understanding** <note>The Sefirah of Binah</note> heard this. Therefore, you will find that **Sovereignty** <term>Malchut</term> is called Beth-El, the House of Elohim, and the House of YHVH, corresponding to Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob—each according to his attribute. Abraham is the attribute of **El** <note>Kindness/Chesed</note>; Isaac is the attribute of **Elohim** <note>Judgment/Gevurah</note>; and Jacob is the attribute of **YHVH** <note>Beauty/Tiferet</note>. This is the secret of "the God of Abraham, the Fear of Isaac, and the God of Jacob."

The great secret that connects all the **Sefirot** within **Sovereignty** <term>Malchut</term> is achieved through Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob. This is what the scripture says in a Psalm of Asaph: "The God of gods, the Lord <note>original: "El Elohim YHVH"</note>, has spoken and called the earth from the rising of the sun to its setting" <note>Psalm 50:1</note>. Here, in this verse, is a hint to the secret of the Patriarchs' excellence: how they joined all the Sefirot together and repaired all the channels that **Adam the First** <note>the biblical Adam</note> had corrupted. The sign is "A Psalm of Asaph"—literally "for Asaph" <note>the word 'asaph' in Hebrew also means 'to gather'</note>, as in the sign "and a pure man shall gather" <note>Numbers 19:9</note> or "Gather yourselves together <note>He'asfu</note> that I may tell you" <note>Genesis 49:1</note>. As we have already explained, "The God of gods, the Lord, has spoken and called the earth," and then the three Supernal attributes were joined, corresponding to Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob. They were the reason the earth was called "Holy Convocation" <note>Mikra Kodesh</note>... for the earth had been cast out and defiled by Adam the First. When it says "The God of gods, the Lord, has spoken," then the earth was joined and illuminated by His glory. The secret is found at the end of the verse: "from the rising of the sun to its setting." We have already informed you what "from the rising of the sun to its setting" means: it is the secret of the connection of the Sefirot and the Perfect Unification through Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob. Therefore, we say: "Remember Abraham, Isaac, and Israel Your servants..." and "the covenant which He made with Abraham, and His oath to Isaac, and He established it for Jacob as a statute, for Israel as an everlasting covenant" <note>Psalm 105:9-10</note>. Thus, it is called Beth-El, the House of Elohim, and the House of YHVH because of the "buildings" their roots constructed.

Thus, the Blessed One is unified through the Patriarchs. The channels that Adam the First corrupted and caused to be separated were grasped and reconnected by Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob. Afterward, the Psalm returns and says: "Out of Zion, the perfection of beauty, God has shined" <note>Psalm 50:2</note>. This means that after Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob repaired the channels, where is the meeting place of the Sefirot? It is in **Sovereignty** <term>Malchut</term>, for the unification is the Sefirah called **Zion**. Therefore, "Out of Zion, the perfection of beauty, God has shined." Consequently, Jacob is called the "Mighty One of Jacob," being the third of the Patriarchs. One who understands these fundamental secrets will understand the secret of "Only in your fathers did the Lord delight <note>original: "hashak," meaning a passionate desire</note> to love them"—literally a "delight"—and the secret of "And He chose their seed after them." This is the secret of the **Living God** <note>El Chai</note> and the **Shield**, which is the secret of the "Shield of David" <note>Magen David</note>, as it is said: "Your neck is like the Tower of David, built for an armory, whereon hang a thousand shields" <note>Song of Songs 4:4</note>. Therefore, observe that whenever you find the name **El Shaddai** <note>God Almighty</note>, it refers to the secret of the connection of the Sefirot—**Foundation** <term>Yesod</term> within **Sovereignty** <term>Malchut</term>—and the matter of Unification. This is the unification that the Patriarchs repaired. This is why the scripture says: "I appeared to Abraham, to Isaac, and to Jacob as **El Shaddai**, but by my name **YHVH** I was not known to them" <note>Exodus 6:3</note>.

**And know** that when the "Secret" <note>referring to the Sefirah of Yesod</note> is found with **Sovereignty** in the unification of **El Shaddai**, the name **YHVH** (which is **Beauty** <term>Tiferet</term>) is present with it. This is because the "Secret" is the tip of the lower letter *Vav* <note>The letter Vav in the Name YHVH represents the six central Sefirot, including Tiferet and Yesod</note>. The sign for this is "your father Jacob." Therefore, He appeared in **El Shaddai**, and the name **YHVH** was with him. The sign for this is "the hooks <note>Vavim</note> of the pillars and their sockets." If you should ask, "But did it not say 'I was not known to them'?" know that the name **YHVH** was visible to them through the "garment" of the name **El Shaddai**. This is the **dim lens** <note>original: "aspaklaria she-eina me'ira," a lens that does not shine; a mediated form of prophecy compared to the clear vision of Moses</note>. Therefore, they saw the King...

<meta>This page continues the exploration of how the Patriarchs—Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob—serve as the spiritual foundation for the Divine Chariot. It specifically links their actions and names to the Sefirotic structure, explaining how they draw life from the higher realms into the world.</meta>

...near to the world of life, which is the world of **Understanding** <term>Binah, the third Sefirah associated with the divine mother and the source of life-force</term>. Its sign is "The Living God" <note>original: אל חי — El Chai</note>. When the attribute of **Kindness** <note>represented by the name El</note> radiates and connects to **Sovereignty** <term>Malchut, the lowest Sefirah representing the physical world</term>, she is then called "The Sea" to merit life. This sustains the entire world with mercy, existence, and life. 

This is the secret behind the phrasing established in the prayer: "Who resurrects the dead with great mercy" <note>A line from the Amidah, the central prayer of Jewish liturgy</note>. For if Abraham <note>representing Mercy/Kindness</note> had not preceded Isaac <note>representing Judgment/Severity</note>, there could be no resurrection of the dead. Therefore, "God tested Abraham" <note>Genesis 22:1</note> and it is written: "and he took the fire and the knife in his hand" <note>Genesis 22:6</note>—within this verse, the ten **Sefirot** <term>the ten divine attributes through which God interacts with the world</term> are hinted at. 

And if you should ask: "Is it not written: 'And His name was called Wonderful, Counselor, Mighty God, Eternal Father, Prince of Peace'?" <note>Isaiah 9:5</note>. Truly, "Wonderful" <note>Pele</note> is the secret of the three upper Sefirot <note>Keter, Chochmah, and Binah</note>, which is the place of wonders. This is what the scripture means: "I praise You, for I am fearfully and wonderfully made; wonderful are Your works" <note>Psalm 139:14</note>, and "To Him who alone does great wonders" <note>Psalm 136:4</note>—meaning "Alone" in a literal sense. The Lord is exalted, and the Lord is alone.

**Behold**, I shall now explain the Psalm called the "Great Hallel" <note>Psalm 136</note>: "Give thanks," etc. "Give thanks to the Lord, for He is good," "Give thanks to the God of gods," "Give thanks to the Lord of lords." In these three verses lies the secret of the **Crown** <term>Keter</term>, **Beauty** <term>Tiferet</term>, and **Sovereignty** <term>Malchut</term>. The entire Psalm is threefold in its explanation. The Psalm returns to the first first, and to the last last. The explanation of "To Him who alone does great wonders" is the secret of "Give thanks to the Lord, for He is good," which is the secret of "Alone." 

Truly, He explained next to it: "To Him who by understanding made the heavens" <note>Psalm 136:5</note>, which is the secret of "Give thanks to the God of gods"—this is the sustaining of the Work of Creation. Next to it, he explains: "To Him who struck Egypt in their firstborn" <note>Psalm 136:10</note>, which is the secret of "Give thanks to the Lord of lords"—this is the secret of miracles and the changing of nature. 

For this reason, it says in this place, "And His name was called Wonderful." This is the secret of "Counselor"—"With whom did He take counsel, and who gave Him understanding?" <note>Isaiah 40:14</note>. And afterward, near to it is "**God**" <note>El</note>, which is the attribute of **Greatness** <term>Gedulah/Chesed</term>. Afterward, near to it is "**Mighty**" <note>Gibbor</note>, which is the attribute of **Strength** <term>Gevurah/Judgment</term>. Afterward, "**Eternal Father**" <note>Avi-Ad</note>, which ascends above and below, being **Beauty** <term>Tiferet</term> that lasts forever and ever to every side, blessed be He. And "**Prince of Peace**" <note>Sar-Shalom</note> is **Foundation** <term>Yesod</term>. One who understands understands that this refers to **Sovereignty** <term>Malchut</term>; therefore, he said near to

---

this verse: "Of the increase of his government and peace there shall be no end, upon the throne of David, and upon his kingdom, to order it, and to establish it with judgment and with justice" <note>Isaiah 9:6</note>. Therefore, open your eyes and see the wonders as we have explained regarding the "Living God" <note>El Chai</note>. You will see in every place the Torah mentions a "Sacred Watch," it alludes to a specific place and a specific attribute being exercised. This is what the Torah calls "God Almighty" <note>original: אל שדי — El Shaddai</note>.

**Behold**, I am opening for you the gates of the upper realms: Know that every place you find the name "Shaddai" in the Torah, it is the secret of **Sovereignty** <term>Malchut</term> for us. And every place you find "Shaddai," it signifies the union of **Foundation** <term>Yesod</term> and **Sovereignty** <term>Malchut</term>, at which time Sovereignty is filled with life and blessing.

**Know** that Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob—the three of them together—joined **Foundation** to **Sovereignty**, as this is the primary repair of the **Chariot** <term>Merkavah, the structure of divine forces</term> and the service of the **Divine Presence** <term>Shekhinah</term> below. Therefore, the Torah said: "I appeared to Abraham, to Isaac, and to Jacob as **God Almighty** <note>El Shaddai</note>" <note>Exodus 6:3</note>. This is because one does not enter to see the levels of prophecy except by the joining of "El Shaddai." 

Therefore, the Sages said: "The Patriarchs are themselves the Chariot." They established the "House of Rest" for **Foundation** through its union with **Sovereignty**. Abraham's name was called "Beth-El" <note>House of God</note>: "And he moved from there to the mountain on the east of Beth-El" <note>Genesis 12:8</note>. It is also said: "And he went on his journeys from the south even to Beth-El" <note>Genesis 13:3</note>. This is because Abraham moved Mercy from the **Crown** <term>Keter</term>, which is the Ancient One of the world, through the aspect of "El" which he joined with the Crown. From which side? From the "South" <note>Negev</note>, which is the **Kindness** <term>Chesed</term> of Abraham. 

Therefore, "he went on his journeys from the south even to Beth-El," for Abraham's place was the South, as it is said: "And Abraham journeyed, going on toward the South" <note>Genesis 12:9</note>. This is what is meant by "The right hand of the Lord," and from there the emanation descends to dwell in **Sovereignty**. Isaac, however, fenced all the breaches on the left side due to "Fear" <note>the attribute of Judgment</note>, and it was called "The House of Elohim" in Isaac's name. The secret is: "And he was afraid, and said: How full of awe is this place! This is none other than the house of God <note>Elohim</note>, and this is the gate of heaven" <note>Genesis 28:17</note>. "And he was afraid" is the secret of the "Fear of Isaac" <note>original: פחד יצחק — Pachad Yitzchak</note>. Thus, Isaac inherited the attribute of **Elohim**.

<summary>This page explains the Sefirotic mysteries behind Divine Names like El Shaddai and the Great Hallel (Psalm 136). It details how the Patriarchs acted as the "Divine Chariot" by unifying different spiritual attributes, specifically linking Abraham to Kindness and the South, and Isaac to Fear and Judgment.</summary>

It transitions from the previous page's focus on Isaac’s role in the Divine Chariot to the nature of impurity, the sin of the Generation of the Division (Babel), and the Sefirah of "Good" (Yesod), which connects the light of Beauty to Sovereignty.</meta>

And this is the secret of the verse: "And by your sins you defiled my land, and my inheritance you made an abomination" <note>A paraphrase of Jeremiah 2:7</note>, for "he has defiled the sanctuary of the Lord" <note>Malachi 2:11</note>. 

Therefore, the sin of the First Man <term>Adam HaRishon</term> was to utilize the sanctuary through impurity—that is to say, through evil. Similarly, the people of the Generation of the Division <term>Dor HaHaflagah, the builders of the Tower of Babel</term> desired this. Therefore, they were exiled. Regarding them, the sages of blessed memory said: "Which the children of men built" <note>Genesis 11:5</note>—these were the "children of the First Man" <note>original: בנוי דאדם קדמאה — bunai d'Adam Kadma'ah</note>. Their sin was similar to the sin of their father: they sought to separate the "stream of God full of water" <note>Psalm 65:10</note> from the "well of living waters" <note>Song of Songs 4:15</note> which are filled from that very stream. They wanted to fill the well with "insolent waters" <note>Psalm 124:5</note>, the "bitter waters" <note>Numbers 5:18</note>, and they sought to make use of it. 

This is what the verse means: "To make cakes for the Queen of Heaven" <note>Jeremiah 7:18</note>. And from the time "we ceased to burn incense to the Queen of Heaven, we have lacked everything" <note>Jeremiah 44:18</note>. This was the primary iniquity during the time of the Kings of Israel; even in the time of King Manasseh, they were separating the divine flow from the "Stream" <term>Peleg, associated here with the Sefirah of Yesod</term> and they wanted to utilize it through impurity. This is what the scripture means: "He shall not see the streams, the rivers, the brooks of honey and butter" <note>Job 20:17</note>. He who looks only at the "divisions" <note>original: פלגות — palgot, a pun on the "Stream" (Peleg) and the "Division" (Haflagah)</note> does not see the rivers of honey and butter. 

Abraham our father, peace be upon him, held in his hands all the powers of impurity and purity. Purity was within, and impurity was without. He handed over the powers of purity to Isaac, as it is said: "And the Lord blessed Abraham with everything" <note>Genesis 24:1</note> and "Abraham gave all that he had to Isaac" <note>Genesis 25:5</note>. As for the powers of external impurity, he handed them over to the sons of the concubines. This is the meaning of: "But to the sons of the concubines whom Abraham had, Abraham gave gifts" <note>Genesis 25:6</note>. "And he sent them away from Isaac his son, while he was still living, eastward to the land of the East." Here, Abraham our father understood that the pure Chariots were within—for Isaac, the son of Sarah—and the impure ones were without—for the sons of the concubines. 

This is what was said regarding King Solomon, peace be upon him: "And Solomon's wisdom excelled the wisdom of all the children of the East" <note>1 Kings 5:10</note>. For the "children of the East" were the descendants of the concubines to whom their father had given "gifts," which refers to the "Names of Impurity" <note>The occult or magical names used in dark arts</note>. As our Sages of blessed memory said, in this way they would worship idols during the time the Holy Temple stood, performing deeds and making use of the Names of Impurity. The Generation of the Division wanted to prepare the "Kingdoms" <term>the lower spiritual realms</term> to be a throne for the powers of impurity, similar to the matter of the Tree of Knowledge of 

---

Good and Evil. And they said: "Let us make ourselves a name" <note>Genesis 11:4</note>—a literal "Name." This was the sin of the First Man. Therefore, the Blessed Name said: "The earth was divided" and "now nothing will be withheld from them" <note>Genesis 10:25, 11:6</note>. Thus, Eber named his son Peleg, because in his days the earth was "divided" <note>original: נפלגה — niflegah</note>. They sought to separate the source of living waters called the "Stream of God full of water." Therefore, "men of blood and treachery" were divided from them; "they shall not live out half their days" <note>Psalm 55:24</note>. Behold, the matter of the "Stream" <term>Peleg</term> is explained to you. This attribute is called in the Torah:

### Good
Because it is the ninth of the radiating **Sefirot** <term>the ten divine attributes</term>. It is the secret of the "tip of the lower *Vav*" <note>The letter Vav (ו) often represents the central column of the Sefirot; its "tip" connects it to the realm below</note> and the letter *Beit* <note>ב</note>, which is the secret of **Sovereignty** <term>Malchut</term> which is adjacent to it. Its sign is: "**B**erashit **B**ara Elohim" <note>Genesis 1:1, beginning with the letter Beit</note>, "the Great House" <note>Beit Rabta</note>, "The House of God" <note>Beit El</note>, "The House of the Lord."

### And Know
That all the light created in the Work of Creation is sealed within this attribute. This is the light that flows from **Beauty** <term>Tiferet, the sixth Sefirah</term>, as in the matter of: "And God said, let there be light." It is written: "And God saw the light, that it was **good**; and God divided..." <note>Genesis 1:4</note>. He hid it for the righteous. He truly hid it within **Sovereignty** <term>Malchut</term>—"Light is sown for the righteous" <note>Psalm 97:11</note>. The "Righteous One" <term>Tzaddik, associated with Yesod</term> and "Justice" <term>Tzedek, associated with Malchut</term> join together in this "sown light." 

It is also written: "And from the wicked their light is withheld" <note>Job 38:15</note>, for this light shines only within **Sovereignty**. All the external forces that are outside of Sovereignty do not see and do not benefit from this light, because it is hidden within Sovereignty. And Sovereignty was given to Israel alone; the nations of the world have no portion in it. Therefore: "And God saw the light, that it was good; and God divided between the light and the darkness." The light is for Israel, and the darkness is for the nations of the world. So it says: "For behold, darkness shall cover the earth, and thick darkness the peoples; but the Lord shall shine upon you, and His glory shall be seen upon you" <note>Isaiah 60:2</note>. 

This is the secret of the **Havdalah** <term>the ceremony marking the end of the Sabbath, meaning "Separation"</term>: "Who separates between holy and profane, between light and darkness, between Israel and the nations, between the seventh day and the six days of work." When you understand the secret of the entire order of Havdalah, you will see that "Holy," "Light," "Israel," and "The Seventh Day" are all one concept. Then you will understand "And God saw the light that it was good," and so on.

### And Behold
On the second day, which is the secret of the "Separation" <term>Havdalah</term>, the Gates of Justice...

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the "Generation of the Division" (the builders of the Tower of Babel) and their attempt to manipulate divine forces. It focuses on the distinction between the "Source of Living Water" (holy emanation) and the "Broken Cisterns" (impure forces). It also explains how the different names of God serve as "garments" or portals for understanding the hidden depths of the Torah.</meta>

<margin>
The explanation: And these are the wells outside the world of life, and all of them descend to the pit of destruction and to the place of tumult <note>original: "sha'on," a reference to the roar of the abyss</note>. Regarding them it is said: "He brought me up out of the tumultuous pit, out of the miry clay" <note>Psalm 40:3</note>. And "But You, O God, shall bring them down into the pit of destruction" <note>Psalm 55:24</note>. All of these are from the husks <term>Kelipot; the spiritual "shells" that conceal divine light</term> of the uncircumcision <note>referring to the "Orlah," a state of impurity</note> which are outside the partition of the Temple. The sign for this is: "This is Jerusalem; I have set her in the midst of the nations, and the countries are round about her" <note>Ezekiel 5:5</note>.

**And know** that when Israel sins and acts like those who defile the Sanctuary, they stir up mud and mire, and the sources of life cease and are withdrawn. This is the secret of "a troubled fountain" <note>Proverbs 25:26</note>. And the source is destroyed when the "Righteous One" <term>Tzadik; here referring to the Sefirah of Yesod, the conduit of divine flow</term> falters before the wicked. It is written: "The righteous is gathered up because of the evil" <note>Isaiah 57:1</note>. Know that from the time the Sanctuary was defiled and the House was destroyed, the conduits <note>the spiritual channels of blessing</note> truly ceased, and the fountains were stopped up until the Kingdom <term>Malkhut</term> returns to be reunited with the "Righteous One." For since the Kingdom of Wickedness spread through the world, the "Righteous One" was withdrawn, as it says, "the righteous is gathered up because of the evil." And when the wickedness is finished—"it shall vanish like smoke"—and He removes the dominion of sinners from the earth, then on that day "living waters shall go out from Jerusalem" <note>Zechariah 14:8</note>. And a fountain shall come forth from the House of the Lord <note>original uses the Tetragrammaton abbreviated as יהו"ד</note>. Then "the Lord shall be King over all the earth; in that day shall the Lord be one, and His name one" <note>Zechariah 14:9</note>. This is through the fountain that goes out from Jerusalem, which is where the "Source of Living Waters" connects. This is called the "Stream of God" <term>Peleg Elohim</term>, as the verse says: "The stream of God is full of water" <note>Psalm 65:10</note>. The meaning of the verse is thus: the visitation of the water and the land and its longings shall do much; the stream of God is full of water. And "thou shalt visit thy habitation and shalt not sin" <note>Job 5:24</note> and "the Lord visited Sarah as He had said" <note>Genesis 21:1</note>. 

In this place <note>the spiritual level of Malkhut</note>, the Generation of the Division sought to create a separation, saying "let us make a name for ourselves" <note>Genesis 11:4</note>. They wanted to separate the Foundation <term>Yesod</term> from the Sovereignty <term>Malkhut</term>. They wanted to utilize the Sovereignty through the forces of impurity, via the filth that the well-known Serpent cast into the Sovereignty through the First Man <term>Adam HaRishon</term>, in the secret of the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil. Because the Sanctuary is designated for purity, and afterward the impure entered it, they defiled the Sanctuary. 
And this is...
</margin>

The King in His garment. He does not appear to them except through a garment, and they are not able to understand the reality of His actual self because the garment intervenes. Therefore, "I was not known to them" <note>Exodus 6:3</note>—meaning I was not known in My actual essence to them, because the partitions of the garment intervene. This is the meaning of "In a vision I make myself known to him" <note>Numbers 12:6</note>. But Moses, our teacher <note>original: משה רע"ה — Moshe Rabbenu, Alav HaShalom</note>, removed the garment, as in "I have put off my coat" <note>Song of Songs 5:3</note>. He saw the "back," but he did not see the "face" <note>Exodus 33:23</note>. 

Therefore, take care in every place where you find the name *Shaddai* <note>Almighty</note> in the Torah, or *El Shaddai*, and you will be able to establish the path of every matter upon its proper base, and you will understand great wonders. For from the specific Name mentioned in each matter among the matters of the Torah, you can understand the subject of the section or the verse—to which [spiritual] place it alludes. Then many gates will open before you, and you will be able to enter the Palace of the King. You will see hidden and concealed wonders made clear before your eyes. Understand very deeply all that we have explained, and then you shall make your way prosperous, and then you shall have good success. This attribute is called:

->**Source of Life** and the reason is:<-

Because all the rivers that flow from the "broad places of the river" <note>Rechovot HaNahar</note> which go out from Eden, all of them gather in this place. It is the source that flows through the seventy-two bridges <note>spiritual paths related to the 72-letter Name of God</note> by way of Kindness <term>Chesed</term>. It is that which fills the attribute of Sovereignty <term>Malkhut</term>. Therefore, it is called "Source of Living Water" and it is a "fountain of gardens" <note>Song of Songs 4:15</note>, because the Source of Living Water pours into it every kind of blessing and the flow of life. 

This is what the verse means: "They have forsaken Me, the fountain of living waters, to hew them out cisterns, broken cisterns, that can hold no water" <note>Jeremiah 2:13</note>. Those cisterns are external <note>forces of the "Other Side" or evil</note>; they have no portion in the "Well of Seven" <note>Be'er Sheba</note>, which is the Well of Living Water. Rather, those cisterns are external; they are like those "broken ones which cannot hold the water." Regarding them it is said: "For a harlot is a deep ditch, and a strange woman is a narrow pit" <note>Proverbs 23:27</note>. And "let not the pit shut her mouth upon me" <note>Psalm 69:16</note>.

It interprets the sin of the Tower of Babel as an attempt to separate the divine attributes of Yesod and Malkhut to harness power through the "Serpent's filth."</summary>

--- Page 56 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of the divine conduits and the relationship between the Sefirot. It transitions from the imagery of the "troubled fountain" and the "Righteous One" being withdrawn, into a detailed explanation of the divine names "Tzur" (Rock), "Abir" (Mighty One), and "Tzadik" (Righteous). It connects these attributes to the Patriarchs—Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob—and specifically to King David, explaining how the earthly act of charity (Tzedakah) mirrors and activates the union of the divine realms.</meta>

Through the power of the **Supernal Rock**, He brought forth water from the flinty rock <note>Hebrew: *Tzur Ha-Halamish*</note> to become a pool of water, through the mystery of the "transformation of the rock" into a pool of water by the power of the Supernal Rock. Know that all the generations of Israel emerged from the **Rock**, as the verse says: "The Rock that begot you, you forgot" <note>Deuteronomy 32:18</note>. And it also says: "There is no Rock like our God" <note>1 Samuel 2:2</note>—read it as "there is no *fashioner* <note>original: *tzir*, a wordplay on *tzur* (rock) and *tzayar* (artist/fashioner)</note> like our God."

## And Here We Are
to explain the verse "The Rock, His work is perfect" <note>Deuteronomy 32:4</note>. When this attribute is united with the **Kingdom** <term>Malkhut; the tenth Sefirah, representing the manifest presence of God</term>, it then becomes "perfect" for us. This is the meaning of "You shall be perfect with the Lord your God" <note>Deuteronomy 18:13</note> and "Walk before Me and be perfect." Therefore, King David—peace be upon him—said: "And I will be perfect with Him, and I will keep myself from my iniquity" <note>Psalm 18:24</note>. Consequently, "The Rock, His work is perfect." 

Now, open your eyes and see that it is written: "The way of a snake upon a rock" <note>Proverbs 30:19</note>. This refers to David, who was "ruddy, with beautiful eyes and goodly to look upon" <note>1 Samuel 16:12</note>. This is why it says, "These are the generations of Perez" <note>Ruth 4:18; Perez is the ancestor of David</note>; for "he who breaks <note>original: *poretz*, a wordplay on the name Perez</note> a fence, a snake shall bite him" <note>Ecclesiastes 10:8</note>. Thus, "These are the generations of Perez," who deals with the "crooked snake." When the **Rock** was blemished regarding the matter of the snake—which is the "crooked serpent"—the verse concludes: "For all His ways are justice." Know that this attribute is called:

## Rock
from the side of **Might** <term>Gevurah; the Sefirah of judgment and restraint</term>, yet it draws its influence from **Greatness** <term>Gedulah/Chesed; the Sefirah of mercy and expansion</term>. This is the secret of the <gloss>interlinear: ...looking toward the...</gloss> verse: "Look to the **rock** from which you were hewn, and to the hole of the pit from which you were digged. Look to Abraham your father, and to Sarah who bore you" <note>Isaiah 51:1-2</note>. It also says: "The Rock that begot you, you forgot, and you forgot the God who brought you forth." 

One who knows these secrets can understand the promise "So shall your seed be" <note>Genesis 15:5</note>; for Abraham's seed does not function according to the system of the stars and the constellations, but only through miracles and wonders. This is the secret of "And upon the likeness of the throne was a likeness like the appearance of a man <note>original: *Adam*</note>" <note>Ezekiel 1:26</note>. When David saw the likeness of the "External Thrones"—the shells <term>Kelipot; forces of impurity</term> that are also called "Man" (*Adam*)—and the "Internal Throne" which is the Name of the Lord <note>original: *Adonai*</note>, David said: "The Lord is for me, I will not fear; what can *man* (*Adam*) do to me?" <note>Psalm 118:6</note>. This attribute is also called:

## Mighty One
of Jacob, the "Mighty One of Israel" <note>original: *Abir Yaakov*, *Abir Yisrael*</note>. This is because the attribute of **Foundation** <term>Yesod; the Sefirah that acts as the conduit for divine flow</term> is dependent on Jacob, who represents the **Middle Pillar**. It possesses six names: three above and three below.

**The ones above:**
Greatness (*Gedulah*), Might (*Gevurah*), Beauty (*Tiferet*)

**The ones below:**
Victory (*Netzach*), Splendor (*Hod*), Foundation (*Yesod*)

This is why **Foundation** is called the "Mighty One of Jacob," and not the "Mighty One of Abraham" or the "Mighty One of Isaac." Therefore, contemplate this great principle: "Thus says the Lord, the Lord of Hosts, the Mighty One of Israel: Ah, I will ease Me of My adversaries, and avenge Me of My enemies" <note>Isaiah 1:24</note>. For David is the master of wars and he is the "Throne" for the Lord of Hosts—this is the secret of "Lord" <note>original: *Adon*</note>. Jacob, however, ascended as far as **Understanding** <term>Binah; the third Sefirah, the source of divine intuition</term>, for he "rides upon the heights" of Abraham and Isaac, as we have already explained. 

When the "Mighty One of Jacob" draws from the **Understanding** above, then his spark "blossoms and its flower shoots forth" <note>referring to Numbers 17:23</note>, and the "Eagle" <note>a symbol for the Sefirah of Tiferet/Jacob</note> is filled with plumage. Thus it says: "Does the hawk fly by your wisdom <note>original: *Binah*</note>, and stretch her wings toward the south?" <note>Job 39:26</note>. Understand these supernal wonders! "He spreads his wings toward the south" because the "Mercies of David" <note>original: *Chasdei David*</note> draw through Jacob and Abraham, who is associated with the **South** <note>the right side/mercy</note>. This is what the scripture means by "Shield of Abraham" and "Shield of David," or "Mercy to Abraham" and the "Mercies of David." 

Therefore, "He spreads his wings toward the south"—toward the right side. This occurs when he comes to embrace the **Queen** <term>Malkhut</term>: "His left hand is under my head, and his right hand embraces me" <note>Song of Songs 2:6</note>. "God comes from the South <note>original: *Teiman*</note>... and His glory fills the earth" <note>Habakkuk 3:3</note>. His glory <note>or "praise"</note> indeed! From **Righteousness** <note>original: *Tzedek*</note> to **Charity** <note>original: *Tzedakah*</note>, the Lord God of Judah. Now, open your eyes and understand the wonders of how the Torah is woven within the wisdom of the Blessed Name. This attribute is called:

## Righteous One
meaning that it is a "Wonder" and the "Scales of Judgment." Through it, all the commandments and the worlds are sustained in perfect unity. It provides for everything by means of the attribute of the **Kingdom** (*Malkhut*). It stands in the center—above, below, and to the sides. 

Behold, this attribute is called **Righteous One** <term>Tzadik</term> and the attribute of the Kingdom is called **Righteousness** <term>Tzedek</term>. When the flow of the *Righteous One* is poured into *Righteousness*, that flow is called **Charity** <term>Tzedakah</term>. This is like someone giving a penny to a poor person; that gift is called *Tzedakah*. Thus, the one who gives charity awakens the "Righteous One" to unite with the attribute of "Righteousness," bringing peace between them and binding the Sefirot as one, thereby bringing peace to the world. This is the secret of the verse: "And the work of charity <note>Tzedakah</note> shall be peace" <note>Isaiah 32:17</note>.

**Gates of Justice, Chapter 8, Section 20, and understand.**

<summary>This page explains the kabbalistic significance of the divine names Tzur, Abir, and Tzadik, detailing how the Sefirah of Yesod (the Righteous One) acts as a conduit that unites the divine realms and brings peace to the world through the act of charity.</summary>
<keywords>Tzur, Abir, Tzadik, Sefirot, Malkhut, Binah, Mercies of David, Jacob, Abraham, Tzedakah, Peace, Gates of Justice, Sha'arei Tzedek</keywords>

--- Page 57 ---
<meta>This page continues the exposition on the Sefirot, specifically focusing on the mystery of the "Rock" (Tzur) and its relationship to the attribute of "Good" and the Sefirah of "Foundation" (Yesod). It follows the previous page's discussion of King David's spiritual attributes and the concept of divine "perfection."</meta>

It was not said "that it was good" <note>Referring to the second day of Creation in Genesis 1</note> because on that day Gehenna <note>Hell or the realm of purgation</note> was created. This means that all the forces of impurity of various kinds were suspended within it, and permission was given to the Destroyer to cause harm. Therefore, "that it was good" was not said of it. This is the secret of the "separation" on the second day; for when the upper and lower realms are united, everything is indeed good. One who understands this secret will understand why "It is not good for man to be alone" <note>Genesis 2:18</note> and how "a whisperer separates close friends" <note>Proverbs 16:28; here interpreted as separating the divine emanations</note>. Conversely, it is written: "Whoso findeth a wife findeth a good thing" <note>Proverbs 18:22</note>.

**Know** that all the works of Creation are truly sealed with the attribute of **Good** <term>Tuv; often associated with the Sefirah of Yesod</term>, which is its sustenance, its power, and its unique virtue. From it, goodness, abundance, sustenance, and livelihood flow to the **Kingdom** <term>Malkhut</term>, and from there all creatures of every kind are nourished. If, Heaven forbid, human beings should sin and this "Good" is withdrawn—meaning the **Righteous One** <term>Tzadik; another name for Yesod</term> is gathered away—then it is written: "Your sins have withholden good things from you" <note>Jeremiah 5:25</note>. 

Because this was the attribute of David in this place, it was said of him: "He was ruddy, with beautiful eyes, and goodly to look upon" <note>1 Samuel 16:12</note>. The meaning of "ruddy" <note>Hebrew: *admoni*</note> is that his attribute was comprised of both Judgment and Mercy. From the side of Judgment, the Prince of Edom <note>The guardian angel of Esau/Rome, representing the force of harsh Judgment</note> laid a hand upon him—"and the hollow of Jacob's thigh was strained" <note>Genesis 32:25</note>. Therefore, "These are the generations of Perez" <note>Ruth 4:18, the ancestor of David</note> follows the secret of "A hand upon the throne of the Lord" <note>Exodus 17:16</note>. Esau is Edom, and of David it is said "he was ruddy" (*admoni*); and of Esau it was said, "And the first came out ruddy" <note>Genesis 25:25</note>, until the time came when Zerah <note>Meaning "shining" or "rising"</note> emerged—"And the glory of the Lord shall rise (*yizrakh*) upon thee" <note>Isaiah 60:1</note>. 

This is the Source and the **Foundation** <term>Yesod</term> called **Life of the Worlds** <term>Chay Olamim</term>, because it draws life from **Understanding** <term>Binah; the third Sefirah</term> through the mystery of the "Seventh," and extends it into the Kingdom through the mystery of "Bathsheba" <note>Literally "Daughter of Seven," representing Malkhut</note>. It is called "Life of the Worlds" from world unto world. We have already explained above that it is called the "Living God" <note>original: *El Chai*</note>. Because it is the secret upon which the **Oath** <term>Shevuah; related to the word for "seven" (sheva)</term> depends, it is the "Life of the Worlds." Behold, the secret of the oath is suspended from it; this is the mystery of the phrases "As the Lord lives" <note>original: *Chay Y-H-V-H*</note> and "As your soul lives." 

Truly, we must arrange these into two signs held within this verse: the first is "As the Lord lives" and the second is "As your soul lives." **And here I am** explaining that "As the Lord lives" implies He has no life other than Himself—meaning, aside from the essence of the Sefirot, which are all a single, equal unity. "As your soul lives" refers to the life of the soul; for all souls depend upon Him, as do all other existing things. He is the power and the sustenance in the "Life of the World." He is truly the life of the world, for when He draws life from Understanding into the Kingdom, then all existing things are satisfied with life, blessing, and abundance from the Kingdom. 

This is like a pool that becomes filled with water, from which all the garden beds and all types of seeds and plants are watered. This is the secret of "and swore by the Life of the World" <note>Daniel 12:7</note>. Note that it is not called "Life of the *Worlds*" there, but "Life of the *World*"; understand this well.

**Know** and believe that one who denies an oath denies this attribute, and takes his own life from the world. From this, understand the secret of one who violates an oath: he has no root, sustenance, or standing in the realm of the world, for all seven days of Creation are withdrawn from him. By denying the oath, no existence remains for him in the place of the world; one who denies an oath is uprooted by the "Seventh" <note>Malkhut/The Seventh Day</note>. He has no place in the world to stand or lean upon, and he has no life, nourishment, or sustenance in any matter. Therefore, "he swore by the Life of the World." Understand this very well.

From this place we constantly draw life, as it says: "For with You is the fountain of life" <note>Psalm 36:9</note>, and "They have forsaken Me, the fountain of living waters" <note>Jeremiah 2:13</note>. In this place we ask on Rosh Hashanah: "Remember us for life," for this is the place of **Remembrance** <term>Zichron; a name for Yesod</term>. And we say: "Write us in the Book of Life"—meaning in His book, which is the Kingdom, as it says: "Everyone that is written for life in Jerusalem" <note>Isaiah 4:3</note>. 

This is the Sefirah called **Fashioner of Creation** <term>Yotzer Bereshit</term>, for this is the attribute that shapes all forms within the attribute of the Kingdom. This is the secret of "There is no rock (*tzur*) like our God" <note>1 Samuel 2:2</note>, which can be read as "there is no fashioner (*tzayar*) like our God." I will hint this to you: "And I will put thee in a cleft of the rock (*tzur*) in Horeb" <note>Exodus 33:22</note>. The Rock is called the secret of the Kingdom. Therefore, "And thou shalt see My back" <note>Exodus 33:23</note>; however, he did not place him in the Rock itself. This is the secret of "Behold, I will stand before thee there upon the rock (*tzur*) in Horeb; and thou shalt strike the rock..." <note>Exodus 17:6</note>—referring to the thing called Rock...

<note>original catchword: *ve-nabah* (and it shall flow/gush)</note>

<summary>This page explores the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation) as the source of "Good" and "Life," explaining its role in nourishing the Kingdom (Malkhut). It connects biblical imagery of the "Rock," the "Oath," and King David to the flow of divine abundance and warns of the spiritual consequences of violating oaths.</summary>
