<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation), which was introduced on the previous page as the attribute of "Good." The author now expands on how this attribute connects the celestial heights to the earthly community of Israel.</meta>

## Know
That according to their spiritual levels, the people of Israel are categorized: some are **Righteous**, some are **Elders**, some are **Pious**, and some are **Holy**. Each individual is linked to the specific Supernal <note>Upper or Divine</note> place to which they are attached—understand this well. And know that the attribute of the **Righteous One** <term>Tzadik; the Sefirah of Yesod</term> is that which causes goodness to flow upon the "known Land" <note>Referring to the Sefirah of Malkhut, or Kingdom</note>, and likewise here below. As it is written: *And your people shall all be righteous; they shall inherit the land forever, the branch of My planting, the work of My hands, that I may be glorified* <note>Isaiah 60:21</note>. Contemplate with full understanding the matter of the "branch of My planting," the "work of My hands," and what it means "to be glorified." Consider how beautiful a tree is when its branches are many, spreading its boughs and shoots in every direction. This is the secret of the ornaments called **Glory** <note>original: "Pe'er"</note>, as in: *As a bridegroom decks himself with a turban/glory* <note>Isaiah 61:10</note>, and *You shall make holy garments for Aaron your brother, for honor and for glory/beauty* <note>Exodus 28:2</note>. Understand what we have said in these places; they are all great principles through which one may enter the many chambers of the Torah, which contain many treasures. And this attribute is called:

## Foundation
<note>original: "Yesod"</note>
I must inform you of a great and fundamental principle. Know that there is a **foundation** that is at the bottom of a building, and the building stands upon it; this is the nature of a foundation in this lower, physical world. However, there is also a **foundation** that is in the center, and the building stands around it; this is the nature of the foundations of the heavenly buildings. Since the heavens are circular, every sphere <note>or "wheel" of the cosmos</note> has its foundation in its center, and by means of its center, every sphere revolves and moves. 

Now, if you should say that all the foundations of the lower world are "laborious" <note>active/straining</note>, the truth is the opposite: the buildings of the lower world are quiet and at rest, they do not move. But the heavenly spheres revolve and move constantly. Therefore, the lower things are called "dead," while the heavens and what is within them are called "living." There is also a **foundation** that is above, yet also within the lower buildings; this is the secret of the world of the Angels who are below, yet who yearn and long to ascend and behold the face of the Blessed Lord. In the foundations of the elements: **Water** flows downward, **Air** surrounds, and **Fire** naturally ascends upward—these are the likenesses of the three worlds. After you have known this:

## Know
That the Name <note>God</note> bears all; He is above, and His "burden" <note>Creation</note> is below Him. Thus it says: *Thus says the Lord, High and Lofty* <note>Isaiah 57:15</note>. Once you know this, know that the Sefirah called **Foundation** <term>Yesod</term> is that which gives life and blessing to the **Kingdom** <term>Malkhut</term>, for Foundation is above it. The Sefirah of Kingdom constantly yearns and longs to ascend and be united with the attribute of Foundation. Thus, the attribute of Kingdom is dependent upon the attribute of the **Righteous One, the Foundation of the World** <note>original: "Tzadik Yesod Olam"</note>, just as a building relies on its foundation. 

If, Heaven forbid, the Foundation is lacking, then everything is destroyed. When the attribute of Foundation is withdrawn from the **Assembly of Israel** <term>Knesset Yisrael; another name for Malkhut</term>, the result is the destruction of the world. One who understands this secret will understand what was said: *Remember, O Lord, against the children of Edom the day of Jerusalem; who said: "Raze it, raze it, even to the foundation thereof"* <note>Psalm 137:7; "Raze it" in Hebrew is 'Aru', which also implies uncovering or exposing the foundation</note>. Likewise, it says: *The righteous is gathered away because of the evil* <note>Isaiah 57:1</note>. Contemplate this well and you will see the meaning of *The righteous is the foundation of the world* <note>Proverbs 10:25</note>; for when the "Righteous One" <note>Yesod</note> is withdrawn from "Righteousness" <note>Tzedek, a name for Malkhut</note>, it is then written: *The crown is fallen from our head* <note>Lamentations 5:16</note>, for the Foundation is missing. 

Similarly, it says: *The virgin of Israel is fallen; she shall no more rise* <note>Amos 5:2</note>. Contemplate well the secret of: *Beautiful in elevation, the joy of the whole earth, is Mount Zion, on the sides of the north, the city of the great King* <note>Psalm 48:3</note>, and what is said: *In that day I will raise up the tabernacle of David that is fallen* <note>Amos 9:11</note>, and what the scripture says: *Shake yourself from the dust; arise, and sit down, O Jerusalem* <note>Isaiah 52:2</note>. One who understands this will understand what was said: *How hath the Lord covered [Zion] with a cloud... and cast down from heaven unto the earth the beauty of Israel* <note>Lamentations 2:1</note>. He who understands the construction of the Foundation will understand the matter of the "Fall." However, when the **Unification** <term>Yichud</term> returns to its place, the building shall return in its restoration. This is what is meant by the prayer: "He who restores His Divine Presence to Zion." And so it says: *Out of Zion, the perfection of beauty, God hath shined forth* <note>Psalm 50:2</note> and *Blessed be the Lord out of Zion* <note>Psalm 135:21</note>. For truly, He shines forth from **Zion** <note>Yesod</note> to dwell in **Jerusalem** <note>Malkhut</note>. Because Zion and Jerusalem are Foundation and Kingdom; if there is no Zion, there is no Divine Presence in Jerusalem. Understand this very well. And this attribute is called:

## All
<note>original: "Kol"</note>
The reason for this name is that all types of flow, emanation, blessing, and life are drawn from the **Highest Crown** <term>Keter Elyon</term> through all the "channels," all the "chambers," and all the "supernal treasures." They are all drawn from every side and they all unite as one in the attribute of the **Righteous One, the Foundation.**

<summary>This page explains the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation) as the central channel for divine abundance, using the metaphors of a building's foundation and the celestial axis. It highlights the vital union between Zion (Yesod) and Jerusalem (Malkhut), warning that the withdrawal of "the Righteous One" leads to spiritual and physical destruction.</summary>
<keywords>Yesod, Foundation, Malkhut, Kingdom, Tzadik, Zion, Jerusalem, Sefirot, Divine Flow, Keter, Crown, Unity</keywords>

--- Page 59 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation), specifically focusing on how the actions of human beings—particularly acts of charity and the observance of commandments—directly affect the flow of divine energy between the upper realms and our world. It transitions from defining "Foundation" to explaining the mystical mechanics of "Righteousness."</meta>

### Understand 
<note>original: "Ha-ben"</note> 
I am hinting at a great secret; know it for yourselves: 
"Sow for yourselves for righteousness (*Tzedek*), and reap according to kindness (*Hesed*)" <note>Hosea 10:12</note>. The **Seed** is 
literal, and the **Kindness** is literal. And I am hinting: "Righteousness 
saves from death" <note>Proverbs 10:2</note>. When human beings sin, they separate 
the **Master** <note>original: "Aluph"; a term for the Divine or the connecting principle</note> and the Land remains dry because there is no water 
in the heavens. Then, the judgments of the **Kingdom** <note>the Sefirah of Malkhut</note> descend [bitterly]; 
they take hold even unto the depths of the grave. Anyone who encounters 
her judgment dies without mercy or compassion—woe to that 
hour! But anyone who causes the **Righteous One** <term>Tzadik; the Sefirah of Yesod</term> 
to connect to **Righteousness** <term>Tzedek; the Sefirah of Malkhut</term> causes the rains of blessing to descend to the earth. 
Life is found there. And this is accomplished by one who establishes **charity** 
for the poor, thereby causing "Righteousness" to be enacted above. This is 
the secret of "Righteousness literally saves from death"—for it fills 
the "Righteousness" <note>the Divine Feminine/Malkhut</note> through the union made with the Kingdom; 
otherwise, anyone who encounters her judgment would die. Open your eyes and see 
wonders! Therefore it is said: "For by righteousness shall his throne be established" <note>Proverbs 16:12</note>, 
"And her captives [redeemed] with righteousness" <note>Isaiah 1:27</note>, "In righteousness shall you be established" <note>Isaiah 54:14</note>. "Keep 
justice and do righteousness, for My salvation is near to come" <note>Isaiah 56:1</note>. 
One who understands these matters will understand that the commandment of **Charity** 
is the secret of the connection of the *Sefirot* and their unity. It brings 
peace between the "Righteous One" and "Righteousness." This is a thing that is 
literally visible to the eye. When you study these three names 
that correspond to one another—*Tzedek* (Justice), *Tzedakah* (Righteousness/Charity)—they join 
the "Tent" <note>the Divine Tabernacle</note> to become one. "Righteousness exalts a nation" <note>Proverbs 14:34</note>. 
If so, contemplate the heights of Charity/Righteousness in the context 
of her being the connection between the "Righteous One" and "Righteousness." It 
is she who brings peace between the upper worlds and 
the lower worlds. She brings blessing, abundance, and emanation 
to the world and causes the Unity of Peace. Because of this, 
a person must prepare themselves for every single commandment 
of the Torah, for how many worlds do they sustain 
by their performance! And how many worlds does one destroy 
by neglecting them, and certainly by committing a transgression. This is 
the secret of: "Why is the land ruined and laid waste like a wilderness... 
And the Lord said: Because they have forsaken My Torah" <note>Jeremiah 9:11-12</note>. 
One who understands this secret can understand the secret of "Bring the 
tithes into the storehouse, that there may be food in My house, 
and test Me now in this... if I will not open for you 
the windows of heaven and pour out for you a blessing until [there is no more room]" <note>Malachi 3:10</note>. 

---

And understand what they [the Sages] said regarding the sin of neglecting the *Challah* <note>the dough offering</note>, 
the sin of neglecting tithes and the Sabbatical year of the land, 
and the other commandments. Do not let it enter your mind that these 
are merely matter of punishments where they punish those who neglect 
or transgress them. It is not so. Rather, for anyone 
who neglects a commandment of the commandments, the "Circuit" 
that would have flowed by reason of its performance—such as Charity—
is withheld. It is like one who does not sow his field: he does 
not reap. Or like one who does not wear [clothes]: 
his body becomes cold. For the "punishments" of the commandments are 
actually a natural thing. Just as it is the nature of fire to heat, 
and the nature of water to cool and moisten, and the nature of bread 
to satisfy the hungry, and the nature of water to quench 
the thirsty—so too is it in the nature of every single commandment to bring up 
that Good which is stated regarding it, or those 
punishments which are stated regarding their neglect. This is the 
matter regarding Charity, and likewise regarding transgressions. 

### And Know 
That there are transgressions that touch the **Inner Palace** <note>original: "Heikhal Penimi"</note>, 
such as sexual immorality and the desecration of the Sabbath 
and others like them. And there are transgressions that touch 
outside the Palace, such as the eating of swarming things and crawlers, 
beasts, wild animals, birds, and impure fish. One who transgresses 
in these spoils the "Rows," and breaks the 
"Channels" <note>original: "Tzinorot"; the conduits of divine light</note>, and brings an "Image" <note>an idol</note> into the Palace. The sign 
of this is: "But from the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil, you shall not eat of it" <note>Genesis 2:17</note>. 

### And Know 
That because of this, the **Righteous** are called "Righteous" (*Tzadikim*) 
because they maintain all things 
that are internal in their place within, and things that are external 
without. Nothing leaves its proper boundary. In 
this way, they are called Righteous. One who understands what 
we explained above will understand what is said: "The Righteous 
is the foundation of the world" <note>Proverbs 10:25</note>. That is to say, the attribute of the "Righteous One" is the foundation 
of the world, both upper and lower, for it joins the "Tent" 
to be one, and the upper realms are joined and unified 
with one another. Then the plantings of the Garden are blessed, 
and then all things are in perfection. Through the Righteous 
below, they maintain every single thing in its proper place, 
and every thing is found within its boundary and domain—the internal 
within, and the external without. 

<summary>This page explains that the commandments, especially charity (Tzedakah), act as a natural mechanism to unify the Sefirot of Yesod and Malkhut, ensuring the flow of divine blessing. Transgressions are described not as judicial punishments, but as natural consequences that disrupt these spiritual "conduits" and create separation between the worlds.</summary>
<keywords>Tzedakah, Tzadik, Yesod, Malkhut, Sefirot, spiritual conduits, Challah, tithes, Shmitah, Inner Palace, divine unity, natural law</keywords>

--- Page 60 ---
<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation) as the conduit for divine blessing. The previous page established that human acts of righteousness (Tzedek) allow the "Righteous One" (Yesod) to channel life-giving "water" to the world. This page specifically links the physical act of circumcision to the spiritual "opening" of these conduits and the ultimate redemption of Israel.</meta>

He found for himself a source of seed, justifying the **Foundation of the World** <term>Yesod Olam; the Sefirah that acts as the divine procreative force</term>.
**And this is** the meaning of: "And the Lord remembered Sarah as He had said" <note>Genesis 21:1</note>; all the barren women were remembered [visited] along with her. Behold, Adam the First used this "place" <note>referring to the reproductive organ</note> with the **foreskin** <term>Orlah; the spiritual barrier or "husk" of impurity</term> in the matter of the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil. But Abraham came and removed the foreskin. Then, the "light sown for the righteous" <note>Psalm 97:11</note> was revealed, and Sarah was remembered, and all the barren women with her. Understand this: from here you will understand "So shall your seed be" <note>Genesis 15:5</note>, for He is the creator of the heavens and those who stretch them out beneath them, who spreads out the earth and its offspring, who gives a soul to the people upon it. This is the secret of the **Kingdom** <term>Malkhut; the final Sefirah, representing the physical world and the Divine Presence</term>, and this is the secret of "Thus <note>original: "Koh"; a mystical name for the Kingdom</note> shall you bless" <note>Numbers 6:23</note> and so on. "Thus says the God, I am the Lord... I have called you in righteousness and I will hold your hand" <note>Isaiah 42:5-6</note>.
**And know** that the secret of **Circumcision** <note>original: "Milah"</note> is the secret of **Redemption** <note>original: "Ge’ulah"</note>. I shall detail this: on the "Night of Vigil" <note>Passover</note>, the children of Israel were circumcised.
**And the secret** of Circumcision is the secret of Redemption because the **foreskin**, which was attached to the flesh, was removed during the exile. This is the secret of: "As for you also, by the blood of your covenant, I have sent forth your prisoners out of the pit" <note>Zechariah 9:11</note>. For the **Foundation** <note>Yesod</note> is the secret of Redemption, as it is drawn from the **Jubilee** <term>Yovel; the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding), which represents ultimate freedom</term>, regarding which it is written: "It shall have redemption" <note>Leviticus 25:24</note>, and "In the Jubilee he shall go out," and "In all the land of your possession, you shall grant redemption for the land." The Jubilee is certainly the secret of all redemptions and the secret of all freedom.
**And know** that by the quality of the Jubilee, Israel went out from the land of Egypt, for the **Gates of Understanding** <term>Binah</term> were opened for them. This is the secret of "And the children of Israel went up armed" <note>Exodus 13:18; the Hebrew word "chamushim" (armed) shares a root with "hamishim" (fifty), referencing the 50 Gates of Understanding</note> from the land of Egypt. You will find the Exodus from Egypt mentioned fifty times in the Torah and no more. Therefore, you find that Redemption is dependent upon the **Covenant of Circumcision** <note>the Sefirah of Yesod</note>, and when the Foundation connects with the Kingdom, Redemption is brought close to the Kingdom. This is why [the Sages] said: one must "join Redemption to Prayer" <note>a liturgical rule to not pause between the blessing of redemption and the Amidah prayer</note>, sealing with "the Redeemer of Israel" followed immediately by "O Lord, open my lips." Understand this: anyone who is circumcised merits two redemptions—a redemption above and a redemption below—for he is saved from the "Pit of Destruction," which is the foreskin. Therefore: "Who redeems your life from the pit, who crowns you with kindness and mercy" <note>Psalm 103:4</note>. Understand this well. Understand how great is the power of the Covenant of Circumcision, for this is the **Gate** through which one enters into all redemptions.

<column-break/>

And behold, when the Kingdom (*Malkhut*) draws and receives abundance from the Foundation (*Yesod*)—which is the secret of all redemptions extending from Understanding (*Binah*)—the Kingdom is then called the **Redeeming Angel**. That is to say, it acts as the messenger of the **Foundation of the World**. This is the "Angel who has redeemed me" <note>Genesis 48:16</note> who went with Jacob, called "the God who has been my shepherd" and so on. And this quality is called:

### THE STATUTE
<note>original: "Chok"</note>

And know that in every place in the Torah you find the word **Statute** (*Chok*), it is the secret of the quality of **Foundation** (*Yesod*). And in every place you find **Ordinance** (*Chukah*), it is the secret of the quality of **Kingdom** (*Malkhut*). Know and contemplate that *Chok* is the secret of the place of holiness and purity; it is the secret of inner Mercy. Everything that is adjacent to it on the outside is the secret of the **Husks** <term>Klipot; forces of evil or concealment</term>, the foreskins, and the forces of impurity. The inner is "Holy," and that which is outside of it is "Profane." It is called "God" <note>original: "Eloah"</note>, and what is outside of it is "Darkness." This is the place of separation that "distinguishes between the holy and the profane, between light and darkness." This is because this is the place where the foreskin takes hold from the outside. In this way, the quality of the Kingdom is called the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil**—either this or that. This is "as a rose among the thorns" <note>Song of Songs 2:2</note>. Therefore, in every place you find the language of *Chok* in the Torah, a person must distinguish between the partition of holiness and the partition of the profane.
For *Chok* implies a "limit" or a "fence," as in: "When He set for the sea its *statute* (*chuko*), that the waters should not pass His command" <note>Proverbs 8:29</note>. And it says: "He gave a *statute* that shall not pass" <note>Psalm 148:6</note>. Behold, surrounding this Statute are many kinds of thorns, thistles, and obstacles. This is the "Place of David." Therefore, he required songs [Psalms] to fight against the **Accusers** <term>Mekatregim; spiritual prosecuting forces</term>. He said: "Lord, how many are my adversaries!" <note>Psalm 3:2</note> and "All nations surrounded me; in the name of the Lord I will cut them off!" <note>Psalm 118:10</note>. And "They surrounded me, yes, they surrounded me; in the name of the Lord I will cut them off!" The foreskin is "cut off"—which is the Covenant of Circumcision—"in the name of the Lord, for I will cut them off" <note>original: "Amilam"; a play on the word "Milah" (circumcision)</note>.
**And know** that this is the place where David needed to fight his enemies. Therefore, he required a shield: "And You have given me the shield of Your salvation" <note>Psalm 18:36</note>. This is the **Shield of David** (*Magen David*). Therefore: "I will pursue my enemies and overtake them" <note>Psalm 18:38</note>. Know that David cut away all the forces of the foreskin and impurity surrounding the **Assembly of Israel** <term>Knesset Yisrael; a name for the Sefirah of Malkhut</term> and fought their battles.

<summary>This page explains the mystical significance of circumcision as the removal of the spiritual "foreskin" (impurity), allowing for the union of the Sefirot Yesod and Malkhut, which triggers redemption. It also defines the technical difference between the terms "Chok" (masculine statute/Yesod) and "Chukah" (feminine ordinance/Malkhut) as boundaries protecting holiness from external dark forces.</summary>
<keywords>Yesod, Malkhut, Circumcision (Milah), Redemption (Ge'ulah), Foreskin (Orlah), Jubilee (Yovel), Binah, Statutes (Chok), Husks (Klipot), King David, Shield of David</keywords>

--- Page 61 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of Yesod (Foundation) as the conduit of divine blessing. While the previous page focused on Abraham removing the "foreskin" of impurity to allow Sarah to conceive, this page deepens the mystical identity of Yesod as the quality of "All" (Kol) and its relationship with the tenth Sefirah, Malkhut (Kingdom).</meta>

"Foundation of the World," and from it all good things and all divine influences are drawn into the **Kingdom** <term>Malkhut; the tenth Sefirah, representing the Divine Presence in our physical world</term>. Therefore, it is called in the language of the mystics "**All**" <note>original: "Kol"</note>; that is to say, it is the attribute upon which everything depends. And so the scripture says, "all the precepts of all I have straightened" <note>Psalm 119:128</note>. This is the secret of "I am the Lord, who makes **all** [things]" <note>Isaiah 44:24</note>, meaning that from the attribute of "All," everything emerges. Indeed, this attribute is the Ninth Sefirah, and to it come all the spiritual conduits and all the emanations from the **Infinite** <term>Ein Sof; the hidden, boundless aspect of God</term>. From it, everything is flowed and emanated into the attribute of the Kingdom, and she [the Kingdom] is also blessed by this attribute of "All." 

When she is blessed by it, she is called *Namchiel* <note>A mystical name for the Divine Presence when it is filled with divine flow</note>. Her name is called **Bride** <note>original: "Kallah"</note> because she is "included" <note>original: "kelulah"</note> from "All" [Kol]. This is the "Desire" that she needs and for which she watches, because it is the foundation of everything and everything depends upon it. Because the Kingdom is blessed by it, she is called by its name, "All," as we have written; for the attribute of Kingdom is called by many names in various places, according to the Sefirah that fills her. We have already written great principles regarding this. In this way, the Kingdom is called *Namchiel* or "All," and so it says: "the profit of the earth is in all" <note>Ecclesiastes 5:8</note>. 

This "Desire" was given to the **Patriarchs** as an inheritance, because the three of them emanated their power from the attributes above, as we have explained. **Abraham** prepared conduits and rectifications for himself and it was acquired by him; this is what is meant by "And the Lord blessed Abraham in **all** [things]" <note>Genesis 24:1; original: "Bakol"</note>. It was called "The House of the Lord" after Abraham. It was given to **Isaac** through the law: "And I have eaten of **all**" <note>Genesis 27:33; original: "Mikol"</note>, and it was also called "The House of God" after Isaac. And it was given to **Jacob**, as it is written: "Because God has been gracious to me and because I have **everything**" <note>Genesis 33:11; original: "Kol"</note>. 

Therefore, the three Patriarchs inherited the attribute called "All." They merited "in all," and they lacked nothing "of all" above and below. They are truly called "Clusters" <note>original: "Eshkolot"</note>—in every place, a man upon whom everything depends. And this attribute is called **Zion**, because from Zion is the foundation of the Torah (others say: "because from Zion is the foundation of the world and from there the destruction of the Kingdom") and from the secret of the destruction of the world. Behold, Zion and Jerusalem represent **Foundation** [Yesod] and **Kingdom** [Malkhut]. And so: "Blessed be the Lord out of Zion, who dwells in Jerusalem" <note>Psalm 135:21</note>. And it is said of "He who restores His Divine Presence to Zion" that it is like one who returns a lost item to its owners.

And you already know that the City of David is the Zion of the Lord, and behold, the fourth leg of the Divine Chariot is **David**. This attribute [Yesod] is the secret of the attribute of the **Sabbath**, which is the seventh of the days of the oath <note>referring to the seven lower Sefirot</note>. Truly, "on it He rested from all His work" because in it all things were completed in perfection and fullness. The six days depend upon the Sabbath, and from its blessing and its goodness, the upper and lower worlds are blessed. Therefore, "God blessed the seventh day." It has no partner because it is the seventh; therefore, the **Assembly of Israel** <note>Knesset Yisrael; the spiritual personification of the Jewish people and the Sefirah of Malkhut</note> is its partner. Through them, the Ten Sefirot are united, and everything is blessed and held together. Therefore, regarding the Sabbath, it is said "Remember" [Zakhor] and "Observe" [Shamor]—"Remember" for the day and "Observe" for the night. 

Furthermore, the seal is from Sabbath eve to Sabbath eve. He who desecrates the Sabbath shall surely be put to death because he has denied the "World of Life." To this place, all the court-imposed deaths are connected. Behold, this attribute is literally the secret of **Circumcision**. The secret of the "cut" [Milah] and the "uncovering" [Peri’ah] corresponds to "Remember" and "Observe." Therefore, circumcision overrides the Sabbath. This is the secret of the cycles of seven, the Sabbatical years [Shemitah], and the Jubilees [Yovel]—representing the Sabbatical and Jubilee years above. If, God forbid, the Sabbatical years are nullified, then the land becomes liable for exile: "Surely all the days of its desolation it shall rest, which it did not rest during your Sabbaths"—measure for measure. 

Stand and contemplate the Torah of the hand, the hand. This "Desire" is the foundation of the **Covenant of Circumcision**, the secret of *Milah* and *Peri’ah*. In one, the *Milah* [cutting] corresponds to Yesod, and the *Peri’ah* [uncovering] corresponds to Malkhut. Therefore, "one who circumcises but does not uncover [the membrane], it is as if he did not circumcise." This is the gate through which one enters the secret of circumcision; for were it not for the Covenant of Circumcision, the Torah would not have been given to Israel. For this reason, you know what they said: "Through three things did Israel enter the covenant: Circumcision, Immersion, and the Offering of a Sacrifice." 

Behold, all who are circumcised are attached to Yesod and Malkhut. Therefore, it is written of Abraham: "Walk before Me and be whole... and I will set My covenant between Me and you" <note>Genesis 17:1-2</note>—literally between Me and your children. From that place [the covenant], he merited offspring. Understand this very well: if the source had not been opened, he would not have merited offspring. This is the secret of "And He brought him outside" <note>Genesis 15:5</note>. Once Abraham was circumcised and the source was revealed, Sarah bore him a son. This is why it says: "And He brought him outside and said: Look now toward the heavens and count the stars." And in these...

<summary>This page explains the mystical Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation) as the source of all divine flow, identifying it with the term "All" (Kol) and linking it to the Patriarchs, the Sabbath, and the act of circumcision. It emphasizes that the physical covenant of circumcision is the spiritual "gate" that allows divine energy to flow from the upper worlds into the physical reality of the Kingdom (Malkhut).</summary>

| ...the limit of cutting off the cause. <note>This likely refers to preventing the "external" forces or "husks" from latching onto the divine flow.</note> And Israel bore [the light] from within. This is the secret of the verse: **"none of you shall go out of the door of his house until morning"** <note>Exodus 12:22</note>. One who understands this secret will also understand the secret of **forbidden mixtures** <term>Kilayim; the prohibition against mixing different species of plants or animals</term> and **Sha’atnez** <term>a specific prohibition against mixing wool and linen in clothing</term>. Why were these forbidden to Israel, yet permitted in the **ritual fringes** <term>Tzitzit; the tassels on the corners of a garment</term>? One who understands this secret will understand the secret of the **Kingdoms** <note>The Sefirah of Malkhut/Kingdom as it manifests in various ways</note> regarding which it is written, "wool and flax" and **"She stretches out her hand to the distaff"** <note>Proverbs 31:13, 19</note>. Blessed are you, Israel, who have merited this! Therefore it is written "mixtures" <note>original: "Kilayim"</note>, meaning that one brings two things into a single boundary—he "confines" <note>original: "yikhla," a play on words between kilayim (mixtures) and kela (prison/confinement)</note> them both. "Hand, hand"—both are "hands." "Foot, foot"—both are "feet." "Day, day"—both are "days." However, there are some things that cannot be divided, such as water or millstones. These things are among the **mysteries of the world**.<br/>### And Know<br/>that in the **crowns of Laban** <note>Likely a reference to white priestly garments or a mystical interpretation of the character Laban (whose name means "White")</note> there is a great secret, and in the other **priestly garments** there is a great secret regarding the reason for the prohibition of forbidden mixtures. And if you merit to enter, you will see many wonders. After we have explained this to you, know that when the Torah was given to Israel, it brought with it **statutes and ordinances**. Happy is the people for whom it is thus! Know that the Torah is **bitter on the outside and sweet on the inside**. Therefore it is written, **"there He made for them a statute and an ordinance, and there He tested them"** <note>Exodus 15:25, referring to the site of Marah</note>. In this place lies the secret of miracles and wonders, and "Egypt fled toward it." It is all contained in the word **Statute** <term>Chok; a law without a stated rational reason</term>, because it is joined to **Ordinance** <term>Mishpat; a law based on justice or logic</term>. I shall now explain: "Ordinance" is the attribute of **Beauty** <term>Tiferet; the sixth Sefirah, representing harmony and the written Torah</term>, while "Statute" is the attribute of **Foundation** <term>Yesod; the ninth Sefirah, the site of the covenant</term>. Jacob is grasped within Beauty, while the "Line" is grasped within Ordinance, as it is said: "And my great ordinance is a fire to him." There are statutes and ordinances for the righteous. Because Jacob merited the birthright, he merited the **Statute** which was established with Abraham, his oath to Isaac, and He confirmed it to **Jacob as a statute** <note>Psalm 105:9-10</note>. This is because the Statute depends on Jacob, and he is "Father Jacob," and the Children of Israel are called "Righteous" after "Father Jacob." See, we have written for you a few wonders regarding the matter of the "Statute." I now hand over to you a key regarding what is said: **"There He made for them a statute and an ordinance, and there | He tested them."** | All miracles and wonders depend on **Marah** <note>The place of "Bitterness"</note>. Within her, she is sweet, though from the outside, she is remembered as "bitter." She "went out to the outside." And thus it says, **"And the son of an Israelite woman... went out"** <note>Leviticus 24:10</note>. This is the secret of every place where it says [he went out], for because he "removed himself from the community, he denied the essential principle." One who understands this will understand the secret of **"Before He had made the earth and the fields"** <note>Proverbs 8:26</note>, and **"He brought him outside"** <note>Genesis 15:5</note>, and **"The wife of the deceased shall not be married outside"** <note>Deuteronomy 25:5</note>, and **"he ran to the man outside"** <note>Genesis 24:29</note>. Were it not for the "Evil Eye," his heart would have been focused [within]. Therefore, anyone who **slaughters [a sacrifice] outside** the Temple is subject to "Excision" <note>Karet; spiritual severance</term>. After the Holy Temple was built, all these great principles were included in the word **Statute**. Now, set your heart and contemplate, and you will find the secret of the **"well of living water"** versus the secrets of the **"broken cisterns that can hold no water"** <note>Jeremiah 2:13</note>. This attribute is called **Grace** <note>original: "Chen"</note> by those who know the secrets.<br/>## Joining<br/>The secret of the name "Levi" is found in the verse: **"Now this time my husband will be joined to me"** <note>Genesis 29:34; "joined" is 'yillaveh,' the root of Levi</note>. Therefore, the **Levite** had no portion or inheritance with his brothers. **David** is his inheritance <note>Traditionally, "The Lord is his inheritance," but here David represents the Sefirah of Malkhut</note>, for he has no business on the outside, only on the inside. One who understands this will understand that **"they are a graceful wreath for your head and necklaces for your throat"** <note>Proverbs 1:9</note>. I am hinting here: "like the space of a man and joining <note>original: "livyah"</note> all around." I will add the words: "David is gracious to the poor." All **loans** <note>original: "halva'ot," also from the root L-V-H</note> in the world depend on this place. It is written: **"The wicked borrows and does not repay, but the righteous is gracious and gives"** <note>Psalm 37:21</note>. Therefore, great is the one who lends to the poor in his time of distress. For this reason, the Levite had no portion or inheritance. Know that the Levites necessarily required a "pure inheritance"—a great secret from the secrets of the Faith. **"And thus you shall do to them to cleanse them: sprinkle the water of purification upon them, and let them pass a razor over all their flesh, and let them wash their clothes, and cleanse themselves"** <note>Numbers 8:7</note>. In this place lies the secret of **Purity**. Because impurity surrounds from every side, one needs the "shaving" which is the secret of the **"Songs of David"** <note>A mystical connection between "cutting" the hair/impurity and "pruning" or "singing" (Zemirot)</note>. We have already written the reason above: **"The way of a serpent upon a rock"** <note>Proverbs 30:19</note>. This is the secret of what we wrote above. Therefore, they need the inheritance. Behold, the Great Creation is for the Righteous, and the Levites are on the **Left Side**. These are the **Gates of Righteousness**...<br/>|

<meta>This page continues the exploration of spiritual boundaries and the concept of "mixtures." The previous page discussed how certain forbidden combinations of wool and linen (Sha'atnez) are permitted only in sacred items like ritual fringes, symbolizing the unification of divine forces. This page moves into the distinction between the "inner" holy realm and the "outer" chaotic realm, specifically through the concept of "Statutes" (Chukim)—laws that seem irrational but serve as mystical borders.</meta>

And in the forest of thorns surrounding the vineyard, then came Solomon his son and observed <note>original: "v-tzafah," meaning he watched or envisioned</note> everything in tranquility; there was no Accuser <term>Satan; the spiritual force of prosecution or obstacle</term> and no evil occurrence. He established the House on its foundation. Know that this is why it is said in the section regarding Passover: **"Draw out and take for yourselves..."** <note>Exodus 12:21</note> and so on, and **"You shall observe this thing as a statute for you and your children."** 

This is the place where the wicked stumble, for they find no logical reason for the commandments called "Statutes" <term>Chukim; laws given by God that lack a humanly apparent rational explanation</term> or "Decrees." They do not know the great and good principles that hang upon them. Therefore, the Torah says: **"You shall keep my statutes: You shall not let your cattle mate with a diverse kind [Kilayim]..."** <note>Leviticus 19:19</note>. And it says: **"And you shall keep my statutes."** Our Sages of Blessed Memory said that these are things that the Accuser <note>Satan</note> and the nations of the world use to challenge Israel, such as the prohibition of mixing wool and linen, the prohibition of meat and milk, forbidden mixtures of seeds, the eating of pork, and other similar things. All of these are included in the category of "Statutes."

Behold, the **Red Heifer** <term>Parah Adumah; a cow whose ashes were used to purify those who came into contact with death</term> and the **Scapegoat** <term>Se'ir HaMishtaleach; the goat sent into the wilderness on Yom Kippur</term> are both faithful witnesses that reveal supernal secrets regarding the mystery of Separation <note>Havdalah</note>.

**Know** that surrounding the Kingdom <note>Malkhut; the final Sefirah</note> and the mystery of the Throne are "briars and thorns." And behold, surrounding the Foundation <note>Yesod; the Sefirah preceding Malkhut</note> is the "field full of murderers and accusers." This is the spiritual Prince of Esau <note>Jacob’s brother, representing the external, chaotic forces</note>, as it is written: **"And Esau came from the field"** <note>Genesis 25:29</note>, and he was exhausted because he had shed blood. This is the field in which forbidden relations are uncovered, as it says: **"For he found her in the field, the betrothed girl cried out and there was no one to save her"** <note>Deuteronomy 22:27</note>. This is the field in which Cain killed Abel: **"And it happened when they were in the field, Cain rose up against Abel his brother and killed him"** <note>Genesis 4:8</note>. This is the field where the fallen corpses lie, and this is the secret of the verse: **"And whoever touches one slain by the sword on the open field, or a corpse, or a human bone, or a grave..."** <note>Numbers 19:16</note>. This is the place of impurities because they are external.

**However, there is** in the inner chambers a "Good Field," and it is called the **Field of Apple Trees** <note>original: "Sode Tapuchim," a Kabbalistic term for the Shekhinah or the presence of God</note>. This is the meaning of: **"Like the fragrance of a field which the Lord has blessed"** <note>Genesis 27:27</note>. This implies that there is another field which is cursed—and understand this very well.

Therefore, the Holy One, Blessed be He, commanded the law of the **Red Heifer** and called it a "Statute": **"This is the statute of the Torah"** <note>Numbers 19:2</note>. This means the commandment of the Red Heifer is a great secret that separates between the holy and the profane, and between the impure and the pure. The side facing inward is pure, while the side facing outward is impure. This is why the ash of the Heifer "purifies the impure and renders the pure impure" <note>A famous paradox of the Red Heifer ritual</note>. Observe this great principle, for from it you will enter into all its distant matters.

**And know** that the pig has some signs of purity—it has split hooves—which hints at something, yet it is completely forbidden. There is also a great secret regarding the rabbit and the hare, which chew the cud but do not have split hooves. Our Sages asked: Why is the pig called *Chazir*? Because in the future, the Holy One, Blessed be He, will "return" <note>original: "le-hachazir," a pun on the word for pig, chazir</note> it to Israel to be eaten <note>This is a radical mystical tradition suggesting that in the messianic future, the "outer" forces will be transformed or "returned" to holiness</note>. One who knows these principles will understand how many books and secrets depend on "Statutes" and "Decrees." This is why it says: **"You shall not walk in the statutes of the nations which I cast out before you"** <note>Leviticus 20:23</note>. For the statute that faces inward is *our* statute and it is pure; the statute that faces outward is the statute of the nations and it is impure.

According to this path, you will find two goats on Yom Kippur: one whose blood enters the innermost Sanctuary, and one that is sent outward to **Azazel** in the wilderness. It is written: **"And this shall be an eternal statute for you... on the tenth day of the month."**

**One who understands** this will understand the secret of why Jacob and Esau were born as twins from one womb and yet were separated from each other. He will also understand the secret of: **"And the goat shall bear upon it all their iniquities unto a land of cutting off [Gezirah]"** <note>Leviticus 16:22</note>. These are wonders within the secret of what is written: **"But Esau I hated, and I made his mountains a waste"** <note>Malachi 1:3</note>. Yet both were born in one womb! Both are the secret of the "Statute."

Wisdom <note>Binah</note>, hear this! The secret of: **"Was not Esau Jacob’s brother? says the Lord: Yet I loved Jacob, but Esau I hated"** <note>Malachi 1:2</note>. Both were born in one womb, and the secret of impurity and purity depends on the word "Statute" [Chok]. This is also the secret of the foreskin and circumcision, and the secret of the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil. If you understand these principles, they will be clarified for you wherever you find the language of "Statutes" or "Decrees" in the Torah.

One who understands this will understand the secret of: **"If not for My covenant day and night, I would not have set the statutes of heaven and earth"** <note>Jeremiah 33:25</note>. Deep secrets and hidden wonders are stored in these places. This is the secret of: **"You shall keep My statutes."** From here, you will enter the secret of the Exile and the secret of the Redemption, and the reason why Israel was redeemed from Egypt. **And know** that Egypt is a "borderer" to the Land of Israel, as we have already written. Therefore, it was a place of imprisonment for Israel, who were dwelling on the side of the fence facing outward—which is the place of the exiles. And the Holy One, Blessed be He, commanded...

It uses the Red Heifer, the Scapegoat, and dietary laws to illustrate how these divine decrees manage the interface between purity and impurity.</summary>

<meta>This page continues from the discussion of spiritual statutes (Chukim) and sacred spaces. While the previous page focused on the "Red Heifer" and laws that transcend human logic, this page shifts to the geography of holiness, identifying the physical locations of Jerusalem and the Temple as the essential gateways for the soul and its prayers to reach the Divine.</meta>

As it is said: **"And it shall please the Lord better than an ox or a bullock that has horns and hoofs."** <note>Psalm 69:32</note>

And this is the place where Noah built an altar to the Lord. It is the same place called **Mount Moriah**, and it is where Isaac was bound <term>Akedah; the biblical story of the binding of Isaac, traditionally located on the Temple Mount</term>. And this is the gateway to all the **Sefirot** <term>Divine emanations; the ten attributes through which God interacts with the world</term>. For when a person enters **Malchut** <term>Kingship; the tenth and lowest Sefirah, associated with the physical world and the "Gate of God"</term>, it is written: **"This is the gate of the Lord; the righteous shall enter through it."** <note>Psalm 118:20</note>

When **Adam the first man** held onto these two Sefirot—meaning **Malchut** and **Yesod** <term>Foundation; the ninth Sefirah, representing the channel of Divine light</term>—he would ascend as far as the **Keter** <term>Crown; the highest and most hidden Sefirah</term>, and he had no accuser or obstacle to stop him. Therefore, it is written regarding the Sefirah of Malchut: **"Open for me the gates of righteousness"** <note>Psalm 118:19; *Sha'arei Tzedek* is a common mystical name for Malchut</note>, for there are many gates: the gates of **Yesod**, and the gates of **Binah** <term>Understanding; the third Sefirah</term>, which is the source of the Torah. When a person enters Yesod—which is the "place of the oath"—he has no hater or hinderer. This is what the verse means: **"This is the gate of the Lord; the righteous shall enter through it,"** because this place is the gateway for ascent.

The secret regarding this depth is the **Secret of Jerusalem**, and **Zion** is the inner gate; it is called **Yesodi** <note>Meaning "my foundation" or "of the Sefirah Yesod"</note>. From there, one ascends to the "Place of Will." Therefore, when Israel was in Jerusalem, they would pray in the Holy Temple, and their prayer would ascend directly to the heights without any hinderer or accuser. This is the secret of Solomon’s prayer when he built the Holy Temple <note>Refers to King Solomon's long prayer in 1 Kings 8</note>. One who contemplates this will understand great and awesome principles, as we have explained in this place.

You must contemplate that section of Solomon's prayer. You will find all matters arranged there, and all of this pertains to the "Way of the Land of Israel," Jerusalem, and the Holy Temple. However, the prayer offered from **outside the Land** <term>Chutz La'aretz; the Diaspora</term> faces many accusers before it can ascend. The prayer from outside the Land does not ascend directly upward; rather, it travels to the Land of Israel, and from there to Jerusalem, and from there to the Holy Temple, just as it is written in the prayer of Solomon (peace be upon him). When it reaches the Holy Temple, then it ascends on high.

Therefore, the People of Israel who are in exile pray toward Jerusalem from the four corners of the earth, with Jerusalem in the center. Thus, all those in the exile who are somewhat to the West pray toward the East. Likewise, those in the East pray toward the West. And those in the North turn their faces and pray <note>The text says "to the North," likely a scribal error for "to the South," as one North of Jerusalem must face South to see it</note>. Thus it is found that all of Israel sends their prayers to Jerusalem and to the Holy Temple, for from there, prayers ascend to the Name, may He be blessed.

### Now

difference between the prayer of the Land
and the prayer from outside the Land.

### Know
That the prayer of the Land of Israel
is like someone who needs
to speak with the King, and his house is very close
to the King's palace. There is no evil mishap on the road
and no evil adversary <note>Satan</note>. But the prayer from outside the Land

is very far from the King's palace. On the road, there are 
many bandits, many thieves, and many 
wild beasts. Now, see how 
careful one must be to escape the damage of the road.

to be accepted, as we have said. 

Furthermore, know 
how many obstacles and accusers face the prayers of 
Israel as they travel to the Land of Israel, due to 
the forces of impurity belonging to the nations of the world. 
See how much we must purify 
ourselves and focus our intention in our prayers in the 
Diaspora, so that we may be worthy 
to pray to the Name, may He be blessed. Now that

like hints and parables—know that the secret of "To the Lord"

It contrasts the direct, safe "ascent" of prayers from within the Land of Israel with the dangerous journey Diaspora prayers must take through a realm of spiritual "bandits" and "accusers."</summary>

--- Page 65 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of the spiritual gates and the geography of the Divine. While the previous page established the physical location of the Temple Mount (Mount Moriah) as the "Gate of the Lord" where the righteous enter, this page explains the internal dynamics of the Sefirot—specifically how the qualities of Kindness and Judgment are joined together—and how the "Righteous One" acts as a channel for Divine flow, contrasting this with the nature of the "Wicked."</meta>

From the side of **Greatness** <note>original: *Gedulah*, another name for the Sefirah of *Chesed*</note> comes **Kindness**, while from the side of **Might** <note>original: *Gevurah*</note> comes **Judgment**. Therefore, they all accompany and gather in this attribute called **Joining** <note>original: *Leviyah*; the text is playing on the word for "Levite" and the Hebrew root meaning "to join" or "to accompany"</note>. Behold, from this joining came forth the **Priests**, who represent pure Kindness, and the rest of the **Levites**, who represent Judgment. Let the **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*; the third Sefirah</note> hear this! For this reason, a Levite is truly called a **Levite**.

And I shall now explain: **"Leviathan the piercing serpent, Leviathan the crooked serpent"** <note>Isaiah 27:1</note>; **"The way of a serpent upon a rock"** <note>Proverbs 30:19</note>. For within it, the external forces take hold through the secret of Mercy and Judgment. There is one who **borrows and repays**, and there is one who **borrows and does not pay back**.

The one who **borrows and repays** is the **Righteous One** <term>Tzaddik; here referring to the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation), which acts as the conduit for Divine energy</term> of the inner countenances, who draws the supreme powers from the **Sefirot** <term>Divine emanations; the ten attributes through which God interacts with the world</term> and extends them into the **Assembly of Israel** <term>Knesset Yisrael; often associated with the Sefirah of Malchut (Kingship) or the Shekhinah</term>. Then, the entire world is filled with blessing. However, the forces of impurity receive but do not pass the flow onward; they cut off the goodness from the world. This is the secret of the verse: **"The wicked borrows and does not repay, but the righteous is gracious and gives"** <note>Psalm 37:21</note>—giving to all as explained. And this is the secret of: **"He who is gracious to the poor lends to the Lord, and He will repay him for his deed"** <note>Proverbs 19:17</note>, from the language of "Bestowing kindness" and "bestowing with his hand."

### And Know
that there is a **Leviathan**, and it is in the **sea** <note>In Kabbalah, the "sea" often represents the Sefirah of Malchut or the hidden depths of the Divine</note>. From it, the righteous are destined to make a feast—this is said of all things—and through it, they enter into the life of the **World to Come**. Whoever merits the "Feast of the Leviathan" merits the life of the World to Come and has a portion there. This is the secret of: **"Your people shall all be righteous; they shall inherit the land forever"** <note>Isaiah 60:21</note>. Let what we have hinted at in this place be sufficient for you. This is the attribute that is called in every place:

### Remember
<note>original: *Zakhor*</note> because it is the place of **Memory**, from which one ascends to the place of **Understanding** <note>Binah</note>. This is what the scripture means: **"He has made a memorial for His wonders"** <note>Psalm 111:4</note>. We have already explained to you that the three highest Sefirot are the place of deep thought and emanation. All things are like the image of a spring: a person sees the bubbling source but does not know the place of its beginning. This is why it says: **"To Him who alone does great wonders"** <note>Psalm 136:4</note>, and it is written: **"I will praise You, for I am fearfully and wonderfully made; wonderful are Your works"** <note>Psalm 139:14</note>.

The great secret is: **"Awesome in praises, doing wonders"** <note>Exodus 15:11</note>. He who understands the secret of "Awesome" <note>Nora</note> and the secret of "Praises" <note>Tehillot</note> will understand the place of the "Wonders" <note>Niflaot</note>. Because that place is hidden and concealed, the Holy One, blessed be He, prepared for it a place of "nurturing," and this is the secret of what is our **Seventh** <term>likely referring to Yesod, the seventh from the top if counting from Chesed, which "nurtures" the world</term>. It draws from that supreme place the knowledge of secrets, hidden things, and wonders. Therefore it is written: **"He has made a memorial for His wonders; happy is the people for whom it is so."**

Up to this place called **Remember** <note>Zakhor</note>, we say "All," because everything is within it, and from it, we enter into all the supreme clouds. This is the place of **Blessing**, from which the **Assembly of Israel** is blessed. This is what the scripture says: **"In every place where I cause My name to be mentioned, I will come to you and bless you"** <note>Exodus 20:21</note>. This is related to the expression "The Hand" and "The Lord has remembered us, He will bless" <note>Psalm 115:12</note>.

Know that in every place where the **Male** <note>Zakhar; a pun on *Zakhor* (Remember)</note> principle shines, blessing is found. The secret is: **"The memory of the righteous is a blessing"** <note>Proverbs 10:7</note>, because he borrows and repays. The Lord is our inheritance, but the wicked borrow and do not repay. It is written: **"The wicked borrows and does not repay"**; this is **"the name of the wicked shall rot"** <note>Proverbs 10:7</note>, like rot that consumes wood. Now, contemplate this verse that says: **"The memory of the righteous is a blessing, but the name of the wicked shall rot."**

### And Behold
We have explained to you that this attribute is called **Oath** <note>Shevuah</note>, because oaths depend upon it. One who denies an oath has no place or existence left in any of the Sefirot. Such a person is "vile like a withered leaf" <note>a play on the word *Naval* (vile/foolish) and *Nivelet* (withered)</note>, because he has no place to draw sustenance from the Sefirot and no foundation upon which to lean. We have already provided a great and honorable explanation of this above.

### And Know
that this is the **Gate** through which one enters to see the face of the King, the Lord. Therefore, the **Holy Temple** was built on **Mount Zion**. Know and believe that this is the place where **Adam the first man** offered a sacrifice, as it is said:

and bloodshed by his hand. But regarding David it is written: **(and he was ruddy, with beautiful eyes and a good appearance)** <note>1 Samuel 16:12</note>. Therefore, when David clothed himself in the attribute of **ruddy** <note>original: *Admoni*; a reddish hue associated with the Sefirah of *Gevurah* or Judgment</note>, he fought against his enemies, and his sword never returned empty. And when he clothed himself with **beautiful eyes**, he provided for Israel and performed many acts of kindness with a **generous eye** <note>The Hebrew "generous eye" (*ayin yafeh*) is a pun on "beautiful eyes" (*yafeh einayim*)</note>. And when he clothed himself in **good appearance**, he was meticulous in the study of Torah, descending into its depths and gazing upon its secrets. For these three attributes were joined in the attribute of the **Living God** <note>original: *El Chai*, a name associated with the Sefirah of *Yesod*</note>, which are Kindness, Judgment, and Mercy. David took hold of the Table and he, too, became the **fourth leg** for the Throne that supports the three. This is the secret of what was said: **"The Tachkemonite, head of the three, and he had a name among the three"** <note>2 Samuel 23:8; interpreted here as David joining the three Patriarchs—Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob—as the foundations of the Divine Chariot</note>.

**And know** that from these two attributes, called **YHWH** <note>the Tetragrammaton, the four-letter name of God</note> and **Elohim Tzevaot** <note>God of Hosts</note>, all the hosts of the world are drawn forth according to their kinds, and from them proceed all types of wars in the world. He fights the wars on high; **"The zeal of YHWH Tzevaot will perform this"** <note>Isaiah 9:6</note>. From these, the Prophets draw their nourishment, as it is said regarding the Prophets in many places: **"Thus says YHWH"**. Because they did not attain the level of the Supreme Name, they said, **"Thus says the Lord."**

**And know** that the level of Moses our Teacher, peace be upon him, is above all other prophets. Moses our Teacher took hold of the **Source**, while all other prophets take hold of the **Branches of the Stream** <note>original: *Ikarei ha-Nachal*</note> below. The Writings <note>original: *Ketuvim*; the third section of the Hebrew Bible</note> take hold of the **Pool**. These two attributes are called **Nachaliel** <term>Inheritance of God; also a pun on "Stream of God"</term>. that pours everything into it is called **Ten** <note>original: *Yod*; likely referring to the ten Sefirot or the first letter of the Divine Name</note>. We find these four attributes included in a single verse: **"All the streams flow to the sea, yet the sea is not full"** <note>Ecclesiastes 1:7</note>. And as for what is said, **"the sea is not full,"** it means it has no fullness of its own. Furthermore, the sea receives from one side and satisfies all the inhabitants of the world from the other side. This is the secret of: **"And a river went out of Eden to water the garden; from there it divided and became four heads"** <note>Genesis 2:10</note>. After we have informed you of this regarding these two attributes, which are **YHWH** and **Elohim Tzevaot**, we must enter into the explanation of each one individually, according to the proper measure, with the help of the Creator and according to our ability. **"O God of Hosts, save us and we shall be saved, and show us Your face."**

# Elohim Tzevaot
### And the reason it is called

**Elohim Tzevaot** is that this attribute draws all types of Judgment and Might from the **Left Side** <note>the side of *Gevurah*</note>, drawing from **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*</note> and from **Fear** <note>original: *Pachad*; another name for the Sefirah of *Gevurah*</note>. It fights the wars of **YHWH** among the heavenly hosts and among all the multitudes below. This is what is written: **"On that day, YHWH will punish the host of the high heaven on high, and the kings of the earth upon the earth"** <note>Isaiah 24:21</note>. We must inform you of the core of the matter: just as I have already informed you that the name **Adonai** <term>Lord; associated with the Sefirah of *Malchut* or Sovereignty</term> rules over all the multitudes of above and below, and He is the great Provider for all creatures. In His hand are all the King's treasures, all the warriors of war, and all the weapons of war: **horses of fire and chariots of fire**, armaments, spears, swords, bows, arrows, stones for catapults, and all other instruments of war. There are **sixty warriors** surrounding this attribute, **"all of them skilled in war, each with his sword upon his thigh because of the fear in the night"** <note>Song of Songs 3:7-8</note>. They all receive abundance from the side of **Fear** and **Kindness**. I have already informed you that the name called **Adonai** represents the attribute of **Sovereignty** <note>original: *Malchut*</note>, and He is the ruler over all creatures.

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the "fourth leg" of the Divine Throne, identifying King David's role within the Sefirotic system. It transitions from a description of David’s character into a mystical explanation of the mechanics of prayer and the specific Divine Names that govern different spiritual forces.</meta>

When the prayer cleaves to the **Living God** <term>El Chai; the Sefirah of Yesod, which acts as the foundation and conduit for divine energy</term>, it enters through all the chambers where the Glory resides. This is the "Ascent of Prayer" to the place of **Understanding** <term>Binah; the third Sefirah, often called the "Divine Mother" from which the lower attributes emerge</term>.
The place of **Understanding** then cleaves to **Wisdom** <term>Chokhmah; the second Sefirah, representing the primal flash of intuition</term>, which is called **Will** <note>original: *Ratzon*</note>. This is the secret of the "time of favor." Therefore, we say at the beginning of the prayer, **"Lord, open my lips"** <note>Psalm 51:17</note>, for this is the gateway to enter. At the end of the prayer, we say, **"May the words of my mouth be for favor"** <note>Psalm 19:14</note>, because **Will** is the pinnacle of the levels. It is the letter *Yod* of the Unique Name <note>YHVH</note>, which is called **Will**. From this **Will**, which is the secret of the *Yod*, the prayer ascends to the tip of the *Yod* <note>the highest point of the letter, symbolizing the bridge to the unknowable</note> and cleaves to the **Crown** <term>Keter; the highest Sefirah, representing Divine Will above intellect</term>, which is called the **Infinite** <term>Ein Sof</term>, for prayers ascend until...

<column-break/>

### YHVH God of Hosts

**"Hear my prayer, give ear, O God of Jacob, Selah"** <note>Psalm 84:9</note>. For the two names, **YHVH of Hosts** and **God of Hosts**, are unified one against the other. Both are prepared to pour out [abundance] through the channels from above into the attribute of the **Living God**—that is, the drawing down of the **Divine Emanations** <term>Sefirot</term>. Both act according to the righteous Law, like all the hosts of the world. One acts from the side of **Kindness** <term>Chesed</term> and the second acts from the side of **Judgment** <term>Din</term>. Both draw **Judgment** and **Mercy** <term>Rachamim</term> into the attribute of the **Living God**. 
**We** must inform you that the attribute of **YHVH of Hosts** is what draws down higher **Kindness**, the kindness of **Abraham**, and eternal life into the attribute of the **Living God**. The attribute of **God of Hosts** is what draws the power of **Might** <term>Gevurah</term>, **Judgment**, **Fear**, and **Punishment** into the attribute of the **Living God**. Thus, the attribute of the **Living God** includes everything; therefore, it is called **"All"** <note>original: *Kol*</note>, for in it is Kindness, Mercy, and Judgment. This is the secret of **"I am YHVH, Maker of all"** <note>Isaiah 44:24</note>, and it is written: **"Therefore all the precepts of YHVH regarding all things I have deemed right"** <note>Psalm 119:128</note>.

---

The place of the **Crown** <note>Keter</note> was established in the *Musaf* <note>the "Additional" prayer service recited on Sabbaths and Festivals</note> when we say, "And they shall give to You, YHVH our God." This is said in the *Musaf* prayer and not in the *Yotzer* <note>the standard morning prayer</note>. For in the *Yotzer* prayer, not all the prayers have been said yet, and they have not reached the **Crown**. But in the *Musaf* prayer, the *Yotzer* prayers are already completed and have ascended to the head of the **Crown**. 
After I have awakened you to all these principles, we must now enter into the explanation of the rest of the Names until [we reach] the Levels and the Name—may He be blessed. May He lead us and teach us the way of truth, so that we may be cleaving to Him and His descendants forever, Amen.

---

And it says, **"And YHVH blessed Abraham with everything"** <note>Genesis 24:1</note>. The reason is because all the channels and the flows for **Judgment** and **Mercy** all flow into the attribute of the **Living God**. And "from whom" <gloss>who</gloss>? From the attributes of **YHVH of Hosts** and **God of Hosts**, which are two attributes, to the right and to the left. From these two attributes, together with the third in the middle, the attribute of **"All"** <note>Kol</note> is comprised. 
Because King **David** (peace be upon him) was clothed in the attribute of the **Living God**, along with the tip of the letter *Vav*—which is the secret of the fourth leg of the **Divine Chariot** <term>Merkavah; the vision of God's throne supported by the Patriarchs and David</term>—and he was emptied into the attribute of the **Living God**. Since he included **Kindness**, **Judgment**, and **Mercy**, it was said of him: **"He was ruddy, with beautiful eyes and a good appearance"** <note>1 Samuel 16:12</note>. 
**"He was ruddy"**—this is the secret of the flow of **Judgment**. **"With beautiful eyes"**—this is the secret of the **Kindness of Abraham**. **"And a good appearance"**—this is **David**, representing **Beauty** <term>Tiferet; the central Sefirah of balance</term>, which is **Jacob**, in whom "Good" is dependent. 
But regarding **Esau**, what is written of him? **"And the first came out ruddy all over"** <note>Genesis 25:25</note>—without "beautiful eyes" or "good appearance," but rather like a "hairy mantle." Therefore, he was a shedder of blood, and it is written of him, **"By your sword you shall live"** <note>Genesis 27:40</note>, for he inherited blood and shedding...

# Hod <gloss>3</gloss> <note>The third attribute mentioned in the verse from 1 Chronicles 29:11</note> within the verse: "Yours, O Lord, is the Greatness, and the Might, and the Beauty, and the Victory, and the **Splendor**" <note>original: *Hod*</note>.

The reason it is called **Splendor** <note>original: *Hod*</note> is because it clothes itself in the attribute of **Might** <term>Gevurah; the Sefirah of judgment and restraint</term> to humble enemies, to win battles, and to rescue those who love the Most High. This is the secret of: **"my splendor was turned into corruption, and I retained no strength"** <note>Daniel 10:8</note>. All the various types of thanksgivings that David spoke in the Book of Psalms are attached to this attribute. The thanksgiving for miracles and wonders are fixed in this place, following the secret of: **"Let them give thanks to the Lord for His goodness, and for His wonders to the children of men"** <note>Psalm 107:8</note>. 

This is also the secret of the **"four who must give thanks"** <note>The Talmudic requirement for those who survived a sea voyage, a desert crossing, illness, or imprisonment to offer a blessing of gratitude</note>. From this place, He bestows good upon the undeserving, in partnership with **Lord of Hosts** <note>original: *YHVH Tzevaot*; associated with the Sefirah of Netzach</note>, which is the secret of the three-fold **acts of lovingkindness** <note>original: *Gemilut Hasadim*</note>. This is the secret of **"Give thanks to the Lord, for He is good"** <note>Psalm 118:1</note> in its entirety, and the secret of the **Thanksgiving** <note>original: *Modim*</note> prayer established in the liturgy. The **"Thanksgiving of the Sages"** <note>original: *Modim d'Rabbanan*; a communal response during the cantor's repetition of the Amidah</note> is also fixed in this place. 

For **Lord God of Hosts** is the secret attribute of the **disciples of the wise** <note>original: *Talmidei Chachamim*; scholars</note>. Because of this, it is said: **"scholars increase peace in the world"** <note>Talmud Berakhot 64a</note>. Its secret is known to the masters of esoteric wisdom <note>original: *Yodei Chen*; Kabbalists</note>, for they are the ones who increase peace. This is the secret of: **"For the seed shall be prosperous; the vine shall give her fruit, and the ground shall give her increase, and the heavens shall give their dew"** <note>Zechariah 8:12</note>. You already know that the **"dew of Hermon"** descends upon the mountains of Zion, which are **Victory** and **Splendor** <term>Netzach and Hod; the seventh and eighth Sefirot</term>. Because they are the place of thanksgivings, the Sages said that one must **bow** <note>original: *Kri'ah*</note> during the *Modim* prayer. These bows are the secret of the submission required for those entering into **True Knowledge**. 

Regarding this, it is said: **"to You every knee shall bow"** <note>Isaiah 45:23</note>. Every blessing that comes into the world comes through these two. Therefore, one who does not bow during the *Modim* prayer, his spine becomes a **snake** after seventy years. The secret of the **seventy years** refers to the **seventy ministers** <note>The celestial patrons of the seventy nations of the world</note> who stand like a line in a circle; for the serpent lurks on the outside, corresponding to **Victory** and **Splendor**. Anyone who is not careful with them deserves to be bitten by the snake. This is the secret of: **"he who breaches the words of the Sages deserves the bite of the snake"** <note>Ecclesiastes 10:8</note>. You already know that **Wisdom** is found in **Victory** and **Splendor** <note>Often referred to as the "kidneys that advise"</note>. According to this path, when the Name **Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*</note> clothes itself in the garment of **Splendor**, it wins all the battles of Israel, takes vengeance upon our adversaries, and pays recompense to all the enemies of our souls. Therefore, one must give thanks to Him who clothes Himself in the garments of **Splendor** and **Victory** for war. This is the secret of: **"You are clothed with splendor and majesty"** <note>Psalm 104:1</note>. This is the "God of Thanksgivings, Master of Wonders." Therefore, contemplate every place where you find the language of:

...the Prophets, and He is the one who gives life and death, grants inheritance and wealth, humbles and exalts, strikes and heals. All these things the Name **Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*; representing the Sefirah of Malchut (Sovereignty), the interface between the divine and physical worlds</note> performs upon every single thing, according to the influence flowing to it from the higher levels—whether for good or for evil, for war or for peace, for famine or for plenty.

## Know
That when the Name (blessed be He) wishes to uproot or humble a nation below, He does not humble it until He humbles its **Prince** <term>Sar; a celestial guardian angel or spiritual archetype representing a specific nation</term> first in the heavens. For our Sages, of blessed memory, have said: "No nation falls unless its Prince falls first," as it is said: **"On that day the Lord will punish the host of heaven in heaven, and the kings of the earth on the earth"** <note>Isaiah 24:21</note>.

## And Know
And believe, that when Pharaoh pursued Israel, the scripture says of him: **"And he took six hundred chosen chariots and all the chariots of Egypt"** <note>Exodus 14:7</note>. For the war was both above and below, and the war above in the heavens was intense. 

The secret of **"six hundred chosen chariots"** is that the Prince of Egypt joined together with **Samael** <note>the primary figure of evil or the accuser in Kabbalistic thought</note>, and thus six hundred "chosen" chariots were added in addition to the chariots of Egypt. Otherwise, why would it be written: "He took six hundred chosen chariots **and** all the chariots of Egypt"? Were they not all chariots of Egypt? Rather, it says "all the chariots of Egypt" to show they all came to fight against the **Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*</note>. This is what Moses our Teacher <note>original: *Moshe Rabbenu*</note> told Israel: "Know that the war is not yours, but belongs to the Name (blessed be He)," 

---

as it is said: **"The Lord will fight for you, and you shall remain silent"** <note>Exodus 14:14</note>. And if the Name **Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*</note> were not present in the war, why would the Torah say: "The Lord <note>original: *YHVH*</note> will fight for you" and "The Lord is a man of war"? 

Rather, a war was taking place in the heavens. Because Israel was rebellious at the Sea, the war against the "External Princes" <note>the spiritual forces of the "Other Side" or impurity</note> was difficult. What did the attribute of **Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*</note> do? It clothed itself in three types of garments:
1. The **Garment of Royalty**, which is the **Beauty** <term>Tiferet; the central Sefirah</term> of the "Master of Jacob."
2. The **Garment of Kindness** <term>Chesed</term> from the side of Abraham, to save Israel.
3. The **Garment of War** from the side of **Might** <term>Gevurah</term>, which is Isaac, to fight against the upper chariots of Egypt and the wicked **Samael**.

He was clothed in the secret of three garments, corresponding to the secret of the verses **"And He moved..."** (*Vayisa*), **"And He came..."** (*Vayavo*), and **"And He stretched out..."** (*Vayet*) <note>Exodus 14:19-21. In Kabbalah, these three verses, each having 72 letters, form the 72-letter Name of God used for miracles</note>. These are the secret of the three garments we mentioned that waged the war of Egypt—against the Prince of Egypt—and defeated them. 

This is the secret of the verse: **"And He removed the wheels of their chariots and made them drive heavily; and Egypt said: 'Let us flee from Israel, for the Lord fights for them against Egypt'"** <note>Exodus 14:25</note>. The secret of **"He removed the wheels of their chariots"** refers to the **Upper Wheels** <term>Ofanim; a class of angels often associated with the movement of the Divine Chariot</term> of Egypt that were driving the Egypt below with haste. When the "Wagon" <note>the spiritual source</note> fled and those Wheels were broken, the powers of Egypt below were likewise broken. 

Consider the parable of a wagon: when its wheels are broken, how can it carry the load upon it? So too, when the **Upper Wheels** of Egypt were broken, all the multitudes of Egypt below—who are the "load" of those Wheels—remained driving with great heaviness. When the Egyptians found themselves in this heavy state, they knew that a "Wheel" had fallen—meaning the Prince of Egypt above. Thus it is written: **"Let us flee from Israel... for the Lord <note>original: *YHVH*</note> fights for them against Egypt"**—meaning the Egypt that is above.

And by whose power did the **Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*</note> win all these wars? By the power of **God of Hosts** <note>original: *Elohim Tzevaot*</note> in which He was clothed. This is the meaning of: **"The Lord of Hosts will punish the host of heaven in heaven and the kings of the earth on the earth,"** etc. And it says: **"For My sword has drunk its fill in heaven; behold, it shall come down upon Edom"** <note>Isaiah 34:5</note>. And it says: **"Curse Meroz, says the angel of the Lord of Hosts..."** <note>Judges 5:23</note>.

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic mechanics of divine warfare, specifically how the Sefirah of Malchut (Adonai) uses the power of Hod (Elohim Tzevaot) to defeat the spiritual "Princes" of enemy nations in the heavens before their earthly counterparts fall. It uses the Exodus and the breaking of the "celestial wheels" of Egypt as the primary example of this spiritual victory.</summary>
<keywords>Adonai, Elohim Tzevaot, Samael, Prince of Egypt, Merkavah, Ofanim, Three Garments, Gevurah, Chesed, Tiferet, 72-letter Name, Exodus</keywords>
