<meta>This page continues the exploration of divine Names and the Sefirot (divine attributes), specifically how the higher realms of Understanding (Binah) and the lower "pillars" of Victory and Splendor (Netzach and Hod) interact to govern the world and respond to human prayer and repentance.</meta>

Because the Prophets knew this great secret, they were able to nullify all the decrees issued in the **Heavenly Court** <note>original: *Beit Din shel Ma'alah*</note>. This is the secret of the verse: **"A righteous man rules through the fear of God"** <note>2 Samuel 23:3</note>. The Holy One, blessed be He, said: "Who rules over man? I do. And who rules over Me? The righteous person; for I issue a decree, and he nullifies it." 

And this is why it says, **"And Moses implored the face of the Lord his God"** <note>Exodus 32:11; the word "implored" (Vayechal) is interpreted here as reaching a state of "profaning" or "undoing" the decree</note>, for he raised his thought up to the place where a decree is nullified. This is also what our Sages of blessed memory meant when they asked: "From where do we know that even a signed decree can be torn up?" For it is said: **"Who is like the Lord our God in all we call out to Him?"** <note>Deuteronomy 4:7</note>. He is the one certainly called "**Who**" <note>original: *Mi*; in Kabbalah, "Who" is a technical name for the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding), the source of cosmic forgiveness</note>, as in the phrase "**Who** can annul it?"

This realm is called the "Place of Releasing Vows," for the release of all vows depends upon this level. This is the secret of the verse: **"With whom did He take counsel, that He might give Him understanding?"** <note>Isaiah 40:14</note>. Certainly, "with **Who**" (*Mi*) did the Holy One take counsel? He emanated **Understanding** <note>Binah</note> and created the world. This is also the "Place of Repentance." 

Therefore, even if a legal decree has been sealed on high, the People of Israel are able to nullify it through the power of **Repentance** <term>Teshuvah; literally "Return," referring here to the Sefirah of Binah</term>, which is the secret of **Understanding**, the secret of "**Who**" (*Mi*), and the secret of "Releasing Vows." Regarding the verse "His hand is stretched out," who can certainly turn His hand back? The reason is that His "hand" receives its flow and meaning from the attribute called "**Who**" (*Mi*). This is the secret of: **"Lift up your eyes on high and see: Who (*Mi*) created these (*eleh*)?"** <note>Isaiah 40:26; Kabbalistically, Mi (Who) + Eleh (These) = Elohim</note>. 

Behold the secret where it is said: **"Turn from Your fierce anger and repent"** <note>Exodus 32:12</note>; everything depends upon the "Place of Repentance." As it says: **"And his return was to Ramah, for there was his home"** <note>1 Samuel 7:17; a pun on "Ramah" meaning "the high place," i.e., Binah</note>. And: **"In the Jubilee year, each of you shall return to his ancestral possession"** <note>Leviticus 25:13; the Jubilee/50th year represents the 50 Gates of Understanding</note>.

**Know** and contemplate these awesome wonders: The **Book of Creation** <term>Sefer Yetzirah; one of the earliest and most influential mystical Jewish texts</term> says that the Name (blessed be He) placed **Understanding** in the brain, **Judgment** in the heart, and **Counsel** is completed in the kidneys. Each one stands over against the other. Therefore, contemplate this: every decree issued in the Heavenly Court begins by consulting **Understanding** (*Binah*) and the counsel is completed in **Victory** (*Netzach*) and **Splendor** (*Hod*).

**And now** we must inform you and enlighten your face regarding the verse: **"His legs are pillars of marble, set on bases of fine gold"** <note>Song of Songs 5:15</note>. You already know that the **Middle Column**—which is **Beauty** <term>Tiferet; the central Sefirah representing balance and the Written Torah</term>—is the secret of the letter *Vav* [ו] in the Divine Name. These two pillars, **Victory** (*Netzach*) and **Splendor** (*Hod*), are beneath the *Vav*, for the Divine Name [YHVH] is the secret of "six" <note>the numerical value of Vav is 6</note>. It sustains six attributes: **Greatness** (Chesed), **Might** (Gevurah), **Beauty** (Tiferet), **Victory** (Netzach), **Splendor** (Hod), and **Foundation** (Yesod). 

Understand that His "legs"—which are **Victory** and **Splendor**—are pillars. And upon whom are they pillars? Upon the **Foundation** (*Yesod*) of the Name **Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*</note>. **Know** that in the place of **Foundation**, **Splendor**, and **Victory**, the **Seraphim** <term>an order of angels associated with fire and purification</term> were created, those with "six wings." 

Why are they called *Seraphim*? Because they "burn" (*shorfim*) any person who does not treat the glory of their Creator with awe when gazing into the **Work of the Chariot** <term>Ma'aseh Merkavah; the highest form of Jewish mystical speculation</term> or engaging in the secrets of the **Glowing Amber** <note>original: *Chashmal*; the mystical light seen by Ezekiel</note>. They are the ones who "burn" all those who make improper use of the **Holy Names**. 

These are the places that extend from the **Six Orders of the Mishnah** <note>the fundamental code of Jewish Oral Law</note> until they reach the attribute of **Lord** (*Adonai*), which is called the **Oral Torah**. Because these pillars are "pillars of six," they are the secrets of the two **Tablets** upon which the **Written Torah** was inscribed. This Written Torah comes from **Beauty** (*Tiferet*) and is joined with the **Oral Torah** through the six orders of the Mishnah. Thus, you have the two Tablets and the six orders of the Mishnah coming as one, bound together by the secret of the "legs," which are the two pillars of six. Indeed, the existence of the Oral Torah is the unique expression of the Holy One, blessed be He.

**Know** and see that in these heavenly palaces, there is one angel appointed over the legal verdicts that go out from the Great Court to all the inhabitants of the world. Under his hand are two scribes. Before those verdicts go out into the world, the angel in charge has the power to tear them up or to amend them according to the power of **Repentance** shown by the one who was judged. There are many other great and awesome matters in the treasuries of **Victory** and **Splendor** that we shall yet awaken you to, with the help of God.

->The Lord of Hosts is with us;<-
->The God of Jacob is our stronghold.<-
->Selah.<-

<summary>This page explains how the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding) acts as a source of repentance and nullification for divine decrees, and describes the Sefirot of Netzach and Hod as the "pillars" that support the divine structure and the Oral Law.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the divine emanations (Sefirot), moving from the higher realms of Understanding and Repentance to the "pillars" of the divine structure: Victory (Netzach) and Splendor (Hod). It explores how human actions influence the flow of divine sustenance and introduces the concept of the indestructible "Luz" bone.</meta>

Regarding **Victory** and **Splendor** <note>original: *Netzach* and *Hod*; the seventh and eighth Sefirot, representing the legs or pillars of the divine chariot</note> and my guidance, the secret of the **Good Flow** <note>original: *Shefa*; the divine energy that sustains existence</note> pours out. It is fitting for you to give thanks, and if you are worthy, you will yet see great and wonderful things here.

### And Know
That **Victory** and **Splendor**, which are the divine names **Lord God of Hosts** <note>original: *YHVH Elohim Tzevaot*</note>, are both called throughout the Torah the **Skies** <note>original: *Shechakim*; traditionally the third of the seven heavens</note>. These are the two names called "Skies" in which they "grind manna for the righteous." Know this with certainty and clarity: through the secret of the Skies, the **Manna** and the **Dew** are drawn down. The sign for this is: **"And when the dew fell upon the camp in the night, the manna fell upon it"** <note>Numbers 11:9</note>. You already know that the **"Dew of Hermon"** <note>Psalm 133:3</note> descends upon the mountains of Zion—and for whom? For the "Righteous ones" within them, which are the qualities of **Righteous** <note>the Sefirah of *Yesod*</note> and **Righteousness** <note>the Sefirah of *Malkhut*</note>. Through these, the power of sustenance and life is received for all the righteous people named after them.

When the people of Israel are righteous, they support the **Upper Chariot** <note>the divine structure</note>. The Name "rides the heavens" with the help of Israel. Do not be surprised that I say "with the help of Israel," for our sages of blessed memory said that the upper realms require the assistance of the lower realms. Thus the verse says: **"There is none like God, O Jeshurun, who rides the heavens in your help"** <note>Deuteronomy 33:26</note>. It also says: **"Or let him take hold of My strength, that he may make peace with Me"** <note>Isaiah 27:5</note>, and: **"And now, I pray, let the power of my Lord be great"** <note>Numbers 14:17</note>. Conversely, if—God forbid—you turn away, the verse says: **"Of the Rock that begot you, you were unmindful"** <note>Deuteronomy 32:18</note>, and: **"You have wearied the Lord with your words"** <note>Malachi 2:17</note>, and: **"He has drawn back His right hand"** <note>Lamentations 2:3</note>. 

Returning to our subject: I say that the Holy One, blessed be He, "rides the heavens" with the help of Israel. What does He do? He prepares the **Skies** to pour out blessing and life upon Israel. This is the secret of the phrase **"And in His majesty, the skies."** After He "rides the heavens"—meaning He is exalted over the heavens—He prepares the Skies together, as it is said: **"Which the skies pour down and drop upon man abundantly"** <note>Job 36:28</note>. It also says: **"He commanded the skies from above, and the doors of heaven He opened"** <note>Psalm 78:23</note>. The great secret is: **"Drop down, O skies, from above, and let the skies pour down righteousness"** <note>Isaiah 45:8</note>. This refers to literal **Righteousness** <note>*Tzedek*</note>, for they grind manna for the righteous and for the one called **Righteous** <note>*Tzadik*</note>. What is written immediately after? **"Let the earth open, that they may bring forth salvation."** Behold, I am enlightening your eyes.

---

For the two Names beneath them—which are **Victory** and **Splendor**, the names **Lord of Hosts** and **God of Hosts**—are called **Skies**. The **Heavens** are above, and the **Skies** are beneath them.

### And Know
Regarding these two Names, it is said: **"O You who dwell in the heavens"** <note>Psalm 123:1</note> and **"May You hear in the heavens."** Following this path, contemplate the secret of **"Who rides the heavens in your help, and in His majesty, the skies."** The secret is that first the "heavens" literally drop down, and afterward **"the skies pour down righteousness."** If you understand the secret of what the sages said—"What is heaven (*Shamayim*)? It is Fire (*Esh*) and Water (*Mayim*)"—then the fire and its glow are the name **God** (*Elohim*) and **God** (*El*). 

Know and believe that from the place called **Heavens**, the flow comes to the place called **Skies**. This is the secret of **"Drop down, O heavens, from above"** and then **"and let the skies pour down righteousness."** This is also the meaning of **"Rider of the heavens in your help, and in His majesty, the skies,"** as well as the verse **"He commanded the skies."** The explanation of the verse is that He commanded the skies from the place that is above them, and from them **"the doors of heaven He opened."** 

Know that **Victory** and **Splendor** are called the **Essence of the Heavens** <note>original: *Etzem ha-Shamayim*</note> for their purity. Regarding them it is said: **"And like the essence of the heavens for purity"** <note>Exodus 24:10</note>, because their very essence and power come from the place called **Heavens**. They are contained within a known secret involving the explanation of the **Thirty-Six Knowings** <note>original: *Lamed-Vav De'im*; likely a reference to a specific mystical configuration of divine knowledge</note> which are the literal "Essence of the Heavens." They receive all "essences" of every kind. **"On that very day"** <note>original: *Be-etzem ha-yom ha-zeh*—a pun on "essence" (*etzem*) and "day"</note> it receives power. This is the secret of the verse: **"All my bones shall say: Lord, who is like You?"** <note>Psalm 35:10; again, "bones" and "essence" share the same Hebrew root, *Etzem*</note>.

### And Know
There is a certain bone formed in the human body called the **Luz** <note>a legendary, indestructible bone from which the body will be rebuilt at the time of the Resurrection</note>, which is the very essence of man. It is his core and his root; from it, man was built. It is like the "essence of the drop" <note>the primordial seed</note>. Even in the grinding down of a person, this bone does not diminish. It does not dissolve; even if they were to put it in fire, it would not burn. In a mill, it cannot be ground. With a hammer, it cannot be shattered. It is the bone that exists forever. It is this bone that receives delight and pleasure after the death of a righteous man, and concerning it, it is said: **"He will strengthen your bones"** <note>Isaiah 58:11</note>. It is also the bone that receives punishment in the wicked, of whom it is said: **"And their iniquities shall be upon their bones"** <note>Ezekiel 32:27</note>. And the bone...

It also introduces the mystical "Luz" bone as the indestructible essence of the human body that carries one's spiritual legacy into the afterlife.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the lower Sefirot, specifically Victory (Netzach) and Splendor (Hod), and their relationship to the physical body and the prophetic experience. It transitions from the "Manna" of the previous page to the "bone" that sustains the human form and the power of prayer for children, life, and sustenance.</meta>

And this bone’s essential root is derived from the **essence of the heavens** <note>original: *Etzem ha-Shamayim*; a reference to Exodus 24:10. In Kabbalah, this refers to a spiritual substance from which the physical "Luz" bone—the indestructible bone of resurrection—originates.</note>. From there, it receives the strength, power, permanence, and stability of the entire human body.

**And know** that the decisive place in man is found in the secret of the bones. Because jealousy <note>or "zeal"</note> stems from the **essence of the heavens**, wherever you find "jealousy" attributed to the One Above in the Torah—as it says, **"For the Lord your God is a jealous God"** <note>Deuteronomy 4:24</note>, and it says, **"A jealous and avenging God"** <note>Nahum 1:2</note>, and **"The Lord was jealous for His land"** <note>Joel 2:18</note>, and **"They have moved Me to jealousy with that which is not God"** <note>Deuteronomy 32:21</note>—this is why the verse states, **"Envy [jealousy] is the rottenness of the bones"** <note>Proverbs 14:30</note>. The secret of this is: **"The zeal [jealousy] of the Lord of Hosts will perform this"** <note>Isaiah 9:6</note>. Because jealousy is dependent upon this place <note>the Sefirot of Netzach and Hod</note>, it is said regarding the meal offering of a suspected adulteress, **"It is a meal offering of jealousy, a memorial offering bringing iniquity to remembrance"** <note>Numbers 5:15</note>. Those who know the root principle know that **Jealousy** and **Remembrance** are eternally linked.

**Victory** <note>Netzach</note> and **Splendor** <note>Hod</note> are the source from which the prophets draw their nourishment. From this place, the prophets emerged; therefore, you will find the prophets saying in their prophecies: **"Thus says the Lord of Hosts"** <note>YHVH Tzevaot</note>. You will find them mentioning three lower Sefirot in their prophecies, which are: **My Lord God of Hosts** <note>Adonai Elohim Tzevaot</note>, **The Lord of Hosts** <note>YHVH Tzevaot</note>, and **God of Hosts** <note>Elohim Tzevaot</note>. All of these are included in the secret of **"Thus says the Lord of Hosts,"** as I have already informed you.

By this path, you shall know that because they are the secret of the **Mirrors** <note>or "visions"</note> through which the prophets gaze, you will find that at the time of prophecy, the bodies of the prophets would recoil and be filled with fear; their entire bodies would shake, and all their bones would be terrified until they were overturned from their original state, until their souls were purified. Only then would they see what they saw. Concerning this, it is said: **"In a vision I will make Myself known to him"** <note>Numbers 12:6</note>. Sometimes it would be a dream; as the "partitions" <note>the veils between the human and divine</note> increase, the parables and riddles within them increase. However, the **Chariot** <note>the divine structure</note> functioned without the mediation of Victory and Splendor when [Moses] prophesied, for the "Moon" <note>the Sefirah of Malkhut</note> entered into the innermost chambers. This is what the Scripture means by: **"Mouth to mouth I speak with him"** <note>Numbers 12:8</note>. Therefore, the name **Lord of Hosts** <note>YHVH Tzevaot</note> is not mentioned in the Torah <note>the Five Books of Moses</note>, because his place was within the innermost realms, and all the hosts of above and below would go out and come in according to his word.

**And all** those who come to pray before the Lord, may He be blessed,

to request children, focus their intention toward **Victory** and **Splendor**, to draw the power down to the name **My Lord** <note>Adonai / Malkhut</note>, from which all the hosts of heaven and earth are drawn forth according to their kinds. From them, the flow <note>Shefa</note> comes to all species to make them grow and give birth, each according to its nature. The "Book of the Generations of Adam" <note>the lineage of humanity</note> is dependent on this place. All of this occurs when **Victory** and **Splendor** connect with the **Foundation** <note>Yesod</note>. Toward this quality, all those who came to ask children of God would focus their hearts.

When **Hannah** prayed, she did not pray merely to the "Palace" that she needed. What is written? **"And she vowed a vow and said: O Lord of Hosts, if You will indeed look on the affliction of Your maidservant"** <note>1 Samuel 1:11</note>. By saying **Lord of Hosts**, she mentioned the place of the generations of all who are born. To what height did Hannah intend her prayer to rise? Even to the place of the **Supreme Destiny** <note>original: *Mazal Elyon*; literally "Constellation" or "Flow," referring here to the highest source of influence</note>, known in the secret of the Thirteen Attributes of Mercy in the Palace of Mercy, which is fixed in the **Crown** <note>Keter</note>. From that "Destiny," all things depend—even a Torah Scroll in the Palace draws from that place and receives from it. This is what our Sages of blessed memory meant when they said: "Everything depends on **Destiny** <note>Mazal</note>, even a Torah Scroll in the Palace."

When Hannah focused her prayer, she ascended to the place of the Destiny, higher and higher. This is the meaning of: **"And she was in bitterness of soul and prayed *over* the Lord"** <note>1 Samuel 1:10; the Hebrew *Al* usually means "to" but can mean "over" or "above"</note>. It does not say "to the Lord" <note>El YHVH</note> but "over/above the Lord" <note>Al YHVH</note>. You already know that there are three Names: **My Lord** <note>Adonai</note> below, **The Lord** <note>YHVH</note> in the middle, and **I Will Be** <note>Ehyeh</note> above. Within the secret of **I Will Be** <note>Ehyeh/Keter</note>, the Destiny is suspended. This is the secret of **"And she prayed over the Lord"**—literally "above" the name YHVH. This is what our Sages of blessed memory said: **"Children, life, and sustenance do not depend on merit, but on Destiny"** <note>Talmud Moed Katan 28a</note>. The "place of merit" is found in the [Name] composed of the four letters [totaling] seventy, which is called **God** <note>Elohim</note>, the quality of Strength and Awe, as will be explained in its place with God's help. Rather, it depends on Destiny.

In the sphere of the **Crown** <note>Keter</note>, he who wishes to give birth to children by way of a miracle must ascend to this place. Similarly, one who wishes to attain life, or one who wishes to attain sustenance beyond what is strictly decreed for him. And from where do we know that these three things—life, children, and sustenance—are from above that Name? Regarding life, it is written: **"Grace me, add to your days fifteen years"** <note>referencing King Hezekiah in Isaiah 38:5</note>...

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Sefirot (Divine Attributes), moving from the concept of "Jealousy" and "Remembrance" associated with the lower Sefirot to a detailed examination of the fourth Divine Name, "The Lord of Hosts," and its connection to the Sefirah of Victory (Netzach).</meta>

# The Fourth Name of the Divine Names

The seventieth <note>original: "ע'" (70). In Kabbalah, seventy often refers to the seventy names of God or the seventy "nations" or degrees of the celestial hierarchy.</note> of the levels from bottom to top is the one called 
**The Lord of Hosts** <note>original: *Y-H-W-H Tzevaot*</note>. Although we have already explained much of the meaning 
of this Name in the previous gate <note>or chapter</note>, regarding the Name *Elohim*, 
**The Lord of Hosts** draws from the side of 
**Mercy** and **Kindness** <term>Chesed: the Sefirah of expansive love and benevolence</term> and brings down all the blessings through the Name 
**The Living God** <term>El Chai: the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation), the channel for divine flow</term> and through the Name unique to this attribute of Mercy 
called **The Lord of Hosts**. It is what is called **Victory** <term>Netzach: the seventh Sefirah, representing endurance, victory, and the right "leg" of the divine body</term>. And this is 
the **Victory** that maintains His kindness for Israel, as it says: 
**"Has His kindness ceased forever?"** <note>Psalm 77:9. In Hebrew, "forever" is *la-Netzach*, linking the verse to the Sefirah of Netzach.</note> And this is the source that decrees 
good decrees upon Israel by cleaving to the 
**Supreme Crown** <term>Keter: the highest Sefirah, the Divine Will</term>. And He never retracts them. And this is the secret of the verse: **"Moreover, the Glory [Victory] of Israel will not lie nor repent"** <note>1 Samuel 15:29</note>. And because 
Israel always needs to strengthen themselves and cleave 
to the attribute of **Victory**, which is the secret of the "Right Side" that stands 
to overcome the "Left Side" <note>the side of Judgment or Gevurah</note>. It is called **Victory** <note>*Netzach* also means "conquer" or "overcome"</note> because 
it wins the wars for Israel. Therefore, David, 
King of Israel, always had to say in the book of Psalms, 
**"To the Choirmaster [the Victor], a Psalm of David."** <note>original: *La-Menatzeach*. Kabbalistically, this addresses the Sefirah of Netzach.</note> And every place 
where he mentions "To the Choirmaster," which corresponds to the attribute of Victory, 
I must awaken you to a great and deep matter.

### And know
that three lights shine from the whiteness of the **Eye of 
Mercy**, and they are called **Kindness**, **The Lord** 
<note>representing *Tiferet* (Beauty/Glory)</note>, and **Victory**. While they are united, then 
the entire world is in joy, gladness, and perfection, 
and there is no adversary and no evil occurrence.

### And sometimes
this attribute is called 
**Victory** if it stands 
in its place below; then it is possible to decree a judgment for good, 
but that judgment might turn into a punishment, all according 
to one's deeds. But if the attribute of Victory has ascended through 
the channels upward and cleaves to the **Forehead of Will** 
in the **Head of the Crown**, and a good decree is issued for Israel—
even though they are not worthy of that goodness—
it does not retract. The reason is that it has already ascended 
to the place that is not called "Man," which is the Crown. And this is the secret of 
**"Moreover, the Glory [Victory] of Israel will not lie, for He is not a man to repent"** <note>1 Samuel 15:29</note>. But if the attribute of Victory stands below

<column-break/>

in **Beauty** <term>Tiferet</term>, it is possible "to repent" <note>meaning the decree can change</note>, for that is the 
known place upon which there is "the likeness of the appearance of a man upon it 
from above" <note>Ezekiel 1:26</note>. Wherever it stands, 
so it is established.

### And know
that there are established houses of judgment 
called **Eternities** <note>original: *Netzachim*</note>, as it is written: 
**"For ever and ever"** <note>Isaiah 34:10; original: *le-netzach netzachim*</note>. And they are the ones who triumph over all 
those who stand on the Left Side, and all the accusers. 
Therefore, the scripture says against the side of the accusers 
of every generation, **"It shall lie waste forever and ever; none shall pass through it"** <note>Isaiah 34:10</note>. That is to say, every city whose houses 
of judgment are of **Victory of Victories** <note>*Netzach Netzachim*</note> in their places and levels, 
the side of the accusers has no existence, only destruction 
and wandering.

### And know
that corresponding to the attribute of Victory, Solomon—peace be upon him—made 
the right-hand pillar called **Yachin** <note>1 Kings 7:21</note>. And he who knows 
the secret of **Yachin and Boaz** <note>the two pillars of Solomon's Temple</note> knows how the Soul and the Spirit 
soar by means of **The Living God** <note>*El Chai*</note> and **The Lord** <note>*Adonai*</note> through the influence 
of these two pillars, and in the secret of the Tabernacle 
and their forms. And by this pillar shall be built and established 
the City of Zion and Jerusalem. The sign for this is: **"You established [prepare] in Your goodness for the poor, O God"** <note>Psalm 68:11</note>. If you understand these three words, which are 
"You established in the goodness of the poor," you will know how **The Lord of Hosts** 
prepares and sends the goodness through the attribute of **The Living God** to pour 
the flow and the blessing into the attribute of **The Lord** <note>*Adonai* / *Malchut*</note> when she is 
empty, like the image of a poor person who has nothing. 
The sign for this is: **"And let the dry land appear"** <note>Genesis 1:9. In Kabbalah, the "dry land" represents the Sefirah of Malchut before it receives divine flow.</note>.

### Know
and contemplate that the structure of all 
the channels and all the forms <note>original: *golemim* (unformed substances or physical bodies)</note> 
received shapes and structure in this place 
through the attachment of the **Emanation** <term>Atzilut: the highest of the four worlds in Kabbalah</term> with the Name **The Lord**. And all 
the limbs within man received their forms 
and structures in this place. 
And the secret 
is: **"He made you 
and established you."** <note>Deuteronomy 32:6</note>

<summary>This page explains the Sefirah of Netzach (Victory/Eternity) and its associated Divine Name, the Lord of Hosts. It describes how divine decrees become irrevocable when elevated to the highest spiritual levels and connects these mystical concepts to the architecture of Solomon's Temple and the formation of the human body.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the analysis of the Divine Name "The Lord of Hosts" (Y-H-W-H Tzevaot), specifically exploring how different prophets receive their visions through varying balances of Mercy and Judgment, and how the Sefirot (Divine Attributes) are encoded within the letters of the Tetragrammaton.</meta>

For some prophets receive their visions through **Absolute Mercy**, while others through **Absolute Judgment**, and others through a combination of both **Judgment and Mercy**. It is written that **Wisdom**, **Kindness**, and **Mercy** <term>Chokhmah, Chesed, and Rachamim: the three primary expansive attributes</term> are present in the act of Creation. In the conduct of the Blessed Name with the world, He sometimes acts with absolute mercy, sometimes with absolute judgment, and sometimes with judgment and mercy combined. Sometimes you will find the Name of the Blessed Lord paired with the word **Hosts** <term>Tzevaot: literally "armies" or "hosts," signifying God's mastery over all celestial and earthly forces</term>. You will not find this pairing except in the books of the Prophets and the Writings. In the Prophets, it says: "Thus says the Lord of Hosts," and in the Writings, it appears in many places, such as: "The Lord of Hosts is with us, the God of Jacob is our fortress. Selah" <note>Psalm 46:7</note>.

Know that when this Name <note>the Tetragrammaton, Y-H-W-H</note> "clothes" itself within the Name called **Hosts**, He is performing justice and judgment, whether for good or for bad. Just as judgment proceeds through three expressions—**God, the Judge, the Lord** <note>original: "אל אלדי"ם יד' ו'" (El, Elohim, Y-H-W-H)</note>—the Lord completes the judgment by clothing Himself in the "Lord of Hosts." The secret of this is found in the verse: "But the Lord of Hosts is exalted in judgment" <note>Isaiah 5:16</note>. I will now hint at a great matter regarding this verse: when God performs judgment through the Prophets, He acts as the **Judge** <term>Elohim</term>, or He clothes Himself in **garments of jealousy** <note>referring to Divine zeal or righteous indignation</note> and clothes Himself in the Name **Lord of Hosts**, as it says: "The zeal of the Lord of Hosts will perform this" <note>Isaiah 9:6</note>. Because the prophets come to rebuke Israel and warn them, they were required to mention the Name **Lord of Hosts** in the context of testimony and warning, for both good and evil. 

When judgment was finalized against Israel because they had provoked Him so greatly, the merit of the Patriarchs <note>Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob, whose merits are said to shield their descendants</note> would have been exhausted, and had the Lord of Hosts remained only in the heights and acted with strict justice against Israel, Heaven forbid, no remnant or survivor would have remained. The secret of this is: "But the Lord of Hosts is exalted in judgment, and the Holy God is sanctified through righteousness" <note>Isaiah 5:16</note>. Consider how far this reaches: "unto the Holy God." This refers to the attribute of **Kindness** <term>Chesed</term>. What does the Holy One, Blessed be He, do with Israel? He performs **Righteousness** <term>Tzedakah: also meaning "charity" or "saving justice"</term> and does not bring them to total destruction. This is the meaning of: "To You, O Lord, belongs righteousness, but to us, shame of face" <note>Daniel 9:7</note>.

**And sometimes** you will find these three names in the verse "God of Hosts, hear my prayer" <note>Psalm 84:9</note>. The intention here is that the attribute of Mercy, the attribute of Judgment, and the attribute of Fear each clothe themselves in their proper place.

---

For **God** <term>El</term> corresponds to the attribute of **Victory** <term>Netzach: the seventh Sefirah, associated with the right leg and endurance</term>, and **the Judge** <term>Elohim</term> corresponds to the attribute of **Glory** <term>Hod: the eighth Sefirah, associated with the left leg and splendor</term>. These four attributes are clothed in four "garments" for the attribute of the Judge. The Name **Hosts** <term>Tzevaot</term> includes three names: **The Lord, Victory, Glory, and Foundation** <note>original: "יי' נצ"ח הו"ד יסו"ד"</note>. When the scripture says, "O Lord, God of Hosts," five Sefirot are implied, and each one takes hold of what is appropriate for it, and the four are united in **Foundation** <term>Yesod: the ninth Sefirah, the channel that gathers the energies of the upper Sefirot to pass them to the final Sefirah</term>. Therefore, it is hinted at in the verse: "O Lord God of Hosts, restore us; cause Your face to shine, and we shall be saved" <note>Psalm 80:20</note>. And therefore it is written: "The Lord is near to all who call upon Him... in truth" <note>Psalm 145:18</note>. The matter of "truth" in this context is that one must know how to pray, understanding which "place" <note>which Sefirah or level</note> they are standing at in that verse, or which Name they are mentioning. One must direct their intention <term>Kavvanah</term> to that specific Sefirah within the Blessed Name. Now, we must enter into the secret of these matters according to the intention of this book, with the help of the Supernal One who is always with us.

**The principle** and the root that you must know is that these four letters, which are **Y-H-W-H** <term>the Tetragrammaton</term>, include all ten Sefirot. Within them lies the perfect, true secret. When a person knows how to direct their intention to the ten Sefirot within the four letters—which is the [true] Unity according to the hint—the matter of uniting the ten Sefirot through two Names for the Blessed Lord works in this way:

**The final Heh** <note>ה: the last letter of Y-H-W-H</note> represents the names **Lord**, **Almighty**, and **God** <note>original: "אדני שרי אלדי"ם"</note>. These are the secret of the **Divine Presence** <term>Shekhinah</term> and the attribute of **Sovereignty** <term>Malchut: the tenth and final Sefirah</term> and the **Community of Israel**, along with all the other designations known for this attribute in the Torah.

**The Vav** <note>ו: the third letter of Y-H-W-H</note> of the Name is the secret of six names known for the **six Sefirot** <note>the "Z'er Anpin" or the six Sefirot from Chesed to Yesod</note> included in the letter *Vav* <note>the numerical value of Vav is 6</note>. These are: **Living God** <term>El Chai</term>, **God of Hosts**, **Lord of Hosts**, **[Name Permutation]**, and **the Judge** <term>Elohim</term>. The secret of these Sefirot is as follows: **Living God** is known in the attribute of **Foundation** <note>OCR says "Yod Yod," likely referring to the foundation of the 'Yod'</note> and is called the "Left Side" <note>this is an unusual association, as Yesod is usually central; the text may be following a specific kabbalistic school</note> along with other matters we have hinted at in their mention. **The Lord of Hosts** is known as the attribute of **Victory** <term>Netzach</term> and is called the "Right Side." **The Lord** <term>Y-H-W-H</term> is the secret of **Beauty** <term>Tiferet</term>, which is the secret of the "Middle Pillar" along with many other designations. **The Judge** <term>Elohim</term> is the attribute of **Might** <term>Gevurah</term> and **Fear** <term>Pachad</term> among the four [attributes] above, along with many other designations.

It also provides a structural mapping of the Sefirot onto the letters of the Tetragrammaton, particularly focusing on the final Heh (Malchut) and the Vav (the six intermediate attributes).</summary>

<meta>This page continues the discussion on the Names of God used in prophecy and prayer. The previous page established that the Name "Lord of Hosts" (Y-H-W-H Tzevaot) is used when God acts with a combination of Mercy and Judgment, specifically through the Prophets. This page transitions into how one should direct their heart in prayer to reach the divine source beyond the individual attributes.</meta>

### Concerning the Prayer of Hannah: "And Hannah prayed upon the Lord . . ."
<note>original: "ותתפלל חנה על יי'" (1 Samuel 1:10)</note>

She did not say "**to** the Lord" <note>original: "אל יי'" (el Adonai)</note> but rather "**upon** the Lord" <note>original: "על יי'" (al Adonai)</note>. From where do we learn that this refers to one’s sustenance? As it is said: "**Cast upon the Lord your burden, and He shall sustain you**" <note>Psalm 55:23</note>. It says "**upon** the Lord" and does not say "**to** the Lord." The reason is that anyone who wishes to achieve these things <note>referring to fundamental needs like sustenance or children</note> cannot achieve them in this world through the path of strict Law <term>Din: the attribute of rigorous justice</term> in the place called "Merit" <note>Zakhut</note>. Rather, it must come from the **Great Court** <term>Beit Din HaGadol: the supreme heavenly tribunal</term>, which is aligned against the **Middle Attribute**, which is the Tetragrammaton <note>original: "יהוה" (Y-H-W-H), the four-letter Name of God representing Mercy</term>.

## And according to
these paths, know and understand that even though we say that one who wishes to direct their desire should focus on one of the **Ten Names** known for the **Ten Sefirot** <term>Sefirot: the ten emanations or attributes through which God interacts with the universe</term>, our intention is not to say that their focus should reach that specific Name and stop there. Rather, one must extend their intention until the **Highest Source**, that place from which the desired thing was born, until the divine flow <term>Shefa: the spiritual energy or "bounty" flowing from the Divine</term> reaches that place where they are focusing. In doing so, the one who prays is found to be **Unifying the Sefirot** and bringing them closer to one another.

Afterward, contemplate what the Blessed Name said to Abraham: "**So shall your seed be**" <note>Genesis 15:5</note>. This means you must ascend from the attribute of **"So"** <note>original: "כה" (Koh), often associated with the lowest Sefirah, Malkhut</note>, which is the lowest, until the attribute of **Lord** <note>original: "אדני" (Adonai)</note>, which is higher, if you desire to obtain offspring. This is the secret behind what King David, peace be upon him, said: "**A Song of Ascents: Out of the depths I have called You, O Lord**" <note>Psalm 130:1</note>. He called from that place called "**Deep, Deep**," which is the "Upper Depth"—meaning the **Infinite** <term>Ein Sof: God as He is in Himself, without limit or end</term>.

Do not think that "depth" only refers to what is below. Anything that is very far from being grasped is called "depth." Likewise: "**That which was is far off, and deep, deep, who can find it?**" <note>Ecclesiastes 7:24</note>. The secret of **"A Song of Ascents: Out of the depths I have called You"** is this: how can one use "Ascents" (rising) together with "Depths" (falling)? This is what our colleagues, of blessed memory, said regarding the verse "**And Joshua lodged that night in the midst of the valley**" <note>Joshua 8:9</note>—this teaches that he lodged in the **depths of the Law** <note>Halakhah</note>. And in the *Book of Creation* <term>Sefer Yetzirah: an ancient foundational text of Jewish mysticism</term>, it mentions: **The depth of Beginning and the depth of End, the depth of Good and the depth of Evil, the depth of Above and the depth of Below, the depth of East and the depth of West, the depth of North and the depth of South** <note>The author is paraphrasing Sefer Yetzirah 1:5, which equates "depth" with the infinite dimensions of existence</note>. It equates "depth" to all sides. And behold—

---

—the Scripture says: "**The heavens for height, and the earth for depth**" <note>Proverbs 25:3</note>. The explanation of the verse is this: anything that is hidden from the hosts of the earth is called by the term "deep" because it is below in the depths. Do you not see what the Scripture says? "**In the appearance of the plague, it is deeper than the skin**" <note>Leviticus 13:3</note>. And our Rabbis, of blessed memory, said: "**Like the appearance of sunlight, which is deeper than the shadow.**"

It seems to me that what I have awakened in you regarding this matter is sufficient. I must now lead you into other matters appropriate to the intention of the Name.

## Know
that these two attributes <note>Netzach and Hod</note> are the place of **Counsel** from the "Upper Realm," according to the secret of: "**For the Lord of Hosts has counseled, and who shall annul it? And His hand is stretched out, and who shall turn it back?**" <note>Isaiah 14:27</note>. When the Blessed Name decrees from the "Upper Realm," which is called the **Great Sanhedrin** <term>Sanhedri Gedolah: the supreme council of seventy-one elders, here applied to the divine hierarchy</term>, He consults with these two attributes, which are **Victory** <term>Netzach</term> and **Splendor** <term>Hod</term>. This is the secret of what is written: "**This is the counsel that is counseled upon all the earth**" <note>Isaiah 14:26</note>. And even though the beginning of counsel is from the place of **Understanding** <term>Binah</term>, the end of counsel is in **Victory** and **Splendor**.

This is the great secret of: "**With whom did He take counsel, and who instructed Him, and taught Him in the path of judgment?**" <note>Isaiah 40:14</note>. And it says: "**Counsel is mine, and sound wisdom; I am understanding, I have might**" <note>Proverbs 8:14</note>. And the great secret regarding the child's birth: "**And his name shall be called Wonderful, Counselor, Mighty God, Father of Eternity, Prince of Peace**" <note>Isaiah 9:5</note>.
- **Wonderful** <note>Pele</note> refers to **Wisdom** <term>Chokhmah</term>.
- **Counselor** <note>Yoetz</note> refers to **Understanding** <term>Binah</term>.
- **God** <note>El</note> refers to **Greatness** <term>Gedulah/Chesed</term>.
- **Mighty** <note>Gibbor</note> refers to **Might** <term>Gevurah</term>.
- **Father of Eternity** <note>Avi-ad</note> refers to **Beauty** <term>Tiferet</term>.
- **Prince of Peace** <note>Sar-Shalom</note> refers to **Foundation** <term>Yesod</term>, which includes within it **Victory** and **Splendor**.

Regarding the verse "**Counsel is mine, and sound wisdom; I am understanding, I have might**," I shall explain it to you, God willing.

## Know
that the "Great Sanhedrin" of the Upper Realm is called the **Great House of Judgment** <note>original: "בד הגדול" (Beit Din HaGadol)</note>. It is a "House" of **Might** <note>Gevurah</note>. When they come to decree judgment upon any creature, they deliberate and take counsel with **Understanding** <note>Binah</note>, which is above them, and with **Victory** and **Splendor**, which are below them. The secret of "**Counsel is mine**" refers to **Victory** and **Splendor**. "**I am Understanding**" refers to **Understanding**. "**I have might**" refers to the Great House of Judgment on the side of **Might**. This is the great secret of: "**For the Lord of Hosts has counseled, and who shall annul it?**" The word "**Who**" <note>original: "מי" (Mi)</note> refers to **Foundation** <note>Yesod</note>, according to the secret of "**With whom** <note>Mi</note> **did He take counsel, and who instructed Him?**"

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Divine Names. While the previous page discussed the Name "Lord of Hosts" and how the Patriarchs and Prophets accessed Divine Mercy through prayer, this page focuses on the primary four-letter Name (the Tetragrammaton) and its relationship with the Name "Elohim" during the creation and completion of the world.</meta>

# The Name
->The fifth of His names, <gloss>the Holy</gloss><-

**Know** and believe that the Name **Y-H-W-H** <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter ineffable Name of God, often translated as "The LORD."</note> (may He be blessed), following the path of the divine degrees, is what is called "The Lord." It is that which stands over all the Sefirot <term>Sefirot: the ten emanations or attributes through which the Infinite interacts with the universe</term>, both the upper and the lower, and they are all held within it. Through it, everything is grasped from below to above and flows from above to below. This is the Name toward which all eyes look; all His other Holy Names are drawn from it and return to it. It is what unifies all the Sefirot through its letters. Through it, all created beings exist, and all the "Chariots" <term>Merkavot: the celestial structures or "vehicles" of the divine realm</term> in the world, both upper and lower, are drawn forth by it. All the spheres of the world, its foundations, and its structures depend upon it. There is nothing in all the worlds that is perfected except through the Name of the Lord (may He be blessed). Before we proceed, we must inform you of a core principle in which you will find rest regarding many matters concerning the Name.

**Know** that you will find this honored and awesome Name, Y-H-W-H, used in several ways and contexts in the Torah. Sometimes you will find it standing alone in most of the Torah, such as when it says: "And the Lord spoke," "And the Lord said," or "And the Lord did." Yet sometimes you find another of His Holy Names joined to it, as it says after the Work of Creation: "On the day the Lord God <note>original: "יי׳ אלהים" (Adonai Elohim)</note> made earth and heaven" <note>Genesis 2:4</note>. You will not find the Name "God" <note>original: "אלהים" (Elohim)</note> alone in the entire [account of the] Work of Creation <note>This refers to the transition from the first chapter of Genesis to the second</note>. When the Torah begins to speak of the perfection of creation in the section "These are the generations of the heavens and the earth," it begins to mention the Name "Lord God." We must inform you of the reason for this.

**Know** that since the Name is perfected and all things are held within it through the Sefirot—as I have already informed you—it was not necessary to mention it during the Work of Creation. This is because the works were not yet finished in their completion, and the world was not yet at its fullness; rather, all things were still being made and coming into being. But once all things and all creatures were in their completion and perfect repair, then the Name "Lord God" was mentioned. These are two names that unify: "God" <note>Elohim</note> is the secret of "My Lord" <note>original: "אדני" (Adonai)</note>. Even though "God" is the secret of Might and Judgment <term>Gevurah and Din: the divine attributes of restriction, power, and strict justice</term>, and even though [the Sages] said He began to create the world with the Attribute of Judgment—"In the beginning God [Elohim] created"—He saw that the world could not endure, so He arose and associated the Attribute of Mercy <term>Midat HaRachamim: the quality of compassion associated with the name Y-H-W-H</term> with it. This is what is written: "On the day the [Lord] God made earth and heaven." This matter is true, correct, and established. Nevertheless, the Attribute of Might and the "leaven of the powers" flowed into the Name "Lord" <note>Adonai</note> and was clothed in the Name called "God" <note>Elohim</note> to create the world. This is why the Name "God" <note>Elohim</note> is sometimes deficient and sometimes full. It was necessary to mention it during the creation of the world while the world was not yet "full" in its fullness.

The secret is: "Who makes one mute <note>original: "אלם" (ilem)</note>, or deaf, or seeing, or blind? Is it not I, the Lord?" <note>Exodus 4:11</note>. The secret of "Who makes one mute, is it not I, the Lord" is the secret of the Name "God" <note>Elohim</note>. For if you remove from the Name **God** [Elohim] the two letters of the Name **Y-H** <note>The first two letters of the Tetragrammaton, Y-H-W-H</note>, you are left with "Mute" [Elem] <note>The name Elohim (א-ל-ה-י-ם) minus the letters Yud (י) and Heh (ה) leaves Aleph, Lamed, Mem (א-ל-ם), which spells "mute."</note> in the secret of "I was bound in silence" <note>Psalm 39:3</note>. Therefore, the world was created with the Name "God" [Elohim]. But when all things and all creations were perfected and the world was in its fullness, it was necessary to mention the Name "Lord God." This is what our Sages of blessed memory meant by saying: "He mentioned a full Name over a full world." Therefore, after the Work of Creation, He began to specify "Lord God" regarding the fullness of the world. Therefore, you should understand that wherever you find "Lord God" in the Torah, it is a "Full Name." All the matters found in a passage that mentions the Full Name operate under all the conditions of the Attribute of Judgment and the Attribute of Mercy combined. You find this in the creation of the First Man, in his judgment, and in his expulsion, and in those matters. Indeed, in every place in the Torah where you find "Lord God," you must contemplate that the Attribute of Mercy is clothed with the Attribute of Judgment. Each of the attributes then acts in perfection, whether for judgment or for mercy. Contemplate this principle deeply and guard it always.

**The secret** of the sounding of the Shofar <note>The ram's horn blown on the New Year (Rosh Hashanah)</note> is the "plain blast" <note>Tekiah</note> and the "broken blast" <note>Teruah</note>, and the sign is: "God [Elohim] has ascended with a shout [Teruah], the Lord [Y-H-W-H] with the sound of a horn [Shofar]" <note>Psalm 47:6</note>. Sometimes you will find the Name Y-H-W-H unified with the Name Adonai...

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic significance of the names Y-H-W-H and Elohim, arguing that the world was created with the name Elohim (representing Judgment) but only reached completion when joined with Y-H-W-H (representing Mercy). <meta>This page continues the technical breakdown of the Divine Name Y-H-W-H (the Tetragrammaton). While the previous page established that all existence depends on this Name, this page delves into the specific mystical correspondences of each letter and explains how the Name provides the mathematical and spiritual structure for the entire universe, specifically through the ten Sefirot.</meta>

Appellations • **El** <note>Hebrew: *El*, a name of God often associated with power or the attribute of Mercy</note> is the secret of the attribute of Loving-kindness (*Chesed*) and Greatness (*Gedulah*), the "Good Well" stemming from the Supreme Source. Along with several other appellations, the first **H** <note>The second letter of the four-letter Name Y-H-W-H</note> of the Name is the secret of the Name written with the letters **Y-W-D** but pronounced **Adonai** <note>The Hebrew word for "Lord," used as a substitute for the ineffable Name</note>; it is the secret of Understanding (*Binah*), the secret of the Jubilee (*Yovel*), the secret of Supreme Justice, and various
-> other appellations. <-

### Yud
The letter **Yud** <note>The first letter of the Name Y-H-W-H</note> of the Name is the secret of the Divine Name called **Yah**; it is the secret of Wisdom (*Chokhmah*), which is called the "Beginning of Desire." It is also the secret of Fear and Will, along with various other appellations.

### And the Secret
The tip of the upper **Yud** <note>The tiny decorative stroke at the top of the Hebrew letter Yud</note> is the secret of the **Crown** (*Keter*) within the Name called **Ehyeh** <note>Meaning "I Will Be," the name revealed to Moses at the burning bush</note>. This is the secret of that which is called the **Infinite** (*Ein Sof*) and is also called **Nothingness** (*Ayin*), as in the verse: "From where [**Ayin**] shall my help come?" <note>Psalm 121:1. Kabbalists pun on the word *Ayin*, which means both "from where" and "Nothingness," to suggest help comes from the highest, most unknowable level of God</note>. Through that tip, the Crown is unified with the rest of the Divine Emanations (*Sefirot*). Thus, we find that this Name—the **Yud**—includes all ten *Sefirot* within its four letters and within all the Holy Names known in the Torah. Everything depends on this Name; everything is unified in it, and there is nothing in the world that does not emerge from it. When a person recites the *Shema* <note>The central Jewish prayer: "Hear O Israel, the Lord our God, the Lord is One"</note> and reaches the mention of "The Lord is One," it is proper to focus on His four letters and unify the ten Names within them with great intention (*Kavanah*). Through this unification of His Will, the person will be beloved and desired before the Lord.

### And We
Must inform you that an inner tradition has already been received regarding the matter of the *Shema*. When a person recites the *Shema*, they should focus on the four letters

---

of the Name in this manner: In the phrase "Hear O Israel," when one reaches the mention of **The Lord** (*Y-H-W-H*), one should intend the first letter of this Name, which is the letter **Yud**. At the word "**Our God**" (*Eloheinu*), one should intend the second letter, which is the letter **He**. At the next mention of "**The Lord**" (*Y-H-W-H*), one should intend the third letter, which is the letter **Vav**. At the word "**One**" (*Echad*), one should intend the fourth letter, which is the final **He** of the Name. Thus you have learned that a person can have two different focuses while reciting the *Shema* regarding the Unity of the Blessed Name. The first method is to include all ten *Sefirot* and the ten Holy Names within the four letters of the Name as I previously informed you. The second is to intend each of the four letters of the Name specifically as one mentions "The Lord, Our God, The Lord is One," assigning each letter to a specific word.

### And After
we have informed you of this, we must show you how this Name is the foundation and principle for all calculations in the world. You already know that according to the scholars of research <note>A reference to medieval philosophers/mathematicians</note>, there are only nine digits. Some of them believe that "One" is not a number <note>In ancient Greek and medieval philosophy, "1" was often considered the source of numbers rather than a number itself</note>. We need not speak of this now except in a general way. You already know that the system of the Name **Y-H** is included from above and below in three Names: **Ehyeh**, **Y-H-W-H**, and **Adonai**. The letters of the Great Name are only three <note>Y, H, and W (as the H is repeated)</note>, and the arrangements of the Divine Chariot follow three paths. You also know that the first three *Sefirot* are unified together: **Crown** (*Keter*), **Wisdom** (*Chokhmah*), and **Understanding** (*Binah*). The three middle *Sefirot* are unified together: **Greatness** (*Gedulah/Chesed*), **Strength** (*Gevurah*), and **Beauty** (*Tiferet*). The three final *Sefirot* are unified as one: **Victory** (*Netzach*), **Splendor** (*Hod*), and **Foundation** (*Yesod*). Thus there are nine *Sefirot* in nine systems. All of these nine *Sefirot* channel their blessings upon the **Foundation**, which is the ninth *Sefirah*, and onto the tenth and final *Sefirah*,
-> which is **Adonai** <note>Corresponding to the Sefirah of *Malchut* (Kingship)</note> <-

### And Now
open your eyes and see that just as the system of nine *Sefirot* revolves around the Unique Name, so too are all the calculations in the world. There are only nine digits, for when you begin to count from one to ten, you find the nine digits are the specific units of ten. But when the "one" reaches **Yud** (10), the **Yud** acts in the likeness of a new "One." When you count 1, 2, 3 (*Aleph, Bet, Gimel*), you then count 10, 20, 30 (*Yud, Kaf, Lamed*). Thus the **Yud** (10) is like the **Aleph** (1), the **Kaf** (20) is like the **Bet** (2), and the **Lamed** (30) is like the **Gimel** (3). This rule holds until you reach **Tzadi** (90), which you will find is the ninth of the tens. But when you count...

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Divine Names and the mathematical structure of the universe. The previous page concluded by identifying the letter Yud with Wisdom and the "Crown" with the Infinite (Ein Sof). This page expands on the numerical systems (like the Nine Chambers) used to understand the structure of the Sefirot and the transformative power of letter permutations.</meta>

It is found that they proceed by tenths, and that which remains is only **nine**. How so? A tithe of one hundred is ten; a tithe of ten is one. Thus, the entirety of the calculation is but nine. Consequently, the Holy Names are specifically designated within **nine Sefirot** <note>The ten divine emanations; here, the author focuses on the nine that emanate from the first</note>, and these nine Sefirot are included within the Name **Y-H-W-D A-D-N-Y** <note>A combination of the Tetragrammaton and the name Adonai</note>. Regarding this, you will find that the letters of the Alphabet consist of twenty-seven letters <note>The 22 standard Hebrew letters plus the 5 "final" forms used at the end of words</note>, which are three sets of nine, following the method we call **AIQ BeKeR** <note>A mystical system of "Nine Chambers" where letters are grouped by their numerical values: 1-10-100 (Aleph-Yud-Kuf), 2-20-200 (Beit-Khaf-Reish), etc.</note>. Do you not see that there are only nine triple-levels? The **Aleph** [1] corresponds to the **Yud** [10], and the **Yud** corresponds to the **Kuf** [100], in the secret of **AIQ**.

## And Now
We must inform you that the calculations in the world—which are nine—and the levels—which are ten—all hang upon the **Seal of the Great Name**, may He be blessed, through the permutation of letters. This follows the secret of the ten letters: **A-B G-D H-W Z-H T-Y** <note>The first ten letters of the alphabet paired together</note>, and the permutation of **A-T B-CH G-W D-H** <note>A specific cipher system called At-Bach</note>. Do you not see that **Y-H** [the first two letters of God's name] fills the world and its outcomes? According to the second way of permutation, regarding the guidance of the levels: **A-Y B-T G-CH D-Z** and **H-W**. Do you not see that **H-Y** is the seal of the entire world, the levels, and the forms, according to the order of created beings? Thus, it is the seal of all levels and divine governance.

## And Know
That the **Four-Letter Name** <note>The Tetragrammaton: Y-H-W-H</note> is the secret of the Name of the Blessed One. It testifies and indicates that in the essence of the truth of the Blessed Name, there is no change, no reduction, and no deficiency. Rather, His truth and His greatness remain equal both before He created the world and after He created the world. Even if He wished to destroy the world, He would remain in His existence—equal, established, and eternal—without any change in the world.

## And I Must
Set before you a great matter. It is said in the **Book of Formation** <note>Sefer Yetzirah, one of the oldest Jewish mystical texts</note> that the Blessed Name created all creatures according to the nature of the twenty-two letters, and He permuted the letters, and from them, He created all the various types of created beings. You must understand what the term "permutation" <note>Hebrew: *Tziruf*, the practice of rearranging letters to reveal hidden meanings or create new realities</note> means.

## Know
That if you see pieces of

---

white silver, even though it is white, you do not know with absolute certainty if there is an admixture of something else. When will the matter be known to you with certainty? When you place it inside a furnace upon the fire of the **refiner** <note>Hebrew: *Metzaref*, a word sharing the same root as *Tziruf* (permutation)</note>. Then you will know for certain if there is any admixture in it. As it says: "And I will refine them as silver is refined, and test them as gold is tested." This is called a "refiner"—anything that tests the truth of the silver to see if it contains an admixture. Just so, in this way, when the Blessed Name joins these letters to one another and creates words—as you might say: **K-T-R** [Kaf-Tav-Reish] and you make from them **Keter** <note>Crown</note>—you must permute these letters and test if there is an admixture in them that does not belong in the word *Keter*. You will find through the permutation that it contains **Karet** <note>Excision or spiritual cutting off</note>, which is the opposite of *Keter*. And who revealed this permutation? Just as the refiner from the outside tests the silver, so too the permutation from the outside tests the words. This is the secret of: "The word of the Lord is refined" <note>Psalm 18:30</note>; the word of the Lord, the word of pure things, "silver refined in a crucible on the ground"; truly, "the word of the Lord is refined."

## And After
They have informed you of this so that you might know that one of the Names indicating Greatness and Loving-kindness and Mercy is the secret of **El** <note>God</note>, as we say: "God [**El**], King, sitting upon a throne of mercy," and it says: "Who is a God [**El**] like You, who pardons iniquity and passes over transgression?" Even though you see the word **El** in this great level, if you put it into the "refiner" <note>permute it</note>, you will find it becomes **Lo** <note>Hebrew: L-A, meaning "No" or "Not"</note>. Now, see the difference between the word **El** [God/Power] and the word **Lo** [Not/Nothing]. This is the secret mentioned by the author of the *Book of Formation*: three letters **A-M-Sh** <note>Aleph, Mem, Shin—representing Air, Water, and Fire</note>; the male is in **A-M-Sh** and the female is in **A-Sh-M**. Do you not see that in **A-M-Sh** and **A-Sh-M**, the letters are the same? And what caused one to beget male and the other female? One must say: the reversal of the letters. Even though the letters are equal, as it is said: for the Good is **Oneg** <note>Pleasure/Delight</note>, but reverse and read it and you find **Nega** <note>Plague/Affliction</note>. The *Book of Formation* says that the secret of all opposites was created in one utterance: "There is no good higher than **Oneg** [Delight] and no evil lower than **Nega** [Plague]."

<meta>This page continues the discussion of divine structures and letter permutations, specifically transitioning from the numerical systems (AIQ BeKeR) to the mystical combinations of the Divine Names. The Name **Adonai** <note>Hebrew: *Adonai*, meaning "My Lord"</note> appears in two aspects: where the Name **Y-H-W-H** <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter Name of God; original: "יי"</note> precedes it, and where **Adonai** precedes **Y-H-W-H**. In the verse, *"The Lord [Y-H-W-H] God [Adonai] is my strength"* <note>Habakkuk 3:19</note>, and likewise, *"God, to the Lord, the issues..."* <note>Psalm 68:21</note>, or *"For to You, O Lord [Y-H-W-H] my Lord [Adonai], are my eyes..."* <note>Psalm 141:8</note>, you should know that when these two Names are mentioned together, the vocalization of the Name is changed. We pronounce it as **Elohim** <note>The Name associated with justice and divine power</note>. Even though it is written as the four-letter Name [Y-H-W-H], it is read with the vowel points of **Elohim**. This is a mystical hint to the attribute of **Binah** <note>The Sefirah of "Understanding," often seen as the divine source of structure</note>, which unites with the attribute of **Malchut** <note>The Sefirah of "Kingship" or sovereignty, associated with the physical world</note>, which is called 

-> **Adonai, and by the mouth of the Lord, may He be blessed** <-

# And Know
And believe that when mentioning **Adonai** in this order, the divine abundance <note>Hebrew: *Shefa*; the flow of spiritual energy</note> descends from the upper **Sefirot** <note>Divine emanations</note> until it reaches the Name **Adonai**, and then the entire world is blessed. However, when one mentions **Adonai** first followed by **Y-H-W-H**, as in the verses: *"My Lord [Adonai] God [Y-H-W-H], what will You give me?"* <note>Genesis 15:2</note>, *"My Lord [Adonai] God [Y-H-W-H], You have begun..."* <note>Deuteronomy 3:24</note>, or *"My Lord [Adonai] God [Y-H-W-H], do not destroy Your people and Your inheritance..."* <note>Deuteronomy 9:26</note>, and similar verses—know and believe that when the Name is mentioned in this order, the intention is to elevate the Sefirot and to unify them from below to above. This represents the desire to be unified and to cling to the Higher Light.

Sometimes you will find the Name **Y-H-W-H** unified with **El** <note>A name of God associated with the attribute of Kindness/Chesed</note>, as in the verse: *"God [El] is the Lord [Y-H-W-H], and He has given us light"* <note>Psalm 118:27</note>. This intention means that when **El** is mentioned with **Y-H-W-H**, the entire world is under the influence of mercy and kindness <note>Hebrew: *Chesed*</note>, and all the lights are in their perfection and fullness. This is because He is the "High God of Kindness," and the abundance of "Kindness to Abraham" is found in the secret of the Name *"The Lord [Y-H-W-H], the Everlasting God [El Olam]"* <note>Genesis 21:33</note>. Furthermore, the Name **Y-H-W-H** itself is the Name of Mercy. When the two Names **Y-H-W-H Y-H-W-H** are unified, all the Sefirot are in joy and perfection, and all created beings receive kindness and mercy from above to below, as it says: *"God [El] is the Lord [Y-H-W-H], and He has given us light."* You will also find that among the Thirteen Attributes of Mercy, these two Names are mentioned alone: *"The Lord [Y-H-W-H], the Lord [Y-H-W-H], God [El], merciful and gracious"* <note>Exodus 34:6</note>, because within them all kindness and mercy are contained.

Sometimes you find the Name **Y-H-W-H** unifying with **El** and with **Elohim**, as in: *"The God [El] of gods [Elohim], the Lord [Y-H-W-H]"* or *"God [Elohim] the Lord [Y-H-W-H]."* It is known from the verse: *"A Psalm of Asaph: The Mighty One, God, the Lord [El Elohim Y-H-W-H], has spoken and called the <unclear>earth</unclear> from the rising of the sun to its setting"* <note>Psalm 50:1</note>. Know that the intention here is for the Name to clothe itself in all its garments,

---

whether for judgment or for mercy. How so? Consider when the children of Reuben, the children of Gad, and the half-tribe of Manasseh built the altar across the Jordan. All of Israel prepared to go up against them in war, but before they fought, they sent Phinehas and ten princes of Israel to speak with them regarding the matter of the altar. What was the response of the children of Reuben, Gad, and the half-tribe of Manasseh? *"The Mighty One, God, the Lord! The Mighty One, God, the Lord! [El Elohim Y-H-W-H, El Elohim Y-H-W-H]* He knows, and let Israel know; if it was in rebellion or in trespass against the Lord, do not save us this day!—in building us an altar to turn away from following the Lord, or if to offer on it burnt offerings or grain offerings... but out of anxiety... [lest] our children say to your children tomorrow... 'See the replica of the altar of the Lord which our fathers made, not for burnt offering or sacrifice, but as a witness between us and you.' Far be it from us to rebel against the Lord!" <note>Joshua 22:22-29</note>. 

Now, when the Reubenites, Gadites, and the half-tribe of Manasseh needed to mention these three attributes together, they mentioned the Name of Kindness—which is **El**—the Name of Judgment, Strength, and Punishment—which is **Elohim**—and the Name that renews through both of them—which is **Y-H-W-H**. They were saying: "If we made the altar for the Lord as a memorial and not for sacrifice, may the Name, blessed be He, pour His blessings, goodness, and kindness upon us through the attribute of **El**, who is the Watcher who knows the intention for which we did this <unclear>thing</unclear>. But if we made this altar to rebel against the Tabernacle of the Lord, for this altar to be for burnt offerings and sacrifices, may **Elohim**—who is the Witness, the Judge, and the Prosecutor—exact punishment from us and punish us for this great transgression." 

Therefore, they were required to mention **El Elohim Y-H-W-H**, which are the secret of the "God of Abraham, God of Isaac, and God of Jacob": Greatness [Chesed], Strength [Gevurah], and Beauty [Tiferet] <note>These correspond to the three patriarchs in Kabbalistic thought</note>. Similarly, regarding the creation of the world, it was necessary to mention in this Psalm: *"The Mighty One, God, the Lord, has spoken and called the earth,"* as the Name clothes itself in the creation of the world in the garment of great Mercies, and from there clothes itself in the garment of absolute Judgment, and from there in the "Middle Path" of Mercy and Judgment combined. All of this follows the secret of the creation of all living beings.

And this is the meaning of the verse: *"I will provoke them with a non-people"* <note>Deuteronomy 32:21</note>. # And After
Having informed you of all these principles, we must return and inform you how the Great Name **Y-H-W-H**, may He be blessed, is the leader of the entire world. Everything is within His power. [We will explain] how all the Holy Names are unified in Him, and how the other titles in the Torah are like "garments" <note>Hebrew: *Malbushim*; a common Kabbalistic metaphor for how the Infinite God expresses Himself through different attributes</note> that the King wears. These garments are not the actual essence of the King, but they are vessels that the King puts on. 

Sometimes He wears **Royal Garments** when the King is at ease, in quiet, and in security throughout all His land, and His servants are all at peace. 
<margin>(Reference) "He made a feast for all his princes and servants" <note>Esther 1:3</note></margin>
This occurs when He shows the "wealth of His glorious kingdom." 

At other times, the King is in a great war. Many enemies plunder and despoil His land, coming to take spoils and destroy His kingdom. Then the King wears different clothes: armor, helmets, and other types of weapons—His sword, spears, shields, and bows. Then the King and His servants are in haste and agitation, and His entire land is in turmoil until the wrath passes. 

And sometimes the King sits in His house, and none of His army or horsemen are there with him, except for the people of His household: His wife, His sons, and those close to Him. The King removes most of His garments and stays with them in quiet and joy. They do many things together that he cannot do in front of the multitude, because the members of His household are as precious to Him as one of His own limbs. He is not concerned about removing some of His garments and gear in their presence. The secret of this is: *"Your Teacher shall not hide Himself anymore"* <note>Isaiah 30:20</note>. 

And sometimes the King secludes Himself from all the members of His household, and no one from the household remains with him except the **Queen** alone. Then the King strips off all His gear entirely, and then he cleaves to the Queen and they become one essence.
<margin>(Reference) "I have taken off my robe; how can I put it on again? etc." <note>Song of Songs 5:3</note></margin>
And after this introduction:

# Know
That the Name, may He be blessed, **Y-H-W-H**, is glorified and crowned with two Holy Names, and all are unified in the truth of this Name, may He be blessed. Each one indicates a specific matter in the way we have described. For instance, when the Name, may He be blessed, wishes to have mercy on His world and to be gracious to them, He wears the garment of Kingship, of Judgment, and of Mercy. The banner that the Name **Y-H-W-H** carries is the banner upon which the form of **El** <note>The Name associated with pure Lovingkindness (*Chesed*)</note> is engraved. This is hinted at in: *"Every man by his own banner, with the ensigns..."* <note>Numbers 2:2</note>. 

When the Name, may He be blessed, rejoices with His creatures, He has mercy on His world and rejoices with them. This is the secret of: *"May the glory of the Lord endure forever; let the Lord rejoice in His works"* <note>Psalm 104:31</note>. Then He wears and glories in his hair <note>A mystical reference to the "hairs" or emanations of the Divine Countenance</note> justice <unclear>m b 8 II</unclear> like His garments.

<meta>This page continues the discussion of divine names and their mystical properties, specifically focusing on how the structure of the Hebrew language reflects the unchanging nature of the Creator. ### Page 28

And there is no "distress" <note>Hebrew: *Tzarah*; also implies a narrowing or constraint</note> below that cannot be prevented. Behold, we have said that there are twenty-two letters, and in them, you find that the word **God** <note>Hebrew: *El* (Aleph-Lamed)</note> has its opposite, **No** <note>Hebrew: *Lo* (Lamed-Aleph)</note>. Now see how much distance there is between *El* (God) and *Lo* (No). And if with only two letters there is such an opposite, how much more so with three letters? For as the number of letters increases, the number of opposites increases. As we have said: two letters constitute two "houses" <note>A term from the *Sefer Yetzirah* (Book of Creation) referring to the possible permutations of a set of letters</note>—such as A-B and B-A. Three letters constitute six "houses"—such as A-B-G, B-N-A, A-G-B, B-A-G, G-B-A, and G-A-B. And you see that for all the words in the world, when you permute them <term>Tziruf|The mystical practice of rearranging Hebrew letters to reveal hidden meanings</term>, you find many opposites within them.

But the letters of **Y-H-W-H** <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter Name of God</note>, may He be blessed—you will find twelve permutations within them, and you will not find a single opposite among them. Rather, all permutations of the Name, may He be blessed, all twelve of them, point to a single matter: and that is the secret of **Existence** <note>Hebrew: *Hovayah*, which shares the same letters as the Name Y-H-W-H</note> regarding the Name, may He be blessed, and His reality. This is the secret of the verse: *"I the Lord do not change"* <note>Malachi 3:6</note>. This is to say: in all the opposites in the world, you find change, but there is no change in His Existence in the world. How so? The permutations of the Name, may He be blessed, follow twelve paths:

->**Y-H-W-H / Y-H-W-W / Y-W-H-H / H-W-H-Y / H-W-Y-H / H-H-Y-W / W-H-Y-H**<-
->**W-H-H-Y / W-Y-H-H / H-Y-H-W / H-Y-W-H / H-Y-H-W / H-H-W-Y**<-

Contemplate these twelve permutations, and you will find no "opposite" <note>In Hebrew grammar and Kabbalah, reversing letters often creates a word with an opposite meaning, but here, the author argues all arrangements of the Tetragrammaton only affirm "Being"</note> in them, nor will you find them testifying to anything other than **Existence** and **Reality**. And behold, when Israel desires to cleave to the Name, may He be blessed—which is **Y-H-W-H** and does not change—so too Israel does not change and is never destroyed, in accordance with the secret of: *"I the Lord do not change; and you, O sons of Jacob, are not consumed"* <note>Malachi 3:6</note>. This means: just as there is no change in Me, so the children of Jacob face no annihilation. And this is what the verse says: *"But you who held fast to the Lord..."* <note>Deuteronomy 4:4</note> and so on. Following this path, understand that just as the Name, may He be blessed, has twelve permutations, so the tribes of the children of Jacob came to the number of twelve. Each tribe has a seal in His great Name, may He be blessed. This is the secret of the twelve stones of the **Ephod** <note>The breastplate worn by the High Priest, containing twelve stones representing the tribes</note>, corresponding to the twelve permutations. Thus, the tribes of the children of Jacob are twelve in number. And the Scripture says: *"For Your Name is called upon Your city and upon Your people"* <note>Daniel 9:19</note>. His Name is certainly called upon His people. How? Twelve permutations corresponding to the twelve tribes; each and every tribe has a known Name out of the twelve.

<margin>Jacob:</margin>
*"Each according to his blessing, he blessed them"* <note>Genesis 49:28</note>. Each and every tribe was blessed according to the Name that fell to its portion from the twelve permutations of the Name; with that specific Name, he blessed them.

**Know** and understand that with each Name from the twelve permutations—according to the Name that rose as the portion of each and every tribe—Jacob blessed them. As it is said: *"All these are the twelve tribes of Israel, and this is what their father spoke to them and blessed them; each according to his blessing, he blessed them"* <note>Genesis 49:28</note>. Why?

**Know** that the Name **Adonai** <note>Lord; the Name associated with divine sovereignty and the physical world</note> is the secret of the **Blessing** <note>Hebrew: *Berakhah*</note> and the **Pool** <note>Hebrew: *Bereikhah*; a wordplay suggesting the Name is a reservoir of divine energy</note>, from which twelve springs flow to the twelve tribes. This is the great secret of the verse: *"And they came to Elim, where there were twelve springs of water and seventy palm trees; and they camped there by the waters"* <note>Exodus 15:27</note>.

**And after** you have known this:

**Know** that the Holy One, blessed be He, has no change whatsoever. This is not the case with all other names and titles in the world. For behold, the primary name among the names is **El** <note>God</note>. And you find its opposite is **Lo** <note>No/Not</note>. When Israel sought to provoke the Unique Name **Y-H-W-H**, they were unable to do so [through its letters]. But with the word *El*, as the prophet Isaiah said when they denied the Lord and said: *"It is not He"* <note>Hebrew: *Lo Hu*; Jeremiah 5:12, though the text attributes the concept to the prophetic era generally</note>. When they denied Y-H-W-H, they could not provoke the Name with the letters of *Y-H-W-H*, but only with the letters of *El*, which they reversed [to *Lo*]. And it is said they intended to deny the name *El*. Behold, the Torah revealed this matter in the Song of **Haazinu**: *"They have provoked Me with a non-god"* <note>Hebrew: *Be-Lo El*; Deuteronomy 32:21</note>. That is, they took *El*, reversed it, and said *Lo* (No). And with that very attribute of "No" <note>The "non-god"</note> with which they provoked Him, I will provoke them. And this...

These 12 permutations correspond to the 12 Tribes of Israel, the 12 stones of the High Priest's breastplate, and the 12 springs at Elim.</summary>
